We propose EffRank/$n$ (effective rank normalized by agent count) and $D_\text{act}$ (mean pairwise KL divergence between agents' action distributions) as low-overhead diagnostics for reward-attribution effects, then test them on competent MAPPO agents in SMACv2 \texttt{protoss\_5\_vs\_5}, where unit type is encoded in the observation. In an observation $\times$ reward-attribution comparison (unit type observed vs.\ masked; individual damage-contribution reward vs.\ shared team reward), geometry follows observation rather than reward. With unit type observed, shared and individual rewards have similar EffRank/$n$ ($0.31{\pm}0.03$ vs.\ $0.29{\pm}0.02$) and probe accuracy ($0.75{\pm}0.05$ vs.\ $0.73{\pm}0.05$, both $\gg 1/3$ chance), while $D_\text{act}$ leans higher under individual rewards ($1.23{\pm}0.06$ vs.\ $1.07{\pm}0.20$). Masking unit type cuts the above-chance probe signal by more than half, to $0.49$ in both reward arms. In short: individually rewarded agents are competent and separable by role, but on SMACv2 the observation explains the geometry and reward attribution shows up mainly in behavior. Thus geometric diagnostics must control for observed role information and test persistent roles that are not directly observed. EffRank/$n$ and $D_\text{act}$ add $<$5\% overhead.

</details>


### [73] [Hierarchical Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16528)
*Chenxiao Yang,Zhiyuan Li,Shai Ben-David,Nathan Srebro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了层次域泛化问题，将其视为从有限观测区域到整个实例空间的外推问题，并用任意域层次结构替代i.i.d.采样。研究表明，主要障碍不仅在于假设类的复杂度，还在于训练/测试域的划分方式。无论假设类多小或训练规模多大，某些划分方式都会导致泛化失败。这表明现代泛化理论必须将域结构作为首要研究对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的i.i.d.假设在现实世界中往往不成立，数据通常具有层次化结构（如不同医院、不同地区的数据分布差异）。作者希望理解在这种层次化域结构下，机器学习模型能否以及如何实现泛化，并找出阻碍泛化的根本原因。

Method: 通过理论分析，将层次域泛化建模为从有限观测区域到整个实例空间的外推问题。作者考察了假设类的复杂度以及训练/测试域的划分方式对泛化性能的影响，并构造反例证明在某些划分下泛化必然失败。

Result: 发现了两个关键结果：1) 泛化失败不仅取决于假设类的复杂度，还取决于训练/测试域的划分方式；2) 无论假设类多么简单、训练样本多么充足，总存在某些域划分方式使得某些目标域的泛化失败。

Conclusion: 泛化理论必须超越传统的i.i.d.假设，将域结构作为第一类对象来研究。层次化域结构下的泛化问题需要新的理论框架。

Abstract: We study hierarchical domain generalization as a problem of extrapolation from finite observed regions to an entire instance space, replacing i.i.d. sampling with arbitrary domain hierarchies. We show that the central obstruction is not only the complexity of the hypothesis class, but the train/test domain partition through which evidence is revealed. In particular, no matter how small the class or how large the training size, some partition makes generalization fail for some target. These results suggest that modern generalization theory must treat domain structure as a first-class object.

</details>


### [74] [Building2Building: A Large Scale Benchmark for Generalizable Real-World Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16534)
*Vincent Taboga,Justin Veilleux,Doseok Jang,Anushree Rankawat,Pierre-Luc Bacon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出B2B，一个基于EnergyPlus的大规模HVAC控制环境套件，用于系统研究强化学习中的泛化、迁移、多任务和元学习问题，具有参数化建筑生成器和标准化评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL策略对动力学、动作空间、观测空间或目标的变化非常脆弱，而现有基准测试缺乏足够的多样性和复杂性，难以严格研究RL中的迁移、多任务学习和元学习。

Method: 基于EnergyPlus构建B2B套件，完全兼容Gymnasium接口；设计参数化建筑生成器以系统性生成多样化建筑配置（异构观测/动作空间）；定义涵盖目标适应、动力学适应、动作空间偏移和跨域迁移的基准任务。

Result: 提供了大规模、多样化、物理驱动的测试平台及标准化评估协议，能够系统研究连续控制中的泛化和迁移问题。

Conclusion: B2B不仅推动RL泛化研究，还通过改进大规模HVAC控制（建筑中最耗能的系统之一）产生重要社会影响。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved strong results in control, yet learned policies remain brittle to changes in dynamics, action spaces, observation spaces, or goals, a critical limitation for real-world deployment. Existing benchmarks offer limited diversity and complexity, making it difficult to rigorously study transfer, multi-task learning, and meta-learning in RL. We introduce Building2Building (B2B), a large-scale suite of realistic Heating, Ventilation, and Air Conditioning (HVAC) control environments built on EnergyPlus, a state-of-the-art building simulator. B2B is fully compatible with the Gymnasium interface and features a parametric building generator, enabling the systematic generation of diverse building configurations with heterogeneous observation and action spaces. Based on this suite, we define benchmark tasks targeting key open challenges in RL, including goal adaptation, dynamics adaptation, action-space shifts, and cross-domain transfer. By providing a large-scale, diverse, and physically grounded testbed with standardized evaluation protocols, B2B enables systematic investigation of generalization and transfer in continuous control. Beyond advancing research on generalization in RL, this new benchmark also carries significant societal implications by enabling improved HVAC control at scale, one of the most energy-intensive systems in buildings.

</details>


### [75] [Discrete Ricci Curvature on Protein Contact Graphs for Lightweight Fold Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16553)
*Jianru Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究离散Ricci曲率作为轻量级结构描述符用于蛋白质折叠分类，发现仅22维的Ricci特征即可超越均值池化的ESM-2（150M）蛋白质语言模型嵌入，与持续同调结合后达到最佳性能（CATH上macro-F1 0.71，SCOPe上0.68）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质折叠分类通常使用序列表示或结构描述符，但轻量级手工设计描述符与预训练蛋白质语言模型嵌入之间的直接比较仍较为有限，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在Cα接触图上计算Ollivier-Ricci和Forman-Ricci边曲率，提取其汇总统计量和分位数构成22维固定长度特征。在CATH top-10拓扑分类和ASTRAL 40%同源性SCOPe top-10折叠基准上，与几何特征、接触图统计、持续同调及均值池化ESM-2（150M）基线进行比较。

Result: 轻量级结构描述符在两个数据集上均显著优于均值池化的ESM-2嵌入，在ASTRAL 40% SCOPe基准上差距更大。仅22维的Ricci特征（占ESM-2维度3.4%）已超越ESM-2基线。Ricci结合持续同调（112维）达到最优：CATH上macro-F1 0.71，SCOPe上0.68。

Conclusion: 轻量级可解释的图描述符在蛋白质折叠分类中可作为预训练蛋白质语言模型嵌入的实用替代方案，存在一个轻量级方法优于大规模预训练模型的性能区间。

Abstract: Protein fold classification can be approached via sequence-based representations or structural descriptors, but direct comparisons between lightweight handcrafted descriptors and pretrained protein language model embeddings remain limited. We investigate discrete Ricci curvature on Calpha contact graphs as a lightweight structural descriptor for fold classification. Each protein domain is represented by a 22-dimensional fixed-length feature derived from summary statistics and quantiles of Ollivier-Ricci and Forman-Ricci edge curvature distributions. We evaluate on CATH top-10 Topology classification and on the ASTRAL 40%-identity SCOPe top-10 Fold benchmark, comparing against geometry, contact-graph statistics, persistent homology, and mean-pooled ESM-2 (150M) baselines. On both datasets, lightweight structural descriptors substantially outperform mean-pooled ESM-2 embeddings, with a larger performance gap on the ASTRAL 40% SCOPe benchmark. Ricci alone uses 22 dimensions, or 3.4% of the ESM-2 baseline dimensionality, and already outperforms mean-pooled ESM-2 on both datasets. Combining Ricci with persistent homology yields the strongest performance, achieving macro-F1 of 0.71 on CATH and 0.68 on SCOPe with a 112-dimensional feature vector. These results identify a regime where lightweight interpretable graph descriptors offer a practical alternative to pretrained protein language model embeddings.

</details>


### [76] [Capacity and Redundancy Trade-offs in Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16554)
*Asif Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出容量-冗余（CR）恒等式分解多任务学习中的预测信息，并引入聚类间隙分解和梯度-TC桥接，理论证明聚类共享优于全局共享的条件，实验表明聚类LoRA显著降低残差耦合项并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习中负迁移常被视为优化伪影，但也可归因于共享容量有限和任务冗余不足。本文旨在从信息论角度量化该效应，并探索聚类共享相比全局共享的优势条件。

Method: 提出容量-冗余（CR）恒等式，将各任务预测信息之和分解为联合预测信息（含通过总相关TC定义的标签冗余）和残差耦合项；证明聚类间隙分解给出聚类共享优于全局共享的充要条件；在高斯多任务模型中建立梯度-TC桥接，形式化梯度余弦相似度作为冗余排序的代理；通过验证集残差相关性估计残差耦合项Δ。

Result: 聚类LoRA显著降低估计的残差耦合项$\widehatΔ$，优于大小匹配的随机分区，并在多种子置信区间下取得统计显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 从信息论角度揭示了多任务学习中负迁移的机制，提出了聚类共享优于全局共享的理论条件，并实证验证了聚类LoRA的有效性。

Abstract: In multi-task learning (MTL) negative transfer is often considered as an optimization artifact, but it can also be viewed as a consequence of limited shared capacity and weak task redundancy. We investigate this effect through a Capacity--Redundancy (CR) identity that decomposes the sum of per-task predictive informations into joint predictive information that includes label redundancy defined via total correlation (TC), and a residual coupling term that quantifies interference left unresolved by the shared representation. Additionally, we show two key results: (i) a clustering-gap decomposition that gives a necessary and sufficient condition for clustered sharing to outperform global sharing, and (ii) a gradient--TC bridge in a Gaussian multi-task model that formally justifies gradient cosine similarity as a proxy for redundancy ordering. Empirically, we estimate the residual coupling $Δ$ from validation residual correlations, showing that clustered LoRA substantially reduces $\widehatΔ$, outperforms size-matched random partitions, and results in statistically significant gains with multi-seed confidence intervals.

</details>


### [77] [Learning from World Feedback: Why Model Uncertainty Fails as a Risk Signal in Model-Based RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16591)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RLxF框架，主张学习信号应来自世界反馈而非内部模型代理。在安全模型预测控制中，标准动力学不确定性代理与安全性反相关（碰撞率从26%增至34%），而使用三种世界反馈信号（传感器裕度、碰撞时间、结果监督反馈模型）可将碰撞率降至1-14%。从中提炼出三条RLxF原则，适用于模型控制和大模型对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MBRL安全方法依赖模型内部不确定性作为安全代理，但这种代理与实际安全性之间存在结构性失配——模型不确定性支持状态预测空间，而任务风险支持约束边界，两者相关性极低（r<0.15）。需要提出替代方案，使学习信号直接来自世界反馈。

Method: 1) 提出RLxF框架的三个设计原则；2) 在四种世界模型架构上（覆盖2x MSE范围）进行MPC规划对比；3) 用TOST统计等价性检验评估MPC性能等价性；4) 设计三种世界反馈信号：最小激光雷达距离（传感器裕度）、碰撞时间（时序信号）、结果监督反馈模型g_psi（类似RLHF中的结果训练奖励模型）；5) 分析模型不确定性代理与安全性之间的结构性失配机制。

Result: 1) 四种世界模型架构下MPC规划统计等价（TOST, n=200）；2) 动力学不确定性惩罚使碰撞率从26%升至34%（代理反相关于安全性）；3) 世界反馈信号将碰撞率降至1-14%；4) 模型不确定性与任务风险在支持空间上正交（r<0.15）。

Conclusion: 提出RLxF三条原则：基于世界结果衡量风险、部署前验证代理有效性、当直接世界信号不可用时用结果训练反馈模型替代。这些原则同时适用于模型预测控制和大语言模型对齐中的验证器或RLHF方法。

Abstract: The RLxF programme argues that learning signals should come from world feedback rather than from internal model proxies. We instantiate this position in safe model-based control and distil it into three concrete design principles. Empirically, across four world-model architectures spanning a 2x MSE range, MPC planning is statistically equivalent (TOST, n=200), and dynamics-based uncertainty penalties increase collision rates from 26% to 34%: the standard MBRL safety proxy is anti-correlated with safety in this regime. Replacing the model-internal proxy with three world-feedback signals (a sensor-derived margin via minimum lidar, a temporal signal via time-to-collision, and an outcome-supervised feedback model g_psi trained on prior collision labels, structurally analogous to outcome-trained reward models in RLHF) reduces collisions to 1-14% without retraining the world model or the planner. The mechanism is structural: model uncertainty has support over state-prediction space, whereas task risk has support over constraint boundaries, with empirical correlation r < 0.15. From this we extract three RLxF principles (ground risk in world outcomes, validate proxies before deployment, and substitute outcome-trained feedback models when direct world signals are unavailable) and argue they apply equally to model-based control and to verifier-based or RLHF approaches in LLM alignment.

</details>


### [78] [Privacy Cost as Equity Input: A Group Fairness Criterion for Differentially Private Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16620)
*Rakshit Naidu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出隐私成本公平性指标PCER（隐私-成本公平比率），通过将群体预测阳性率除以其过拟合差距，衡量不同人口群体在差分隐私系统中的隐私暴露与收益是否对等，发现仅关注结果公平会忽略保护群体在隐私成本上承受的双重劣势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私公平性研究仅关注结果公平（如准确率差异），忽视了隐私成本本身（即各群体因训练数据泄露风险承担的不同信息暴露）也是一种伤害。作者基于补偿性公平框架，主张承受更大隐私暴露的群体应获得比例上更大的系统收益。

Method: 定义了PCER指标：群体阳性预测率 / 群体过拟合差距（训练与测试准确率之差），后者通过标准成员推断攻击界约束了群体对推断攻击的脆弱性。仅需各群体的训练和测试准确率，无需影子模型。在包含表格和NLP领域的6个基准数据集上，在多种隐私预算下的DP-SGD训练后进行评测，并与标准公平指标对比。

Result: PCER揭示了结果导向指标无法发现的模式：在COMPAS数据集上，保护群体同时承受更大的隐私暴露和更差的预测结果（双重劣势），而人口统计平价差距完全掩盖了这一点。敏感性分析显示，极强隐私保证下两群体的过拟合均趋于数值下限，导致暴露审计在该情景下无效。

Conclusion: 隐私保护系统的公平性审计必须同时考虑谁承担保护成本，而不仅是谁受益于结果。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) is increasingly deployed to limit membership inference risk in machine-learning systems. Prior work has shown that DP-SGD can widen accuracy disparities across demographic groups, but this framing treats fairness as a purely outcome-side concern. We argue that privacy cost, the information leakage borne by each group, is itself a form of harm, and adopt a compensatory-fairness framework in which a group that involuntarily bears greater privacy exposure is owed proportionally greater benefit from the system. From this principle we derive the \emph{Privacy-Cost Equity Ratio} (PCER), a group fairness metric defined as a group's positive prediction rate normalized by its per-group overfitting gap. By a standard membership inference bound, this overfitting gap upper-bounds each group's vulnerability to inference attacks, making PCER a conservative measure of benefit relative to exposure. PCER needs only per-group train and test accuracy (no shadow models), making it a practical post-hoc audit tool. We evaluate PCER alongside standard fairness metrics across six benchmark--attribute combinations spanning tabular and NLP domains, under DP-SGD at a range of privacy budgets, and validate the overfitting-gap proxy against a direct threshold membership-inference attack. The results reveal patterns that outcome-based metrics miss. On COMPAS, PCER uncovers a persistent double disadvantage: the protected group bears both greater privacy exposure and worse predictive outcomes, something demographic parity gap masks entirely. Sensitivity analysis shows very strong privacy guarantees collapse both groups' overfitting to a numerical floor, rendering exposure-based audits uninformative in that regime. Together, these findings show that fairness audits of privacy-preserving systems must account for who bears the cost of protection, not only who benefits from its outcomes.

</details>


### [79] [Multimodal Attention-based Deep Learning for Emergency Triage with Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16662)
*Hazqeel Afyq Athaillah Kamarul Aryffin,Kamarul Aryffin Baharuddin,Mohd Halim Mohd Noor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多模态深度学习架构，结合表格数据（生命体征）和文本数据（主诉），利用自注意力机制捕捉局部和全局关系，在马来西亚某医院急诊科11,102条分诊数据上验证，相比基线模型准确率提升1.95%，F1-score提升2.49%，ROC AUC提升1.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的分诊决策对避免临床恶化、发病率和死亡率至关重要。现有方法难以有效建模表格数据（生命体征）和文本数据（主诉）两种数据类型的内在时间结构和依赖关系。

Method: 提出一种多模态深度学习架构，同时处理表格数据和文本数据；利用自注意力机制捕获特征之间的局部和全局关系。

Result: 在11,102条急诊分诊数据上，相比基线模型准确率提升1.95%，F1-score提升2.49%，ROC AUC提升1.41%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明该模型在预测分诊决策方面具有潜力，多模态融合与自注意力机制有效提升了分诊准确性。

Abstract: Accurate emergency triage decision is critical to avoid clinical deterioration, morbidity, and mortality. Machine learning-based triage system involves acquiring the main presenting complaint in text form and assessing vital signs in numerical data, enabling an automated and efficient analysis of patient information for timely and accurate prioritization of medical attention. However, modelling the intricacies of both data types requires a comprehensive understanding of the temporal structure and dependencies within the data. Thus, the aim of this study is to propose a multimodal deep learning architecture that can effectively handle both tabular and textual data. Furthermore, the proposed model exploits self-attention to to capture both local and global relationships between the features. A dataset consisting of 11,102 triage data collected from emergency department of Hospital Universiti Sains Malaysia is used for model development and validation. The proposed model demonstrated an increase of 1.95% in accuracy, 2.49% in F1-score, and 1.41% in ROC AUC compared to the baseline model. The experimental results demonstrated the potential of the proposed model in predicting triage decisions.

</details>


### [80] [CLDRoute: Conditional Latent Diffusion for Routability Map Generation in Physical Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16674)
*Kiran Thorat,Nicole Meng,Caiwen Ding,Yingjie Lao,Zhijie Jerry Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CLDRoute框架，将可布线性估计从确定性预测转化为条件生成问题，利用条件潜扩散模型同时生成拥塞和DRC违规的空间分布及其不确定性估计，在CircuitNet 2.0数据集上取得了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于学习的方法将可布线性估计视为确定性预测任务，只能输出单一拥塞或DRC结果，无法提供预测的不确定性信息，导致后路由迭代成本高昂。

Method: 将可布线性估计建模为条件生成问题，提出CLDRoute框架，采用物理感知条件建模和任务特定潜建模，利用条件潜扩散模型同时生成布线路由拥塞和DRC违规的空间分布场，支持基于采样的推理以同时输出均值预测和空间不确定性估计。

Result: 在CircuitNet 2.0 (N28)上，DRC违规生成SSIM 0.9678、MAE 0.0028、TopK@1% 0.3494；拥塞生成SSIM 0.9031、MAE 0.0286、NZ-Pearson 0.3692。

Conclusion: CLDRoute框架通过生成预期结果及其不确定性，为布局阶段的可布线性估计提供了更实用的视角。

Abstract: Accurate routability estimation during physical design is important for reducing costly post-routing iterations. Prior learning-based methods treat this task as deterministic prediction, mapping placement-stage features to a single congestion or DRC outcome. We instead formulate routability estimation as a conditional generation problem, where both routing congestion and DRC violations are modeled as spatially structured routability fields. Our framework, Conditional Latent Diffusion for Routeability estimation (CLDRoute), uses physics-aware conditioning and task-specific latent modeling to handle the different characteristics of congestion and DRC maps. This allows our method to supports sample-based inference, producing both a mean prediction and a spatial uncertainty estimate for the same input design. On CircuitNet 2.0 (N28), our method achieves, for DRC violation generation, an SSIM of 0.9678, an MAE of 0.0028, and a TopK@1% of 0.3494; for congestion generation, it achieves an SSIM of 0.9031, an MAE of 0.0286, and an NZ-Pearson of 0.3692. Overall, our framework provides a more practical view of routability at placement by generating both the expected outcome and its uncertainty.

</details>


### [81] [A Framework for Early Sepsis Prediction via Self-Supervised (JEPA) and Federated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16681)
*Umair bin Mansoor,Munaf Rashid,Roomi Naqvi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种建模范式（自监督JEPA、自监督VICReg、半监督VICReg微调、监督TCN）用于早期脓毒症预测，提出JEPA+XGBoost+平均池化模型在AUPRC上接近SOTA基准，同时减少83%生物标志物使用；半监督VICReg微调模型在时间鲁棒性上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录中的早期脓毒症预测面临不规则采样、高缺失率和类别不平衡的挑战，现有方法未能充分比较不同学习范式在面对这些困难时的表现差异。

Method: 采用统一的预处理流程（小时级分箱+前向填充），对MIMIC-III数据集中7种生物标志物进行建模；对比四种范式：JEPA（掩码潜变量预测）、VICReg（方差-不变性-协方差正则化+双视图增强）、VICReg预训练后半监督微调、监督TCN，并结合XGBoost分类器与不同池化策略。

Result: JEPA+XGBoost+平均池化在发病时刻AUPRC达0.636（接近SupMix基准0.667，生物标志物减少83%）。Tier 1管线（VICReg预训练+半监督微调+XGBoost）AUPRC为0.510，较原始基线提升3.1倍，较TCN提升7.6%。微调VICReg编码器时间退化率仅16.8%（H0→H10），远优于TCN（47.5%）和JEPA（65.3%）。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练结合任务感知微调能在发病时刻保持高判别力，同时在长时间预测范围内保持特征稳定性，为资源受限场景下的临床预测提供有效方案。

Abstract: Early sepsis prediction from electronic health records is challenged by irregular sampling, high missingness, and class imbalance. We systematically compare four modeling paradigms -- self-supervised Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) via masked latent prediction, self-supervised VICReg (variance-invariance-covariance regularization) with two-view augmentation, semi-supervised fine-tuning of a VICReg-pretrained encoder, and supervised Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) -- alongside raw-feature baselines. All models share a common preprocessing pipeline of hourly binning with forward-fill imputation applied to 7 biomarkers selected via sparsity analysis from the MIMIC-III dataset. Our best model (JEPA + XGBoost + mean pooling) achieves AUPRC 0.636 at the time of onset (H0), approaching the SupMix benchmark (0.667) while using 83\% fewer biomarkers. The Tier 1 pipeline -- VICReg pretraining followed by semi-supervised fine-tuning and XGBoost -- achieves AUPRC 0.510 at H0, a 3.1$\times$ improvement over the raw-feature baseline (0.165) and a 7.6\% improvement over the end-to-end supervised TCN (0.474). Crucially, the fine-tuned VICReg encoder exhibits the most temporally persistent representations, degrading only 16.8\% from H0 to H10 compared to 47.5\% for supervised TCN and 65.3\% for JEPA, demonstrating that self-supervised pretraining with task-aware fine-tuning yields features that are both sharp near onset and robust across prediction horizons.

</details>


### [82] [Building a Neural Network from Scratch: Implementation, Evaluation, and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16682)
*Yuanzhe Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完全从零实现（不依赖自动微分或预构建深度学习模块）的自包含神经网络框架，包含多层架构、多种激活函数、正则化技术和先进优化器。该框架在教育上可用于理解前向/反向传播、梯度动力学和优化景观，并在多类分类任务上验证了其正确性、数值稳定性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级深度学习库的广泛使用虽然加速了模型开发，但使得神经网络内部机制越来越抽象，造成了实际使用与基本理解之间的鸿沟。本文旨在通过一个完全从零实现的框架来弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建了一个完全自包含的神经网络框架，不依赖自动微分或预构建的深度学习模块，实现了多层架构、多种激活函数、正则化技术和先进优化器等所有核心组件。

Result: 该框架在多类分类任务上表现出色，成功验证了其正确性、数值稳定性和跨配置的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅可作为教学工具来揭示前向/反向传播、梯度动力学和优化景观，其可扩展设计和清晰的模块化结构也使其成为教育用途和未来研究探索的可靠基线。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of high-level deep learning libraries, while accelerating model development, has increasingly abstracted away the internal mechanics of neural networks, creating a gap between practical usage and fundamental understanding. To address this, the paper presents a self-contained neural network framework implemented entirely from scratch -- without relying on automatic differentiation or pre-built deep learning modules. The implementation encompasses all essential components, including multi-layer architectures, diverse activation functions, regularization techniques, and state-of-the-art optimizers. Beyond serving as a pedagogical instrument that demystifies forward/backward propagation, gradient dynamics, and optimization landscapes, the framework demonstrates robust performance when applied to a multi-class classification task, successfully validating its correctness, numerical stability, and generalization across varied configurations. The extensible design and clean modularity further position it as a reliable baseline for educational purposes and future research exploration.

</details>


### [83] [Effects of width-dependent model hyperparameters and $\ell_2$-regularization on the loss landscape of two-layer ReLU networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16720)
*Haruka Eshima,Makoto Yamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带权重衰减的两层ReLU网络的全局最优解性质，推导了超参数导致全局最小值坍缩到零解的条件，发现AdamW优化器能防止参数坍缩而SGD不能，并在单输入维度下解析推导了全局最优参数集，揭示了ℓ2正则化的宽度不变连接效应和随宽度增加的降维效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度神经网络的理论性质，特别是带权重衰减的两层ReLU网络的理论特性仍然不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 理论推导了超参数导致全局最小值坍缩到零解的充分条件；通过实验对比AdamW和SGD优化器下的参数行为；在输入维度为一维时，解析推导了两层ReLU网络的全局最优参数集。

Result: 发现了全局最小值坍缩到零解的充分条件；AdamW能防止参数坍缩而SGD不能；一维情况下ℓ2正则化具有宽度不变的连接效应，但降维效应随网络宽度增加而增强。

Conclusion: 这些结果为宽度相关的超参数如何影响正则化损失景观的几何形状提供了洞见，并可能解释AdamW在深度学习训练中的成功。

Abstract: Understanding deep neural networks remains a central challenge in machine learning. In particular, the theoretical properties of even two-layer ReLU networks, especially in the presence of weight decay, remain poorly understood. To this end, we derive a sufficient condition on the hyperparameter settings under which the global minima collapse to the zero solution. Interestingly, our experiments reveal that using AdamW as an optimizer prevents the collapse of the learned parameters, whereas using SGD does not, which may help explain the success of AdamW in deep learning training. In addition, when restricting the input dimension to one, we derive an analytical solution for the globally optimal parameter sets of two-layer ReLU networks and show that $\ell_2$-regularization has a width-invariant effect on connectivity, but its dimensionality-reducing effect becomes stronger as the network width increases. These results provide insight into how width-dependent hyperparameters influence the geometry of regularized loss landscapes.

</details>


### [84] [Half the Experts, All the Code: One-Shot Domain Pruning of Mixture-of-Experts LLMs for Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16721)
*Anik Jha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了从混合专家（MoE）代码模型中剪枝专家子网络的效果。发现可以移除一半专家而不显著影响代码生成能力，但不同模型系列的最佳剪枝策略不同。同时指出困惑度作为剪枝评估指标不可靠，轻量微调可恢复约一半损失，剪枝相对于量化仅在低位宽（<3比特）场景有优势，且单次评测会高估压缩方法的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最强的开源代码模型多为MoE架构，其参数量主要来自大量专家子网络，但每个token仅激活少数专家。这导致模型体积庞大，超出大多数开发者的硬件能力。然而对于仅需代码帮助的用户而言，大部分专家编码的能力永远不会被调用。因此研究如何在不显著影响代码能力的前提下精简专家数量，节省存储和推理资源。

Method: 对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B和Gemma-4-26B-A4B两个MoE模型，采用五种专家选择策略进行剪枝，以代码正确性作为评估标准。进一步通过轻量微调和量化基线对比，并引入多轮错误修复（agentic evaluation）评估压缩方法的真实性能损失。

Result: 1）可从任一模型中移除一半专家，代码基准测试上无统计显著下降，损失集中在非代码能力；2）最佳剪枝策略在两个模型系列间不一致；3）困惑度可能将损坏模型评为优于完整模型；4）轻量微调可恢复约一半激进剪枝的损失；5）剪枝仅在量化需降至3比特/权重以下时优于量化；6）五次尝试推翻这一交叉点均失败；7）多轮修复评测显示单次基准测试广泛高估压缩损失，一次修复即可消除2-bit量化的全部损失。

Conclusion: 专家剪枝可以有效压缩代码MoE模型，但不同模型系列需要分别验证最佳策略。困惑度不是可靠的剪枝评估指标。剪枝相对量化的优势仅存在于极低位宽场景。单次评测会系统性地高估压缩方法的性能损失，引入多轮修复的评估方式能更准确反映实际使用中的表现。

Abstract: The strongest open-weight coding models are mixture-of-experts (MoE) networks: most of their size comes from large pools of "expert" subnetworks, of which only a few act on any token. That pool is why these models do not fit on the machines most developers own, yet for a user who only wants coding help, most experts encode abilities that will never be invoked. We ask how many experts can be removed, and which, by pruning two recent open-weight MoE models from different families (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B and Gemma-4-26B-A4B) under five selection strategies, judged the way a user would: by whether the model still writes correct code. Half the experts can be removed from either model with no statistically detectable loss on the primary code benchmark, and the damage lands almost entirely on abilities outside coding, the intended trade. But the winning strategy flips between the two models, so a recipe validated on one family cannot be assumed to work on another. We further show that perplexity, the metric much of the pruning literature leans on, can rate a broken model above an intact one; that a lightweight fine-tune recovers about half of what aggressive pruning loses; and that against quantizing the full model to the same memory, pruning wins only where quantization would have to drop below 3 bits per weight. Five attempts to overturn that crossover, with failure criteria fixed in advance (better calibration, guarded selection, causal expert importance, failure attribution, and an agentic evaluation letting each model repair its failures from execution feedback), all leave it standing; the last shows single-shot benchmarks overstate compression penalties broadly, as one repair turn erases the 2-bit quantization penalty entirely. Expert pruning works, but it demands per-model validation on the task the model will actually serve.

</details>


### [85] [Graph-Embedded Intuitionistic Fuzzy Broad Learning System: A Multi-view Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16728)
*Yogesh Kumar,Manju,Mudasir Ganaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MVGIFBLS，融合多视图学习、图嵌入和直觉模糊理论到BLS框架中，在多个基准数据集上取得了更高的AUC分数和更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BLS对所有数据点赋予相同重要性，忽略了数据的几何结构，对噪声和离群点敏感，且难以处理多源数据。

Method: 将多视图学习、图嵌入（基于局部Fisher判别分析的内在子空间和惩罚子空间）和直觉模糊理论集成到BLS框架中，并结合核邻域分析捕获局部数据结构。

Result: 在UCI、KEEL和AwA数据集上，MVGIFBLS在AUC分数和Gaussian特征噪声鲁棒性方面均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: MVGIFBLS每个组件都对整体性能有正面贡献，模型在分类精度和抗噪能力上表现一致优越。

Abstract: The Broad Learning System (BLS) has been widely used for data classification and is based on a layer-by-layer feed-forward structure. However, it gives the same importance to all data points, which reduces its effectiveness on real-world datasets with noise and outliers. In addition, it does not consider the geometric structure of the data and has limitations in handling data from multiple sources. To address these challenges, we propose a Multi-View Graph-Embedded Intuitionistic Fuzzy Broad Learning System (MVGIFBLS) that integrates multi-view learning, graph embedding, and intuitionistic fuzzy theory into the BLS framework. This design enables the model to combine information from multiple sources and learn more discriminative representations. Graph embedding captures the geometric relationships among samples and improves class separation through intrinsic and penalty subspaces based on local Fisher discriminant analysis. Intuitionistic fuzzy theory enhances robustness to noise, while kernel-based neighborhood analysis captures local data structures. We evaluate the proposed framework on several UCI, KEEL, and AwA benchmark datasets using comparative evaluation, Gaussian feature noise analysis, ablation studies, and statistical analysis. The results demonstrate that each component contributes positively to the overall framework and that the proposed MVGIFBLS consistently achieves higher Area Under the Curve (AUC) scores and maintains robust performance under Gaussian feature noise.

</details>


### [86] [BG4Sea: Biogeochemical Seasonal Forecastability via Progressive Information Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16731)
*Gabriela Martinez Balbontin,Anastase Charantonis,Dominique Bereziat,Stefano Ciavatta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BG4Sea是首个全球尺度的数据驱动海洋生物地球化学季节预报系统，采用模块化架构（列自编码器、潜空间预报器、表面强迫调节、水平耦合模块），在BIORYS4再分析数据上评估，可实现1/4度分辨率、六个月的多元季节预报，性能优于持久性和气候态基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋生物地球化学预报对生态系统和碳循环管理日益重要，但全球季节性预报产品因过程复杂和数据稀缺而远落后于物理海洋学，亟需开发数据驱动的全球尺度预报系统。

Method: 采用模块化架构：列自编码器将垂直剖面压缩为低维潜变量，潜空间预报器进行时间传播，表面强迫调节模块通过FiLM注入物理边界信息，水平耦合模块通过交叉注意力融入相邻网格上下文。在NEMO/PISCES全球海洋再分析BIORYS4上训练评估。

Result: 在1/4度、月分辨率上实现六个月的多元季节预报，涵盖溶解化学、生物学和碳库变量，在大多数变量和预报时效上优于持久性和气候态基准。

Conclusion: BG4Sea作为可解释的基准模型，为未来更具表达力的方法奠定基础；讨论了各模块的可预测性归因及模型结构局限性。

Abstract: Marine biogeochemical forecasting is increasingly important for managing marine ecosystems and the carbon cycle, yet global, seasonal forecast products lag far behind physical oceanography, held back by the complexity of the processes involved and by data scarcity. We introduce BG4Sea, which to our knowledge is the first global, data-driven system to produce multivariate seasonal forecasts of the marine biogeochemical state. BG4Sea is a modular architecture with a column autoencoder that compresses the vertical column into a low-dimensional latent space, a latent forecaster propagates this representation forward in time, a surface-forcing conditioner that injects physical boundary information via Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), and a horizontal-coupling module that incorporates neighboring-column context through cross-attention. The model is trained and evaluated on the global ocean reanalysis BIORYS4 (NEMO/PISCES), and produces six-month forecasts at 1/4 degree, monthly resolution for dissolved chemistry, biology, and carbon-pool variables, outperforming persistence and climatology across most variables and lead times. We position BG4Sea as an interpretable baseline for future, more expressive approaches, and discuss predictability attribution to each component, alongside the model's structural limitations.

</details>


### [87] [The Anatomy of a Truth Direction: Knowledge-Dependent Dimensionality, a Relational Law, and a Convergent Category Geometry in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16741)
*Francesco Karim Vicidomini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM中的truth表示在知识已知时集中在一维子空间（AUC 0.938），随知识下降或异质性增加而扩散；注意力传播truth框架，FFN反向对抗，SwiGLU值流是因果衰减源；跨家族语义排列收敛，几何混合属于知识域而非架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Bürger等人证明truth表示在LLM中跨极性通用且位于多维子空间的基础上，进一步探究：(1) truth子空间的维度如何依赖模型知识；(2) 哪个架构组件构建了truth方向；(3) truth方向究竟是什么的混合。

Method: Part I：基于隐藏态最小对SVD的无训练方向探针，在知识已知/未知/异质性条件下观测维度变化，并进行7项证伪实验。Part II：在4个基础模型（2个家族）上进行预注册预测，分析注意力、FFN、SwiGLU值流对truth框架的因果贡献，通过33类别、888对/关系、6种种子进行压力测试。

Result: (1) truth信号在行为已知事实下集中于一维（AUC 0.938），知识下降时扩散；无需极性标签即可恢复Bürger等人监督方向（余弦0.959）。(2) 注意力传播truth框架，FFN对抗当前块框架，SwiGLU值流是post-peak衰减的因果来源。(3) 跨家族语义排列收敛（Mantel p=0.0009），符号不稳定可通过谱共识标度修复；收敛强度从混合知识的+0.42提升至知识受限类别的+0.74。

Conclusion: truth方向的几何混合属于知识域而非架构，形成知识门控定律：信号维度、方向排列和跨模型收敛均受事实知识状态调控。

Abstract: Bürger et al. (2024) demonstrated that truth representations in large language models are universal across statement polarity but reside within a multidimensional subspace. We extend that framework along three questions: how the dimensionality of the subspace depends on the model's knowledge, which architectural component builds the truth direction, and what the direction is a mixture of. In Part I (one model), a training-free directional probe derived from the SVD of hidden-state minimal pairs shows that the dimensionality of truth is knowledge-dependent: the signal is concentrated on a single axis for behaviorally known facts (held-out AUC 0.938) and becomes diffuse as knowledge decreases or material heterogeneity increases; seven falsification experiments sustain the one-dimensional reading, and the same decomposition recovers the supervised polarity direction of Bürger et al. at cosine 0.959 without polarity labels. In Part II (four base models, two families, pre-registered predictions), a relational law emerges: attention propagates truth frames it did not write, the feed-forward network opposes the frame of its current block, and the genuine post-peak decay is causally attributed on all four models to the SwiGLU value stream; per-category truth axes form a semantically signed arrangement that contradicts the lexical surface and converges across families (Mantel p = 0.0009). Stress tests at three scales (33 categories, 888 pairs per relation, six sampling seeds) expose a sign instability of the per-category orientation, repair it with a declared spectral consensus gauge, and sharpen the convergence into a knowledge-gated law, strengthening from +0.42 on dense mixed-knowledge material to +0.74 on knowledge-restricted categories: the geometry of the mixture belongs to the knowledge domain rather than to the architecture.

</details>


### [88] [Robust Losses from Univariate Base Functions for Noisy-Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16768)
*Peng Hu,Jianwei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用框架，通过单变量基函数构造鲁棒多分类损失函数，并设计了两种互补方案（目标分离和二元约简），理论分析了鲁棒性条件，实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒损失函数大多直接在最终多分类目标层面设计，难以系统地表征和扩展其鲁棒性属性，缺乏统一的构建方法论。

Method: 提出从单变量基函数通过映射算子构造多分类损失的通用框架，设计了两种互补方案：目标分离（类间独立）和二元约简（类间依赖），并分析了对标性与非对称性的充分条件。

Result: 在合成和真实噪声标签基准上，所提损失函数在各种噪声设置下取得竞争性或更优性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为噪声鲁棒损失设计提供了统一的理论准则，并为构建对称损失提供了新路径，是对基于归一化的对称损失设计的补充。

Abstract: Learning with noisy labels is a fundamental problem in training reliable deep neural networks. Robust loss functions provide a direct and effective way to mitigate the adverse effects of label noise. However, most existing robust losses are designed directly at the level of the final multiclass objective, which makes it difficult to systematically characterize and extend their robustness properties. In this paper, we propose a general framework that constructs robust multiclass losses from univariate base functions. By defining mapping operators from base functions to multiclass losses, the robustness of the induced losses can be characterized through simple properties of the base functions. We develop two complementary construction schemes, Target Separation and Binary Reduction, corresponding to inter-class independent and inter-class dependent formulations, respectively. For both schemes, we analyze their symmetry and asymmetry properties and derive corresponding sufficient conditions, which provide theoretical criteria for noise-robust loss design. The proposed framework also provides a new route to constructing symmetric losses, serving as a complement to normalization-based symmetric loss designs. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world noisy-label benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed losses achieve competitive or superior performance under various noise settings.

</details>


### [89] [On the Potential of Graph Neural Networks as Metamodels for Supply Chain Optimization: Dataset, Architectures, and Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16769)
*Tushar Lone,Neha Karanjkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用图神经网络（GNN）作为供应链系统的替代模型，能够同时进行结构和参数优化，并创建了公开数据集SupplyNetPy，探索了GNN架构在节点和网络级预测中的准确性-计算权衡，最后讨论了梯度优化、设计空间探索和敏感性分析等未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统元模型无法同时处理图拓扑结构和参数的联合优化，而GNN具备跨拓扑泛化的能力，但GNN在供应链问题中的应用尚属空白。

Method: 1) 形式化问题定义；2) 创建大规模公开训练数据集，使用SupplyNetPy仿真库程序化生成供应链图及稳态性能指标；3) 探索适用于节点级和网络级预测的GNN架构；4) 分析GNN替代模型与仿真之间的准确性-计算权衡。

Result: 确定了在节点和网络级预测中表现良好的GNN架构，并量化了其与仿真相比较的准确性-计算权衡关系。

Conclusion: GNN作为供应链替代模型前景广阔，可开辟基于梯度的拓扑优化、快速设计空间探索和敏感性分析等新研究方向。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a powerful, differentiable class of learning models for graph-structured systems. Their ability to generalize across topologies opens the prospect of a surrogate for combined structural and parametric optimization, which classical metamodels cannot offer. Supply chains are a natural target, yet the use of GNN surrogates for supply chain problems is largely unexplored. This paper lays the foundation, presents initial steps, and discusses key research directions. As a foundation, we formulate the problem and create a large public training dataset of programmatically generated supply chain graphs with input parameters and steady-state performance metrics obtained using our SupplyNetPy simulation library. As initial steps, we explore GNN architectures that work well as surrogates for node- and network-level predictions, and analyze their accuracy-compute trade-off against simulation. Most importantly, we outline the exciting directions this opens, namely gradient-based optimization over topology, fast design-space exploration, and sensitivity analysis.

</details>


### [90] [MultiLoReFT: Decoupling Shared and Modality-Specific Subspaces in Multimodal Learning via Low-Rank Representation Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16789)
*Sana Tonekaboni,Viktoria Schuster,Caroline Uhler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MultiLoReFT是一个高效的低秩表示微调框架，通过可解释的投影子空间解耦多模态学习中的共享信息和模态特有信息，支持基于预训练单模态模型的多模态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态感知和决策本质上是多模态的，但面临两个主要障碍：1) 收集大规模、良好对齐的配对多模态数据集不切实际，使得端到端多模态训练困难；2) 现有多模态表示常将跨模态共享信息与模态特有信息纠缠，阻碍可解释性和可控性。

Method: 将低秩适配（LoRA）扩展到多模态设置，学习可解释的投影子空间，用于解耦共享信息和模态特有信息。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界基准测试中，MultiLoReFT产生的表示既支持多模态预测，又明确揭示了共享和模态特有信息在模态间的分布方式。

Conclusion: MultiLoReFT是一种高效、可扩展的多模态表示微调方法，有效解决了多模态数据稀缺和表示纠缠的问题，提升了可解释性和可控性。

Abstract: Real-world perception and decision making are inherently multimodal, integrating complementary signals across modalities. However, training multimodal models faces two main obstacles. First, collecting large-scale, well-aligned paired multimodal datasets is often impractical, making end-to-end multimodal training difficult. Second, existing multimodal representations frequently entangle information shared across modalities with modality-specific information, hindering interpretability and control. We introduce MultiLoReFT, an efficient and scalable low-rank representation fine-tuning framework for multimodal learning with pretrained unimodal models. MultiLoReFT extends low-rank adaptation to the multimodal setting and learns interpretable projection subspaces that decouple shared and modality-specific information. Across simulated and real-world benchmarks, it produces representations that support multimodal prediction while explicitly revealing how shared and modality-specific information is distributed across modalities.

</details>


### [91] [Interpretable Anomaly and Drift Detection with Gaussian Mixture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16811)
*Behnam Asadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视高斯混合模型（GMM）作为一种轻量级、可解释的工具，用于异常检测和数据流中的分布漂移检测。通过三个关键技术选择（BIC自动选择分量数、极值理论设定阈值、以及利用"未解释质量"检测漂移），在7个基准上验证了GMM点检测器与主流方法竞争力相当，且漂移检测中"未解释质量"指标在特定场景下与MMD持平，所有告警均可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测和漂移检测方法要么缺乏可解释性（如深度学习模型），要么需要预设过多超参数（如GMM的组件数量）。作者希望提出一个既轻量又完全可解释的解决方案，让每个告警都能追溯到具体的统计原因。

Method: 1) 使用BIC自动选择GMM组件数（k-means初始化）；2) 对正常数据拟合GMM，用负对数似然评分，通过极值理论设定阈值控制误报率；3) 漂移检测使用"未解释质量"——流窗口中不匹配任何高斯分量的数据比例。对比方法包括MMD、两种GMM间散度、以及多种经典异常检测器。

Result: 点检测：GMM与Isolation Forest、LOF、OC-SVM、ECOD、COPOD、自编码器竞争，但很少更优。漂移检测：MMD整体最强，但"未解释质量"在异常形成新簇时与MMD持平，而在纯权重重分配场景下诚实失败。可解释性：异常点距离其最近高斯分量中位数3-10σ，正常点约1σ。

Conclusion: GMM在保持与主流方法竞争性能的同时，提供了独特的可解释性优势。每个告警都能明确解释：异常点偏离其最近"机制"的程度，漂移告警则报告未匹配已知机制的数据比例。该方法适合需要透明度和可解释性的应用场景。

Abstract: We revisit Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) as a lightweight, interpretable tool for anomaly detection and, in particular, for detecting distributional drift in data streams. We make three practical choices explicit and evaluate them on seven public benchmarks. First, the number of mixture components is selected automatically by the Bayesian Information Criterion, initialised by k-means, removing the need to fix it in advance. Second, individual observations are scored by their negative log-likelihood under a GMM fitted to normal data, with thresholds set at a target false-alarm rate using Extreme Value Theory. Third, the same interpretable model extends to distributional drift: each Gaussian component is a named "regime," and the fraction of a stream window that matches no regime -- its unexplained mass -- is a drift signal that is itself the explanation. We benchmark this against a model-free kernel two-sample test (Maximum Mean Discrepancy, MMD) and against two GMM-to-GMM divergences (a closed-form Cauchy-Schwarz divergence and a matching-based KL surrogate). Across seven benchmarks ranging from 3 to 64 dimensions and five random splits, the GMM point detector is competitive with -- though rarely more accurate than -- Isolation Forest, Local Outlier Factor, one-class SVM, ECOD, COPOD and an autoencoder, while uniquely yielding an interpretable model. For drift, MMD is the strongest pure detector, but the interpretable unexplained-mass statistic matches it when anomalies form novel regimes (and honestly fails, as MMD does not, when drift is a pure re-weighting of existing regimes). Every alarm is explainable: anomalies lie a median of 3-10 sigma outside their nearest regime vs. about 1 sigma for normal points, and a drift alarm reports the fraction of the window matching no known regime. All code and experiments are released.

</details>


### [92] [Honest Physical-Support Inference after Latent Dictionary Learning: Collision Singularities and Minimax Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16813)
*Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对字典学习后的稀疏支持不确定性量化问题，提出了一种基于鲁棒训练矩区域、投影置换不变支持空间的置信对应方法，能够在固定维高斯训练-测试实验中提供诚实、分辨率自适应的支持推断，并表征了其统计代价与决策理论收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏支持不确定性量化假设字典已知，但实际中字典是从潜在稀疏混合中学习得到的，这一假设会导致过自信、标签依赖的结论。当相干原子的近碰撞发生时，测试信号可能识别出活跃物理组，但训练数据无法区分组内的物理射线。因此需要一种对字典学习后活跃物理射线（单元原子模符号）进行推断的框架。

Method: 在固定维高斯训练-测试实验中，保留所有与鲁棒训练矩区域兼容的字典，对测试表示在这些字典上取轮廓（profile），然后将存活的配置投影到置换不变的支持空间上。通过残差块方向首先在三次阶影响潜在训练密度，产生关于块内碰撞尺度s的s^6阶信息。最终给出具有高概率训练条件测试覆盖的置信对应关系，其分辨率由父级可检测性、测试时支持分离度和学习字典方向分别控制。

Result: 在已解析的固定壳体制下，投影Hausdorff直径以极小极大最优速率s ∧ (√N s²)^{-1}（至多常数因子）收缩。受限任务定理表明，系数不对称性允许测试复制补充训练信息，而校正不确定性在特定条件下仍不可约。方法能够报告跨片不确定、组分辨率伴子歧义、或细粒度支持分辨率。

Conclusion: 该框架能够产生诚实（honest）、分辨率自适应的支持推断陈述，并指导训练与测试测量之间的资源分配，为字典学习后的不确定性量化提供了决策理论层面的保证。

Abstract: Sparse-support uncertainty is usually quantified by treating the dictionary as known, an assumption that can produce overconfident, label-dependent conclusions when the dictionary is learned from latent sparse mixtures. Near collisions of coherent atoms, a test signal may identify the active physical group even though the training data cannot distinguish the physical rays within it.
  We develop inference for active physical rays, unit atoms modulo sign, after latent dictionary learning. In a fixed-dimensional Gaussian train-test experiment, we retain all dictionaries compatible with a robust training-moment region, profile the test representation over them, and project surviving configurations onto a permutation-invariant support space. The resulting confidence correspondence can report cross-sheet inconclusiveness, group resolution with child ambiguity, or fine-support resolution.
  We characterize both its statistical cost and decision-theoretic benefit. Residual block orientation first affects the latent training density at cubic order, yielding information of order $s^6$, where $s$ is the within-block collision scale. The correspondence provides high-probability-over-training conditional test coverage, with resolution governed separately by parent detectability, test-time support separation, and learned-dictionary orientation. In the resolved fixed-shell regime, its projective Hausdorff diameter contracts at the minimax-optimal rate $s \wedge (\sqrt{N}s^2)^{-1}$, up to constants. A restricted-task theorem further determines when coefficient asymmetry allows test replication to supplement training information and when calibration uncertainty remains irreducible. The framework thus yields honest, resolution-adaptive support statements and guides the allocation of training versus test measurements.

</details>


### [93] [First-Order Predictable but Pairwise Fragile: Local Task Adaptation in Trained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16821)
*Irina Piontkovskaia,Sergey Nikolenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统测量了围绕多任务LoRA工作点的9个Transformer模型（82M-7B）的8种局部性质，发现：沿单一方向的扰动在$10^{-2}$尺度内保持一阶可预测性，但配对组合和更新顺序的通用半径不存在；超过三分之一的（模型、任务对）组合中存在更新顺序敏感性；任务梯度子空间在数十步内旋转；全任务向量尺度下加性失效；顺序任务梯度的主导阶依赖项为Lie括号，其归一化预测中位比达1.002，而起始尺度跨越三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务算术、顺序微调、激活引导和一阶随机搜索等方法都依赖于对预训练检查点的小扰动和局部线性近似，但这些假设（如一阶可预测性、任务更新的可加性、正切结构的稳定性、权重编辑与表征空间的对应性）在不同模型和任务组合下是否成立尚不清楚，缺乏系统性的大规模实证研究。

Method: 围绕多任务LoRA工作点，在9个Transformer（82M-7B）上，使用前瞻性注册的属性列表、阈值和测试集，测量了8种局部性质，包括：一阶可预测性、配对组合干扰、更新顺序敏感性、梯度子空间旋转、加性失效、权重-引导对应性等。对顺序任务梯度步骤，推导出主导阶依赖项为Lie括号$H_B\mathbf{g}_A-H_A\mathbf{g}_B$，并验证其归一化预测$c(\eta)=\eta\kappa+O(\eta^2)$。

Result: 1) 沿单一方向，探针损失的变化在$10^{-2}$尺度内保持一阶可预测，扰动效应本质上是其在梯度上的投影；2) 配对结构脆弱：超1/3的（模型、任务对）组合中更新顺序敏感性的起始点位于上述窗口之内；任务梯度子空间在数十步内旋转；多个模型（含两个7B模型）上全任务向量尺度的加性失效；无模型通过全局平均向量权重-引导对应性测试；3) Lie括号的归一化预测$c(\eta)=\eta\kappa+O(\eta^2)$与实测缺陷的中位比为1.002，起始尺度$\eta^\dagger\approx0.10/\kappa$跨越三个数量级。

Conclusion: 局部一阶可预测性广泛成立，但高阶（配对组合、顺序依赖）性质远比预期脆弱，不能假设存在通用的有效半径；Lie括号理论框架能精准刻画顺序梯度更新的非交换性，但其起始尺度因模型和任务对而异，跨度达三个数量级，提示在模型编辑和持续学习中需谨慎对待局部线性假设。

Abstract: Task arithmetic, sequential fine-tuning, activation steering, and first-order random search all operate through relatively small perturbations around an already trained checkpoint, and they rely on different local approximations: individual perturbations should be first-order predictable, task updates should compose with controlled interference, useful tangent structure should be stable and possible to estimate, and weight edits should have counterparts in representation space. We measure 8 such properties with the same harness around a multitask LoRA operating point, on 9 transformers (82M-7B), with a prospectively registered property list, thresholds, and test split. We find a shared one-direction validity window up to the tested scale $10^{-2}$, but no universal radius for pairwise composition or update ordering. Along individual directions, changes of the probe loss remain first-order predictable throughout the grid: a perturbation's effect on the loss is essentially its projection onto the gradient, which is also what makes local random search work. Pairwise structure, however, proves to be far more fragile: on over a third of the measured (model, task pair) combinations, two-update order sensitivity sets in strictly inside that window; task-gradient subspaces rotate within tens of steps; additivity under our fixed activation probe fails at full task-vector scale on several models, including both held-out 7B models; and no model median passes the registered global mean-vector weight-to-steering correspondence bar. For two sequential task-gradient steps, the leading order-dependent term is the Lie bracket $H_B\textbf{g}_A-H_A\textbf{g}_B$; its normalized prediction $c(η)=ηκ+O(η^2)$ tracks the measured defect at median ratio 1.002, while the onset scale $η^\dagger\approx0.10/κ$ spans three orders of magnitude across models and task pairs.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond Memory Leaderboards: Evaluating Scientific Memory as Budgeted Context Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16848)
*Maksim Sheverev,David Finkelstein,Sergey Nikolenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两个科学文献记忆基准（PAIM和PTr），评估八种记忆/检索系统（含自研Theoria），发现记忆排行榜需结合完整协议（如检索预算、粒度等）才可解释，且稀疏-稠密混合检索是最有效的单一干预手段。研究论证了科学记忆应作为带预算、模态感知的上下文恢复任务来评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆基准多聚焦对话或摘要，缺乏面向研究型代理从完整科学论文中恢复证据的评估。需要构建专门针对科学文献全文本的记忆评测基准。

Method: 构建PAIM（81篇论文，66问）和PTr（252篇论文，98问）两个基准；评估八种记忆/检索系统（含自研Theoria及无检索基线）；通过控制检索预算、粒度、裁判选择等变量进行系统比较；使用多裁判和人工侧对侧校准验证LLM评判一致性。

Result: 记忆排行榜依赖完整协议，如Graphiti在PAIM领先但每次查询消耗260万字符上下文，控制预算后优势消失；在PTr上，稀疏-稠密混合检索是最有效干预，混合变体的Simple RAG、Mem0和Theoria在0.03分内并列领先；LLM裁判与人类评价一致，有效分辨率约为10分制中的1分。

Conclusion: 科学记忆评估应从无约束架构排行榜转向带预算、模态感知的上下文恢复任务。研究公开了数据集、框架、原始输出及复现代码。

Abstract: Long-term memory is becoming a core component of LLM agents, but most memory benchmarks evaluate conversations or compact summaries, while research agents need to restore evidence from full scientific papers. We introduce two full-text scientific-memory benchmarks, Public AI Memory (PAIM; 81 papers, 66 questions) and Public Transformers (PTr; 252 papers, 98 questions). We evaluate eight memory/retrieval systems, including our own proposed Theoria, plus a no-retrieval baseline. Our results show that memory leaderboards are not interpretable without the full protocol: ingestion granularity, raw-text preservation, retrieval budget, retrieval modality, rubric audit, and judge choice all affect the outcome. For example, on PAIM Graphiti wins convincingly but uses 2.6M characters of retrieved context per query, and after controlling for retrieval budget the lead disappears. On PTr, for the systems where BM25 retrieval can be added cleanly, the sparse-dense hybrid is the single most significant intervention: hybrid variants of Simple RAG, Mem0, and Theoria tie for the lead within 0.03 points. Multi-judge and human side-by-side calibration show that LLM-as-a-judge rankings are consistent across frontier judges and agree with human evaluation, with an effective resolution of roughly one point on a ten-point scale. We argue that scientific memory should be evaluated as budgeted, modality-aware context restoration rather than as an unconstrained architecture leaderboard, and we release the datasets, harness, raw outputs, judgments, and scripts to reproduce our results and serve as tools for such evaluation. Our code is available at http://gitlab.com/quantellence/research/scientific-recall-bench , and the datasets are available at http://huggingface.co/datasets/quantellence/srb-data .

</details>


### [95] [Principled Direction-Free Intrinsic Motivation through Model-Free Epistemic Free-Energy Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16858)
*Alireza Furutanpey,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于预期自由能（Expected Free Energy）的无偏好内在奖励，通过参数信息增益（parameter information gain）区分可解释的惊奇与不可约噪声，在混合不确定性环境中实现无监督强化学习的稳定探索与利用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督强化学习方法在处理混合不确定性源时存在缺陷：惊奇最小化局限于不稳定环境，预测误差好奇心奖励包含不可约噪声，而bandit/混合切换方法人为引入非平稳性。需要一种统一的、内在平稳的内在奖励机制。

Method: 提出单一内在奖励函数，来源于无偏好预期自由能目标并以奖励最大化形式表达。核心是参数信息增益——下一状态的预期惊奇减去不可约部分。使用伪计数提供认知价值，探针惩罚捕获偶然方差，短视门保护信息性后继状态。窗口冻结机制使所有奖励定义对象保持平稳，保证Bellman算子平稳性。

Result: 在非参数估计器下，在混合性、平滑性、带宽和容量假设下，得到了学习目标的显式界限和条件均匀集中结果。认知项在未解析动力学区域驱动探索，在已解析区域消失；偶然惩罚偏好低方差转移，无需拟合显式下一状态预测器。

Conclusion: 在主动推理框架下，该智能体在保留新奇性的区域是无偏好的；完全可观测性下标准似然歧义消失；在已解析状态空间区域自然涌现出惊奇最小化行为，实现了平稳且自适应的探索-利用权衡。

Abstract: Across environments with mixed sources of uncertainty, unsupervised reinforcement learning requires intrinsic motivation that does not precommit to a particular direction of surprise. Surprise minimization is scoped by design to ``unstable'' environments. Prediction-error curiosity rewards total expected surprise, including irreducible noise. Bandit or mixture switching between surprise-minimizing and surprise-maximizing rewards reintroduces non-stationarity by construction. We propose a single intrinsic reward, stationary within each window, derived from the novelty contribution of a preference-free Expected Free Energy objective, expressed in reward-maximization form. Our claim is that parameter information gain, the expected surprise of the next state minus its irreducible part, is the appropriate intrinsic signal in both high-entropy and low-entropy components of the state space. Maximizing it seeks exactly the surprise the model can explain away. In regions of unresolved dynamics, this epistemic term drives exploration. As dynamics become resolved, the epistemic term vanishes, while an aleatoric penalty favors lower-variance transitions, all without fitting an explicit next-state predictor. A pseudocount supplies epistemic value, a probe-based penalty captures aleatoric variance, and a short-horizon gate protects informative successors. A window-based freeze of all reward-defining objects yields a stationary Bellman operator, explicit bounds on learning targets, and a conditional uniform-concentration result for the nonparametric estimators under mixing, smoothness, bandwidth, and capacity assumptions. In active-inference terms, the agent is preference-free where novelty is retained, standard likelihood ambiguity vanishes under full observability, a nonstandard transition-entropy penalty is added, and surprise minimization emerges in resolved regions of the state space.

</details>


### [96] [Bridging battery design and health assessment through virtual sensing and physics-informed learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16864)
*Wendi Guo,Søren Byg Vilsen,Daniel Ioan Stroe,Yaqi Li,Yicun Huang,Ashima Verma,Daniel Brandell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息学习框架，结合虚拟传感技术，从标准BMS测量中推断难以测量的电池设计参数，嵌入数字孪生衍生的颗粒开裂机制作为软约束，仅需2%早期数据即可将寿命预测误差降低6-8倍，并实现设计参数的无传感器在线推断，形成部署与开发之间的闭环反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电池管理系统与材料/结构老化根源脱节，限制可解释健康评估和电池设计优化；亟需一种能从标准BMS测量中推断隐藏设计参数、桥接宏观行为与微观退化过程的物理可解释方法。

Method: 提出物理信息学习框架：1) 虚拟传感从标准BMS信号推断固相扩散系数、电极厚度、离子浓度、颗粒尺寸等设计参数；2) 嵌入数字孪生衍生的颗粒开裂机制作为模型软约束；3) 仅需部分验证的物理机制配合有限数据进行联合优化，无需完全解析控制方程。

Result: 轨迹和寿命预测误差相比SOTA机器学习基线降低6-8倍（仅需2%早期观测数据）；容量损失误差降低39%，EOL误差降低17%，预测变异性降低54%。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了部署与开发之间的实用反馈闭环，证明了实际运行数据可持续指导上游设计决策，适用于复杂多物理系统。

Abstract: Supercharging of lithium-ion batteries (LiBs) requires robust health monitoring to ensure durability, safety, and user confidence, particularly for emerging vehicle-to-grid applications with bidirectional energy flows. Yet battery management remains largely disconnected from the material and structural origins of aging, limiting both interpretable health assessment and informed battery design. Here we propose a physics-informed learning framework with virtual sensing that infers hard-to-measure design parameters, including solid-state diffusion coefficient, electrode thickness, ion concentration, and particle size, directly from standard battery management system (BMS) measurements. Across diverse fast-charging strategies and driving profiles, embedding a digital-twin-derived particle-cracking mechanism as a soft constraint reduces trajectory and lifetime prediction errors by 6-8 times relative to state-of-the-art machine learning baselines using only 2% early-life observations. We further show that accurate degradation extrapolation does not require fully resolved governing equations; validated partial mechanisms, jointly refined with limited data, provide sufficient guidance. Virtual sensing transforms standard charging signals into latent design variables without additional sensors, bridging observable battery behavior and underlying aging processes while reducing capacity loss error by up to 39%, end-of-life (EOL) error by 17%, and prediction variability by up to 54%, enabling real-time exploration of new battery configurations. More broadly, the proposed framework establishes a practical feedback loop between deployment and development, demonstrating how real-world operation can continuously inform upstream design decisions across complex multiphysics systems.

</details>


### [97] [HyBDM: Multi-Scale Hybrid Experts for Time Series Forecasting with Bidirectional Dependency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16882)
*Wenqiang Ma,Chen Cheng,Xue Cheng,Jiarui Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyBDM是一个多尺度混合模型，通过全局模式专家（BiConv-Mamba）和局部变化专家（局部窗口Transformer）联合建模时间序列中的全局和局部依赖关系，在六个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSF模型难以同时捕捉多元时间序列中异质性的长程全局模式和短程局部变化，且未能联合利用时间和特征维度信息。

Method: 提出HyBDM模型：(1) 全局模式专家采用增强型BiConv-Mamba模块，集成双向卷积、M-SSM层、遗忘机制和GDD-MLP进行跨通道建模；(2) 局部变化专家采用局部窗口Transformer实现高效的局部感知注意力；(3) 多尺度Patcher和长短路由器实现多分辨率表示和专家自适应融合。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，HyBDM在预测精度和计算效率方面均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: HyBDM有效桥接了多元时间序列预测中的全局-局部依赖关系，验证了混合架构在时间序列建模中的优越性。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) is vital to many applications, yet existing models often struggle to capture the heterogeneous long-range global patterns and short-range local variations in multivariate time series. While some approaches partially model these dependencies, they often do not jointly exploit temporal and feature-wise information. To address this challenge, we propose HyBDM, a multi-scale hybrid model that decomposes temporal dynamics into global patterns and local variations, which are modeled by two specialized experts. The Global Patterns Expert employs an enhanced BiConv-Mamba module that integrates bidirectional convolutions, an M-SSM layer, a forgetting mechanism, and a GDD-MLP module for cross-channel modeling. The Local Variations Expert uses a Local Window Transformer (LWT) to perform efficient locality-aware attention with reduced computational complexity. In addition, a Multi-Scale Patcher and a Long-Short Router enable multi-resolution representations and adaptive fusion of the two experts. Experiments on six benchmark datasets show that HyBDM outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both forecasting accuracy and computational efficiency, demonstrating its effectiveness in bridging global-local dependencies for multivariate TSF.

</details>


### [98] [TVGL-CFM:Generating and Forecasting Time-Varying Trajectories of Dynamic Networks with Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16894)
*Om Roy,Yashar Moshfeghi,Keith Malcolm Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TVGL-CFM是一个统一的生成模型，利用条件流匹配在log-Euclidean空间中学习时变精度矩阵轨迹的分布，能够生成和预测网络结构，在EEG、混沌系统和基因表达数据上优于原始信号基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多复杂系统的网络结构随时间变化，现有方法通常先生成原始信号再估计连接性，缺乏直接生成结构化精度矩阵轨迹的生成模型。

Method: 将时变精度矩阵轨迹通过log-Euclidean映射到平坦向量空间，使用条件流匹配模型进行生成和预测；预测时从粗略外推初始化流，只需学习小修正。

Result: 在EEG运动想象、混沌系统和基因表达数据上，生成的轨迹保持了真实数据的类别判别结构，预测未来连接性优于原始信号基线方法。

Conclusion: 直接生成结构化精度矩阵轨迹比先生成原始信号再估计连接性更忠实可靠。

Abstract: Many complex systems such as brain networks, financial markets, and gene-regulatory circuits are described not by a fixed graph but by one that changes over time. A standard way to summarise such structure at each instant is the sparse precision (inverse-covariance) matrix, and the time-varying graphical lasso (TVGL) turns a multivariate signal into a smooth chain of these matrices. We introduce TVGL-CFM, a single model that learns the distribution of such chains and can both generate new, realistic time-varying network trajectories for a given class and forecast how an observed trajectory will continue. Each precision matrix lives on a curved space of positive-definite matrices, but a log-Euclidean chart flattens an entire trajectory into an ordinary vector space, so a simple conditional flow-matching model can be trained and sampled there while every decoded matrix is guaranteed to be a valid precision matrix. For forecasting we start the flow not from noise but from a rough extrapolation of the recent history, so the model only has to learn a small correction. Across EEG motor-imagery, chaotic systems, and gene-expression data, TVGL-CFM generates trajectories that keep the class-discriminative structure of real data, and it forecasts future connectivity more accurately than raw-signal baselines. Generating the structured precision trajectory directly is therefore more faithful than generating raw signals and estimating connectivity afterwards.

</details>


### [99] [When Can Safe Controllers Adapt? Information before Commitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16895)
*Venkatesh Saligrama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究安全自适应控制中，安全约束是否允许通过在线实验获取足够信息来区分不同模型。核心发现是，在均匀安全策略下，若预承诺信息（commitment前的KL散度）有界，则控制器必须为安全保留一定概率不做出承诺（即不采取会导致无法在备选模型下安全继续的动作），从而导致与先知最优之间的差距（regret）中有一部分不可消除。若该差距为Ω(T)，则任何均匀安全策略都有线性遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全自适应控制分析通常假设闭环系统持续激励（persistent excitation），即数据能区分所有需要不同控制决策的模型对。在该假设下，可行性已解决，只需分析收敛速率。但一个根本问题被忽略了：安全约束本身是否允许进行如此信息丰富的实验？即安全约束是否会阻止控制器收集足够的数据来学习真实模型？

Method: 1. 定义"承诺"（commitment）：第一个会使得在某个备选模型下无法安全继续的动作。2. 定义"预承诺信息"（precommitment information）：在承诺发生之前，学习者可见的随机过程（law）之间的KL散度。3. 提出因果归约（causal reduction）：承诺规则同时决定了(1)在备选模型下安全允许承诺的概率，(2)不承诺时目标侧的成本，(3)决策时可用的信息。4. 证明有界预承诺信息导致固定比例的oracle差距不可消除。5. 在约束线性系统与二次调节成本中验证该障碍，并在确定性线性高斯系统中给出半定上界证书（semidefinite upper certificates）。

Result: 1. 预承诺信息有界时，任何均匀安全策略都无法避免与先知最优之间的固定比例差距。2. 若该差距为Ω(T)，则任何均匀安全策略都有线性遗憾。3. 在约束线性系统与二次调节成本的具体场景中，该障碍确实出现。4. 在确定性线性高斯系统中，可通过半定规划得到上界证书，某些情况下可恢复（即避免线性遗憾）。

Conclusion: 安全约束可能从根本上限制在线学习的效率：即便理论上存在信息丰富的实验，安全约束也可能阻止控制器执行这些实验，从而导致无法避免的线性遗憾。这一发现揭示了安全自适应控制中安全与学习之间的根本权衡。

Abstract: Safe adaptive control is online adaptation under a safety guarantee on the learning trajectory itself. The controller may use any causal, history-dependent rule and act differently across environments as data arrive. Only its safety guarantee is uniform: the same rule must satisfy it under every initially plausible model. Performance is measured against a safe oracle that knows the realized model. Many finite-time analyses assume persistent excitation of the uniformly safe closed loop, so the data distinguish every pair of models requiring different control decisions. Under that assumption, feasibility is already settled; only the rate remains. We ask instead: Do the safety constraints permit such an informative experiment at all?
  While an alternative remains plausible, the controller must preserve a safe continuation under it. We call the first action that forecloses such a continuation commitment. Chance safety allows commitment only on an event rare under the alternative, and the evidence must arrive beforehand: the observation generated by the committing action is too late. We define precommitment information as the KL divergence between learner-visible laws stopped before commitment.
  Our main result is a causal reduction. The commitment rule determines (1) the probability that safety permits commitment under the alternative, (2) the target-side cost of remaining noncommittal, (3) and the information available when the decision is made. Bounded precommitment information therefore leaves a fixed fraction of the oracle gap unavoidable. If the gap is Ω(T), every uniformly safe policy has linear regret. We establish the obstruction in a constrained linear system with quadratic regulation cost. We also prove recovery in special cases and derive semidefinite upper certificates for deterministic linear-Gaussian systems.

</details>


### [100] [Enhancing Personalized Bladder Cancer Treatment Through Reinforcement Learning: A Recurrent Patient State Transition Decision Support Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16916)
*Divyansh Chawla,Anshu Garg,Isshaan Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合患者状态转移模拟、马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）和深度Q网络（DQN）强化学习的递归框架，用于膀胱癌的个性化治疗规划，在模拟环境中实现了63,918.87的累计奖励、0.0056的平均训练损失和6.62%的策略改进，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膀胱癌治疗需要个性化和自适应的决策，尤其对于复发性疾病，治疗效果在连续的临床事件中会发生变化。传统的临床决策支持系统依赖静态治疗指南或单步预测模型，无法捕捉疾病随时间进展的动态过程。

Method: 构建了递归患者状态转移模拟框架，集成预测性状态转移建模、马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）和深度Q网络（DQN）强化学习环境。预测模块估计治疗后肿瘤特征的变化，强化学习智能体通过与模拟患者轨迹交互来顺序优化治疗决策。

Result: 累计奖励63,918.87，平均每轮训练损失0.0056，策略改进得分6.62%。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了递归患者状态转移模拟结合强化学习在个性化膀胱癌治疗规划中作为灵活决策支持系统的潜力，有助于AI辅助的精准肿瘤学发展。

Abstract: Bladder cancer treatment requires personalized and adaptive decision-making, particularly for recurrent disease, where treatment effectiveness changes across successive clinical episodes. Conventional clinical decision support systems typically rely on static treatment guidelines or single-step predictive models, limiting their ability to capture disease progression over time. This paper presents a recurrent patient state-transition simulation framework for bladder cancer treatment planning that integrates predictive state-transition modeling with a Markov Decision Process (MDP) and a Deep Q-Network (DQN) reinforcement learning environment. The predictive module estimates changes in tumor characteristics following treatment, while the reinforcement learning agent sequentially optimizes treatment decisions by interacting with simulated patient trajectories. This framework enables dynamic, patient-specific treatment planning by continuously adapting recommendations to evolving clinical states. It also generates interpretable treatment trajectories and detailed simulation logs to improve transparency and support clinical decision-making. The proposed framework was evaluated against existing reinforcement learning-based treatment planning approaches. It achieved a cumulative reward of 63,918.87, an average training loss per episode of 0.0056, and a policy improvement score of 6.62%, demonstrating effective sequential learning and robust treatment optimization in a simulated recurrent treatment environment. These findings highlight the potential of recurrent patient state-transition simulation with reinforcement learning as a flexible decision-support framework for personalized bladder cancer treatment planning and AI-assisted precision oncology.

</details>


### [101] [Investigation of Polycystic Ovary Syndrome (PCOS) Diagnosis Using Machine Learning Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16941)
*Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib,Md Asif Bin Syed,Md. Ekramul Islam,Tanpia Tasnim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结合特征工程和机器学习（AdaBoost + RF特征重要性 + 最高相关性）来预测多囊卵巢综合征（PCOS），在10个特征上取得最高测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCOS是育龄女性常见激素疾病，传统检测方法依赖临床评估、体格检查和实验室测试，耗时耗钱且资源需求高。现有大量医疗数据使得机器学习可用于早期诊断，因此本文旨在利用机器学习结合特征选择算法来高效预测PCOS。

Method: 采用数据驱动方法，结合特征工程与机器学习。使用了多种特征选择算法（CatBoost、XGBoost、LGBM、AdaBoost、RF）筛选特征子集，并用这些特征训练ML模型。

Result: AdaBoost结合RF特征重要性和最高相关性（HC）选择的10个特征，取得了最高的测试准确率。

Conclusion: 所提出的特征工程+ML方法能够有效预测PCOS，其中AdaBoost在最优特征子集上表现最佳，为PCOS的早期诊断提供了一种低成本、高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Polycystic Ovarian Syndrome (PCOS) is a widespread hormone problem for women of childbearing age. Women with PCOS may not ovulate; they might have high levels of androgens and have many small cysts on the ovaries. It can cause missed or irregular menstrual periods, excess hair growth, acne, infertility, and weight gain. Machine Learning (ML) can effectively diagnose this disease at an earlier stage as tons of medical data are available now. Traditional approaches to detect PCOS encompass a combination of clinical evaluation, medical history assessment, physical examination, and laboratory tests. These approaches aim to identify the characteristic symptoms and hormonal imbalances associated with PCOS. Physical examination requires good resources and costs time and money. In recent times, data-driven techniques have substantially advanced disease prediction within the medical field. We aim to utilize ML approaches, incorporating unique feature selection algorithms, to predict PCOS. This paper introduces a data-driven approach to PCOS diagnosis, combining Feature Engineering and ML. Several feature selection approaches have been considered to select sets of features for training the ML model, including CatBoost, Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), Light Gradient Boosting Machine (LGBM), AdaBoost, Random Forest (RF). Results demonstrate that AdaBoost, with ten features selected by RF Feature Importance and Highest Correlation (HC), provides the highest test accuracy.

</details>


### [102] [CADENCE: Closing the Reasoning Gap via Coverage-Adaptive On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16955)
*Satyam Kumar,Saurabh Jha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CADENCE是一个统一的知识蒸馏框架，通过DRIFT机制（逐token前向/反向KL混合代理损失）以及六个扩展组件（COVA自适应调度、FTB高熵位置梯度增强、CCD稠密奖励、LAP简洁偏好强化、EMR熵匹配正则化、BSD自蒸馏阶段），解决了on-policy知识蒸馏中的冷启动崩溃、状态无关散度调度和二元奖励稀疏三个问题。在GSM8K和MATH-500上，将0.5B学生模型从1.5B教师蒸馏至69.8%准确率（关闭63.2%教师差距），从3B教师蒸馏至72.1%（关闭76.2%），单台Apple Mac Studio即可完成实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy知识蒸馏方法存在三个叠加的失败模式：(i) 冷启动崩溃——新初始化的学生模型对教师偏好的token分配接近零概率；(ii) 状态无关的散度调度——仅依赖时间步的前向/反向KL插值忽略了学生的覆盖状态；(iii) 二元奖励稀疏——通过/失败信号丢弃了部分正确轨迹中的信息。

Method: 提出CADENCE框架，核心是DRIFT机制：在学生采样的轨迹上，对每个token使用前向KL和反向KL代理目标的逐token凸组合（不是序列级别的KL梯度估计器）。六个扩展组件：(A) COVA——覆盖自适应的β调度，加速前向到反向转换；(B) FTB——分叉token增强，通过全局归一化熵参考在高熵位置集中梯度；(C) CCD——稠密奖励，为不正确但接近的轨迹添加数值邻近部分分数；(D) LAP——偏好简洁正确轨迹的强化；(E) EMR——熵匹配校准正则化；(F) BSD——引导自蒸馏阶段。

Result: 在GSM8K和MATH-500上（修正的512-token协议，5个种子报告标准差），CADENCE将0.5B学生从1.5B教师蒸馏到69.8±0.5% GSM8K pass@1（预训练基线48.7%，关闭63.2%教师差距），从3B教师蒸馏到72.1±0.4%（关闭76.2%），比最强的匹配计算量的标签使用基线（DRIFT+二元奖励）高出4.4±0.7个点。所有实验在单台Apple Mac Studio（M系列，64GB统一内存）上运行。

Conclusion: 有原则的蒸馏方法可以在不需要数据中心级硬件的情况下达到强大的推理质量，CADENCE通过针对三个失败模式的定向修复，在小模型上显著提升了推理能力。

Abstract: On-policy knowledge distillation transfers reasoning from large teachers to compact students, but existing approaches suffer three compounding failure modes: (i) cold-start collapse, where a fresh student assigns near-zero mass to teacher-preferred tokens; (ii) state-agnostic divergence scheduling, where time-only forward/reverse-KL interpolation ignores the student's coverage state; and (iii) binary reward sparsity, where pass/fail signals discard information from partially correct traces.
  We present CADENCE, a unified framework with a targeted fix for each. Its DRIFT mechanism schedules a per-token convex mixture of forward-KL and reverse-KL surrogate objectives on student-sampled trajectories (per-token surrogates, not sequence-level KL gradient estimators). Six components extend it: (A) COVA, a coverage-adaptive $β$ schedule accelerating the forward-to-reverse transition; (B) FTB, a forking-token boost concentrating gradient at high-entropy positions via a globally-normalized entropy reference; (C) CCD, a dense reward adding numerical-proximity partial credit for incorrect-but-close traces; (D) LAP, brevity-preferential correct-rollout reinforcement; (E) EMR, an entropy-matching calibration regularizer; (F) BSD, a bootstrapped self-distillation phase.
  On GSM8K and MATH-500 (corrected 512-token protocol, 5 seeds, reported std), CADENCE distills a 0.5B student from a 1.5B teacher to 69.8 $\pm$ 0.5% GSM8K pass@1 (from 48.7% pretrained; 63.2% of the teacher gap closed) and to 72.1 $\pm$ 0.4% with a 3B teacher (76.2% closed), beating the strongest matched-compute label-using baseline (DRIFT+binary reward) by +4.4 $\pm$ 0.7 points. All experiments run on a single Apple Mac Studio (M-series, 64GB unified memory), showing principled distillation reaches strong reasoning quality without datacenter-scale hardware.

</details>


### [103] [SurvCF(t): Counterfactual Explanations for Survival Analysis in Predictive Maintenance Multivariate Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16969)
*Zara Karazian,Panagiotis Papapetrou,Sindri Magnússon,Erik Frisk,Tony Lindgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SurvCF(t)，首个面向时序数据生存模型的反事实解释框架。通过约束优化生成最小、合理且时序一致的操作变化，提升设备预测寿命，在C-MAPSS、N-CMAPSS及Scania真实数据集上验证了可解释性和可操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度生存模型在剩余寿命预测中性能优异，但黑箱特性限制了其在安全关键场景中的应用，缺乏可操作的决策洞察。

Method: 将反事实解释建模为约束优化问题，同时优化有效性、接近性、稀疏性和合理性，生成对资产运行历史的最小、合理且时序一致的修改。

Result: 在C-MAPSS、N-CMAPSS及Scania真实数据集上验证，SurvCF(t)能生成可操作且可解释的干预措施。

Conclusion: SurvCF(t)弥合了生存预测与规范性维护之间的鸿沟，实现了面向可解释和决策导向的AI维护策略。

Abstract: Predictive maintenance relies on accurate Remaining Useful Life estimation, often formulated using survival analysis over multivariate time-series data. While modern deep survival models achieve strong predictive performance, their black-box nature limits their use in safety-critical settings where actionable insight is required. In this work, we introduce \textit{SurvCF(t)}, the first framework for generating counterfactual explanations for survival models operating on time-series data. \textit{SurvCF(t)} identifies minimal, plausible, and temporally consistent changes to an asset's operational history that increase its predicted life time, framing explanation as a constrained optimization problem combining validity, proximity, sparsity, and plausibility. We evaluate the method on multiple benchmarks, including C-MAPSS, N-CMAPSS, and a real-world case study of the Scania Component\_X dataset, demonstrating its ability to produce actionable and interpretable interventions. Our results show that \textit{SurvCF(t)} bridges the gap between survival prediction and prescriptive maintenance, enabling explainable and decision-oriented AI for maintenance strategies.

</details>


### [104] [TurboVec: A Case Study in Cost-Efficient Private Retrieval for Enterprise RAG via Codebook-Oblivious Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16973)
*Navnit Shukla,Kamal Pandey,Omsankar Tiwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TurboVec，一种基于TurboQuant（无需训练的无码本标量量化器）的开源向量索引。在DBpedia OpenAI embeddings基准上，4-bit TurboQuant在相同内存预算下Recall@5比FAISS Product Quantization高8.5-8.9个百分点，且码本无关设计将成员推断攻击准确率降至近随机水平（50.0%），同时通过内核级允许列表过滤在多租户场景下保持0.86-0.93的Recall@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中的向量检索层存在两个未被充分研究的问题：（1）训练后的码本量化器在多租户部署中会在索引构建时暴露语料统计信息，形成泄露通道；（2）用于租户隔离的后期过滤会降低选择性查询的召回率。

Method: 提出TurboQuant，一种无需语料依赖训练的无码本标量量化器，并基于此构建TurboVec向量索引。采用内核级允许列表过滤替代传统的后期过滤机制，实现租户隔离而不会牺牲召回率。

Result: 4-bit TurboQuant在Recall@5上比FAISS PQ高8.5-8.9个百分点；TurboVec在100K向量规模下中位查询延迟为11ms，而暴力扫描需707ms；内核级过滤在10-1000租户工作负载下Recall@10为0.86-0.93（后期过滤基线仅为0.09-0.19）；成员推断准确率50.0%（PQ为57.3%）。

Conclusion: TurboVec通过码本无关量化设计在隐私保护、检索精度和查询效率之间取得了更优的权衡，尤其适合多租户RAG部署场景。局限性包括仅在单一数据集评估、与未压缩HNSW比较以及隐私评估仅基于合成数据。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems increasingly power enterprise LLM applications, yet the vector retrieval layer introduces two underexplored challenges: (1) trained codebook quantizers may expose corpus statistics during index construction, creating a leakage channel in multi-tenant deployments, and (2) post-hoc filtering for tenant isolation degrades recall on selective queries. We study TurboVec, an open-source vector index built on TurboQuant - a codebook-oblivious scalar quantizer requiring no corpus-dependent training. On the DBpedia OpenAI embeddings benchmark (d=1536, 100K-999K vectors), TurboQuant 4-bit outperforms trained FAISS Product Quantization at the same memory budget by 8.5-8.9 percentage points in Recall@5 across all scales. Compared to HNSW (R@5=0.991) and IVF-PQ (R@5=0.840), TurboQuant occupies a distinct design point: higher recall than IVF-PQ without training, at 4-8x less memory than HNSW. Deployed on Snowpark Container Services, TurboVec achieves 11ms median query latency at 100K vectors versus 707ms for warehouse brute-force scan. Kernel-level allowlist filtering maintains 0.86-0.93 Recall@10 across 10-1000 tenant workloads versus 0.09-0.19 for post-filter baselines. Codebook-oblivious design reduces membership inference accuracy to near-random (50.0%) versus 57.3% for PQ codebooks. Limitations include single dataset evaluation, uncompressed HNSW comparison, and privacy evaluation on synthetic data only.

</details>


### [105] [Periodic Bootstrap Thompson Sampling For Periodically Non-Stationary Bandit Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16986)
*Boning Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出周期性自助汤普森采样（PBTS），通过信念重置和自助探索阶段解决传统汤普森采样在周期性非平稳环境中的偏差问题，实验显示其显著降低了累积遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Thompson Sampling在处理周期性非平稳奖励分布时，由于累积所有历史观测数据导致后验分布产生偏差，需要一种能周期性清除过时数据并保持不确定性估计的方法。

Method: PBTS通过将信念重置与已知或推断的周期区间同步，并嵌入结构化自助探索阶段，在清除过时数据的同时维持不确定性估计。

Result: 在人工构建的偏态/平衡奖励分布、不同自助比例及周期错位等场景中，PBTS相比传统TS在周期性非平稳环境下实现了统计上显著的累积遗憾降低。

Conclusion: PBTS通过记忆重置和自助阶段为周期性奖励场景下的bandit算法优化提供了新方法，但存在极端周期错位等局限，未来可探索自适应周期识别等方向。

Abstract: This paper introduces Periodic Bootstrap Thompson Sampling (PBTS), an innovative extension of the classic Thompson Sampling (TS) algorithm tailored for bandit problems with periodic non-stationarity. Conventional TS accumulates all past observations, leading to biased posteriors when reward distributions cycle over time. PBTS overcomes this by synchronizing belief resets with known or inferred period intervals and embedding structured bootstrap exploration phases, effectively purging obsolete data while preserving uncertainty estimates. PBTS is tested in artificially constructed environments, which include skewed and balanced reward distributions, along with different bootstrap proportions and misaligned periodic intervals. Results indicate that PBTS generally achieves statistically significant reductions in cumulative regret against traditional TS in periodic non-stationary environments. Subsequent discussion further articulates the potential of PBTS's real-world deployment. The study mentions limitations like extreme periodic misalignment and proposes future research such as self-adjusting cycle-recognition. With memory reset and bootstrap phase, PBTS introduces a novel approach to optimizing bandit algorithms in periodic reward contexts.

</details>


### [106] [Counterfactual Shapley Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16999)
*Mingxuan Li,Kaizhan-Lee,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实沙普利信用分配框架（φ-SHA），通过反事实沙普利值（φ值）从因果角度解决强化学习中的信用分配问题，在不影响最优策略的前提下，有效提升稀疏因果、高随机性和延迟奖励场景下的信用分配质量，并提出了φ-PPO算法与优先轨迹回放（PTR），实验表明其在样本效率上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信用分配方法（基于时间邻近或事后奖励重加权）无法正确区分智能体的策略（技能）与环境随机性（运气），需要一种基于因果理论的原则性框架来隔离真正的因果驱动因素。

Method: 基于反事实沙普利值（Counterfactual Shapley Value, φ-value）的因果信用分配框架，通过重新分配环境奖励来增强时间信用分配；提出了一致性估计量高效计算φ值，并结合优先轨迹回放（PTR）实现了新型策略梯度方法φ-PPO。

Result: φ值能够精确对齐任务奖励的真实因果原因，在稀疏因果、高随机性和延迟奖励等挑战性环境中，相比现有最优方法具有更优的样本效率，且能收敛到最优策略。

Conclusion: 反事实沙普利值提供了一种原则性的、因果驱动的信用分配方案，能够有效分离技能与运气，显著提升强化学习在复杂环境下的学习效率与可解释性。

Abstract: The Credit Assignment Problem (CAP) is fundamental to developing efficient and explainable Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents. Existing frameworks, whether relying on temporal contiguity or hindsight-conditioned reward reweighting, frequently fail to attribute properly between an agent's policy (skill) and environmental stochasticity (luck). A principled approach to CAP must isolate the true causal drivers of observed outcomes from spurious correlations and environmental randomness. We introduce Counterfactual Shapley Credit Assignment, a novel framework grounded in causal theory that attributes credit and blame via the Counterfactual Shapley Value ($φ$-value). By redistributing environmental rewards, $φ$-values enhance temporal credit assignment across three critical dimensions: sparse causality, high stochasticity, and delayed rewards, all while preserving the optimal policy. We derive a consistent estimator that computes $φ$-values efficiently, enabling a new class of policy gradient methods, $φ$-PPO, combined with Prioritized Trajectory Replay (PTR). Empirical results demonstrate that $φ$-values align precisely to the ground truth causes of task rewards with superior sample efficiency in challenging environments where prior state-of-the-art methods fail to converge.

</details>


### [107] [Scalable Causal Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17003)
*Eylam Tagor,Mingxuan Li,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.LG
