Method: 提出双轴分类法（起源层×可检测性），通过公共漏洞报告和一手压力测试收集5个验证案例，进行机制性根因分析，其中3个案例提供独立复现脚本。

Result: 发现最严重的操作故障是隐性故障——返回HTTP 200状态码、通过所有标准健康检查，但会破坏应用状态，需要语义级可观测性才能检测。首次发现两种隐性故障：并发竞态条件导致历史丢失，以及流式索引冲突破坏工具调用载荷。

Conclusion: LLM网关的隐性故障比显性故障更具操作危害性，现有健康检查机制无法有效检测，需要发展语义级可观测性技术来应对这类故障。

Abstract: Multi-provider LLM gateways reverse proxies that route, load-balance, and rate-limit requests across foundation-model APIs have become critical production infrastructure. Yet the failure modes specific to this architectural layer remain undocumented, scattered across issue trackers and post-mortems with no unifying framework. We introduce \fa{}, a two-axis taxonomy that classifies failures by their \emph{origin layer} (Network/Transport, Streaming/Protocol, State/Session, Model~Behavior, Governance/Cost) and their \emph{detectability} (Loud vs.\ Silent). We populate this taxonomy with five verified catalog entries sourced from public bug reports and first-hand stress testing, each accompanied by a mechanistic root-cause analysis. Three entries include standalone reproduction scripts. Our principal finding is that the most operationally severe failures are \emph{silent}: they return HTTP~200, pass every standard health check, and corrupt application state in ways that require semantic-level observability to detect. Two such silent failures a concurrency race condition causing history loss and a streaming index collision corrupting tool-call payloads were discovered first-hand during \cb{} evaluation campaigns.

</details>


### [143] [Volatility-Aware Extreme Event Detection in High-Frequency Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17555)
*Maorufa Zaman,Haris Md Sahed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于波动率感知的高频比特币限价订单簿极端事件检测方法，通过将目标定义扩展为同时包含大额未来收益和高波动率区间，结合XGBoost模型，将PR-AUC从0.06提升至0.40（六倍以上提升），证明目标设计比模型复杂度更为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频金融市场的极端价格变动预测面临非平稳性、厚尾分布和严重类别不平衡等挑战，传统方法将极端事件视为孤立观测，难以有效检测罕见但具有重大影响的事件。

Method: 基于波动率聚类经验证据，将目标定义从单一的大额未来收益扩展为同时包含大额未来收益和高波动率区间；使用XGBoost树模型，结合时间序列交叉验证和类别不平衡感知评估。

Result: 所提方法PR-AUC约0.40，相比基线方法的0.06提升超过六倍，显著改善了罕见事件的检测性能。

Conclusion: 目标设计在金融机器学习中起关键作用，其影响往往超过模型复杂度的提升；将波动率结构融入标注过程可为高频加密货币市场的极端事件检测提供更有效和更现实的框架。

Abstract: Predicting extreme price movements in high-frequency financial markets is a challenging task due to non-stationarity, heavy-tailed return distributions, and severe class imbalance. In particular, rare but impactful events are often difficult to detect using conventional modeling approaches, which typically treat extreme movements as isolated observations. This study proposes a volatility-aware approach for extreme event detection using high-frequency Bitcoin limit order book (LOB) data. Motivated by empirical evidence of volatility clustering, the target formulation is extended to incorporate both large future returns and high-volatility regimes. This redefinition increases the proportion of informative samples and aligns the learning objective with the underlying market dynamics. Using a tree-based model (XGBoost) with time-series cross-validation and imbalance-aware evaluation, the proposed method achieves a Precision-Recall AUC of approximately 0.40, significantly outperforming the baseline formulation with a PR-AUC of around 0.06. This represents more than a sixfold improvement in detecting rare events. The results highlight that target design plays a critical role in financial machine learning, often exceeding the impact of model complexity. By incorporating volatility structure into the labeling process, the proposed approach provides a more effective and realistic framework for extreme event detection in high-frequency cryptocurrency markets.

</details>


### [144] [CoCurve: Cross-Module Co-Pruning Curvature for Training-Free Structured LLM Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17568)
*Zhiren Gong,Zihao Zeng,Zijie Wang,Tiantong Wang,Chau Yuen,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoCurve是一种无需微调的联合剪枝方法，通过二阶泰勒展开和交叉模块共剪枝曲率矩阵，同时捕捉注意力头与FFN通道组之间的交互效应，将剪枝问题转化为一次性二次规划求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练结构化剪枝方法独立评估各计算单元的重要性，忽略了Transformer中残差流耦合导致的单元间依赖关系（两个单独弱单元可能联合不可或缺），导致误剪。

Method: 基于冻结模型与掩码副本间token级KL散度的二阶泰勒展开，构建Fisher矩阵：对角元为经典节点显著性，非对角元为共剪枝曲率边（联合移除两个单元的额外损失）。在单次消融可加性近似下，矩阵退化为单次消融特征的Gram积，M次前向传播即可恢复完整M×M交互矩阵。剪枝转化为带预算约束的二次规划，一次性求解。

Result: 无需标注数据、微调或恢复训练，仅需校准前向传播即可捕捉跨模块交互效应，实现注意力与FFN单元的联合剪枝。

Conclusion: 通过建模跨模块剪枝交互，CoCurve在保持无训练高效性的同时克服了独立评分方法的缺陷，为LLM结构化剪枝提供了更精确的联合重要性评估框架。

Abstract: Structured pruning compresses large language models (LLMs) by removing whole computational units, such as attention heads and feed-forward (FFN) channel groups. Most training-free methods, however, rank these units independently, implicitly treating the loss from pruning a set as the sum of its individual losses. This view fails for Transformers, whose sublayers are coupled through a shared residual stream. Two individually weak units can thus be jointly indispensable, yet independent scoring is blind to such dependence and removes them together. We introduce CoCurve (Cross-Module Co-Pruning Curvature), a calibration-only, fine-tuning-free method that prunes attention and FFN units jointly. A second-order Taylor expansion of the token-level KL between the frozen model and its masked copy yields a single Fisher matrix whose diagonal is classical node saliency and whose off-diagonal entries are co-pruning curvature edges: the extra damage of removing two units together. Under a single-ablation additivity approximation this matrix reduces to a Gram product of single-unit ablation features, so the full M x M interaction is recovered from M forward passes, with no pairwise sweeps or gradients. Pruning then reduces to one budgeted quadratic program, solved in a single shot under a shared attention--FFN budget, with no labels, fine-tuning, or recovery.

</details>


### [145] [A Weisfeiler-Leman Characterization of Global-Attention Graph Transformers for Mixed-Integer Linear Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17570)
*Md Abrar Jahin,Craig A. Knoblock,Jay Pujara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，具有全局注意力的图基础模型（包括层次图变换器、全局线性注意力、边加权交叉注意力和二分消息传递等架构）的表达能力受限于一维Weisfeiler-Leman（1-WL）测试：1-WL等价的MILP图在任何参数设置下都会得到相同的图嵌入。实验表明，十种不同的图编码器均将1-WL等价的非同构图映射到数值相同的嵌入。超越1-WL的表达能力来源于输入编码（如随机游走位置编码）而非注意力机制本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图基础模型（GFMs）在表示混合整数线性规划（MILP）时的表达能力，特别是全局注意力机制能否捕捉超越标准图神经网络局部性的结构信息。通过图同构测试来刻画GFMs能区分哪些MILP实例。

Method: 通过图同构测试的理论框架，证明层次图变换器（包含全局线性注意力、边加权交叉注意力、二分消息传递）的表达能力受限于1-WL测试。采用组合式证明，论证每个架构组件都是对称多重集函数，从而保持1-WL等价性。在十种图编码器上（包括Graphormer、GraphGPS、Set-Transformer、Gasse等）进行实验验证。

Result: 理论上证明了全局注意力GFMs受限于1-WL测试；实验上验证了十种编码器均将1-WL等价的非同构图映射到数值相同的嵌入；发现超越1-WL的表达能力来自输入编码（如随机游走位置编码）而非注意力机制。

Conclusion: 全局注意力图基础模型的表达能力受限于1-WL测试，1-WL等价类内变化的图不变量无法从这些表示中恢复。这一结果为检测1-WL诱导的表示等价提供了编码器无关的诊断方法。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) with global attention are increasingly used to represent mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs), aiming to capture structure beyond the locality of standard graph neural networks. We study their expressive power through graph isomorphism testing, asking which MILP instances they map to identical representations. We prove that a broad class of hierarchical graph transformers combining global linear attention, edge-weighted cross-attention, and bipartite message passing is bounded by the one-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman (1-WL) test: under any parameter setting, 1-WL-equivalent MILP graphs receive identical graph embeddings. Our compositional proof shows that each architectural component is a symmetric multiset function and thus preserves 1-WL equivalence. We validate this characterization across ten diverse graph encoders, including Graphormer-, GraphGPS-, Set-Transformer-, and Gasse-style models. Across model capacities, graph scales, and pooling operators, every tested encoder maps 1-WL-equivalent non-isomorphic graph pairs to numerically identical embeddings. Consequently, graph invariants that vary within a 1-WL equivalence class cannot be recovered from these representations. We further show that expressiveness beyond 1-WL arises from input encoding rather than attention: random-walk positional encodings separate the constructed pairs, while additional constructions expose the limits of this remedy. These results characterize the expressive power of global-attention GFMs and provide an encoder-agnostic diagnostic for detecting 1-WL-induced representation equivalence.

</details>


### [146] [AGG: Jacobian-Aggregated Group Gradient for Efficient GRPO Training of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17572)
*Ruiyi Ding,Jie Li,He Kang,Ziyan Liu,Chengru Song,Yuan chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAGG通过利用DiT隐藏状态沿轨迹的线性变化特性，将反向传播成本从W步减少到2步，实现了约2倍的反向加速且质量损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO扩展到扩散模型和流匹配模型时，需要在采样轨迹的每个时间步通过高容量DiT主干反向传播梯度，导致高分辨率文本到图像训练成本过高。

Method: JAGG利用DiT隐藏状态和速度预测沿轨迹平滑且近乎线性变化的特性，通过t加权插值近似中间步的Jacobian，将每W个连续步的反向传播从全Transformer反向传播减少到仅2次。

Result: 在T2I基准测试中，JAGG实现了约2倍的反向加速，且质量下降可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 证明了当速度在(z,t)中为线性时插值方法是精确的，并通过余弦相似度路由规则在假设成立时部署JAGG，有效降低了DiT RL训练的计算成本。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is a powerful reinforcement learning algorithm for aligning generative models with human preferences. While successful in large language models~\cite{shao2024deepseekmathpushinglimitsmathematical}, its extension to diffusion and flow matching models introduces a severe computational bottleneck: gradients must be back-propagated through the high-capacity DiT backbone at \emph{every} timestep of the sampling trajectory, making high-resolution text-to-image (T2I) training prohibitively expensive. Training-free DiT inference acceleration methods (e.g., $Δ$-DiT, ScalingCache) exploit the fact that DiT hidden states and velocity predictions vary \emph{smoothly and nearly linearly} along the trajectory. We ask whether the same linearity can reduce the backward-pass cost of DiT RL training, and answer affirmatively with \textbf{JAGG} (\textbf{J}acobian-\textbf{A}ggregated \textbf{G}roup \textbf{G}radient), which reduces full transformer backward passes from $W$ to $2$ per group of $W$ consecutive steps. JAGG approximates intermediate-step Jacobians via $t$-weighted interpolation of the endpoint Jacobians, then aggregates per-step upstream signals into two composite gradients applied through a single joint backward pass. We prove this interpolation is \emph{exact} when the velocity is linear in $(z,t)$, and a cosine-similarity routing rule (\texttt{jagg\_frac}) deploys JAGG only where the assumption holds. Experiments on T2I benchmarks show JAGG delivers $\sim$2$\times$ backward speedup with negligible quality degradation.

</details>


### [147] [ANNLib: A Development Framework for Efficient Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17582)
*Zheqi Shen,Jingbo Su,Zijin Wan,Yan Gu,Yihan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ANNLib是一个基于图算法的近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）编程框架，通过解耦并独立优化算法与数据结构组件，实现了高性能与灵活功能的统一，支持过滤搜索、全动态更新和历史快照查询等多种复杂应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ANNS系统难以同时兼顾高性能与丰富功能，且实现二者的编程工作量大，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: 提出ANNLib库，采用编程框架方式，将ANNS系统的算法与数据结构组件解耦并独立优化，集成最先进的算法与数据结构作为模块，并引入新设计。

Result: 提供了简单统一的接口，在过滤搜索、全动态更新、历史快照查询等多种应用场景中达到了与先前工作相当或更优的性能。

Conclusion: ANNLib有效填补了高性能与灵活功能之间的空白，以最小编程代价实现了复杂ANNS应用的高效部署。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS) plays a pivotal role in modern deep learning pipelines. Recently, many ANNS systems have been proposed to either provide broad functionality or reach high performance. However, it is yet difficult to achieve both with minimal programming efforts. We propose ANNLib to address the gap. ANNLib is a library that provides a programming framework for achieving high performance and flexible functionality in ANNS systems, based on popular graph-based ANNS algorithms. We carefully decouple and independently optimize both the algorithm and the data structure components of an ANNS system. In addition, we integrate state-of-the-art algorithms and data structures into ANNLib as modules, along with our new designs. Users can choose combinations of components to implement sophisticated settings with high performance, such as filter search, fully dynamic updates, and historical queries on snapshots. Our experiments show that our new solution provides a simple interface for various applications and achieves comparable or even better performance than previous work, specifically for each application.

</details>


### [148] [Trustworthy Protein-Ligand Binding Affinity Prediction via Reliability-Aware Multi-Engine Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17601)
*Yongchan Hong,Defu Cao,Wenjin Liu,Thomas Ku,Jordy Homing Lam,Emily Nguyen,Willie Neiswanger,Vsevolod Katritch,Yan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RELIABLE-BA 是一个基于证据融合的多引擎蛋白质-配体结合亲和力预测框架，通过 Normal-Inverse-Gamma 分布对每个对接引擎进行证据建模，利用分子上下文学习可靠性来缩放认知不确定性，并通过闭式聚合融合多个专家预测。在 PDBBind、BDB2020+、SARS-CoV-2 Mpro 和 5HT2A 受体数据集上，该方法在保持竞争性点预测精度的同时显著改善了不确定性校准，且利用不确定性估计进行高置信度筛选可将预测误差降低最多 25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对接引擎在结合亲和力预测上常产生分歧且无法提供可信度指标；传统共识打分和集成方法虽然提升了平均精度，但将所有预测等同对待，缺乏可解释的置信度度量和不确定性分解，忽略了每个蛋白质-配体对的化学上下文信息。

Method: (1) 通过 Normal-Inverse-Gamma 分布将每个对接引擎建模为证据专家；(2) 在保持每个专家预测均值不变的前提下，通过分子上下文学习可靠性参数来缩放认知不确定性；(3) 通过闭式聚合融合多个专家，同时捕获个体不确定性和引擎间分歧。

Result: 在 PDBBind 和 BDB2020+ 基准上取得具有竞争力的点预测精度，同时不确定性校准显著改善；在 SARS-CoV-2 Mpro 和 5HT2A 受体数据集上验证了临床相关药物靶标的适用性；利用不确定性估计筛选高置信度蛋白质-配体对可将预测误差降低最多 25%。

Conclusion: RELIABLE-BA 是首个将证据融合与上下文相关可靠性相结合的多引擎结合亲和力预测框架，为可信赖的 AI 驱动药物发现提供了原理性路径。

Abstract: Accurate protein-ligand binding affinity prediction is central to computational drug discovery, yet modern docking engines frequently disagree without indicating which prediction to trust. Consensus scoring and ensemble methods improve mean accuracy but treat all predictions identically without interpretable confidence measures or uncertainty decomposition, ignoring the chemical context of each protein-ligand pair. To address this limitation, we introduce RELIABLE-BA (RELIABiLity-aware Evidential fusion for Binding Affinity), an evidential framework for multi-engine binding affinity prediction. Our model comprises three steps: (1) modeling each engine as an evidential expert via Normal-Inverse-Gamma distributions, (2) scaling epistemic uncertainty through learned reliability from molecular context while preserving each expert's predictive mean, and (3) fusing experts through closed-form aggregation that captures both individual uncertainty and inter-engine disagreement. Experiments on the PDBBind and BDB2020+ benchmarks demonstrate competitive point prediction with substantially improved uncertainty calibration, and additional validation on the SARS-CoV-2 Mpro dataset and 5HT2A receptor demonstrates applicability to clinically relevant drug targets. Crucially, these uncertainty estimates enable reliable filtering of protein-ligand pairs, reducing prediction error by up to 25% when retaining only high-confidence pairs. To our knowledge, RELIABLE-BA is the first multi-engine binding affinity prediction framework to combine evidential fusion with context-dependent reliability, offering a principled path toward trustworthy AI-guided drug discovery. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yongchand/RELIABLE-BA.

</details>


### [149] [Can Transformers Really Do It All? On the Compatibility of Inductive Biases Across Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17624)
*Damien Teney,Liangze Jiang,Hemanth Saratchandran,Simon Lucey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种优化Transformer架构的方法，通过用学习到的函数替代关键非线性层（GeLU、softmax），发现标准Transformer很少是架构空间中的局部最优解，任务特定架构能带来显著但牺牲通用性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer的设计在不同任务间高度一致，但这是否意味着它对每个具体任务都是最优的？答案可能关乎如何超越单纯扩大模型规模来推动AI发展。

Method: 将Transformer中最关键的非线性层（GeLU和softmax）替换为在保留数据上学到的函数，从而优化给定数据集的架构；然后在其他数据集上训练得到的架构，评估任务间的兼容性。

Result: 在算法玩具任务上，新架构在学习速度、分布内/外泛化和跨种子稳定性方面有显著提升，但高度任务特定；在代码和语言建模数据集上，提升较小但更稳定，且架构在不同数据集和领域间迁移性更好。

Conclusion: 标准Transformer在架构空间中很少是局部最优解，简单的替代方案可以表现更好但牺牲了通用性；暗示可能存在更好地同时支持多种能力（如流畅性和稳健推理）的改进架构。

Abstract: Transformers are remarkably versatile and their design is largely consistent across a variety of applications. But are they optimal for any given task or dataset? The answer may be key for pushing AI beyond merely scaling current designs.
  *Method.* We present a method to optimize a transformer architecture for a given dataset, which we use as a tool to study optimal task-specific inductive biases. This method replaces the most important non-linearities (GeLUs,;softmax) with functions learned on held-out data. We then train the resulting architectures on other datasets, as a way to evaluate the compatibility between pairs of tasks.
  Findings. On algorithmic toy tasks, we identify new architectures with dramatic improvements in learning speed, in- and out-of-distribution generalization, and stability across seeds. The new designs prove very task-specific however, and indicate that these tasks require inductive biases very different from those of standard transformers. On code and language modeling datasets, we also find architectures with consistent, yet smaller improvements. These designs transfer much better across datasets and domains (English & computer code).
  Implications. Our results show that standard transformers are rarely a local optimum in the space of architectures. Simple alternatives can perform much better but sacrifice universality. This suggests that there may be room for improved architectures that better support multiple capabilities simultaneously, such as fluency and robust reasoning.

</details>


### [150] [TypiCore: A Hybrid Active Query Strategy for Class-Incremental Learning on Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17632)
*Gabor Szucs,Samuel Jacsev,Marcell Nemeth,Davide Dalle Pezze,Gian Antonio Susto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多元时间序列的主动类增量学习（ACIL），提出了TypiCore混合查询策略，该策略在主动学习周期中交替使用典型性驱动和多样性驱动的样本选择，在有限标注预算下构建兼具代表性和多样性的记忆缓冲，在TSCIL基准测试中显著超越现有方法，并在多个数据集上以极低标注成本达到或超越全监督持续学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的时间序列数据流标注成本高昂，现有持续学习方法依赖全标注数据，无法适应标注预算受限的实际场景。

Method: 系统评估了多种查询策略与回放方法的组合在ACIL场景下的表现；提出TypiCore混合查询策略，在主动学习周期中交替选择典型性样本和多样性样本，构建高质量记忆缓冲区。

Result: TypiCore在TSCIL基准上显著优于所有基线方法，在多个数据集上仅使用部分标签即可达到或超越全监督持续学习性能。

Conclusion: 不确定性驱动和分布感知方法在有限标注预算下表现受限，而TypiCore通过交替采样策略有效平衡了模型的可塑性、稳定性和标签效率。

Abstract: Time series data play a pivotal role across numerous domains, including healthcare and manufacturing. In real-world environments, models must cope with distribution shifts over time, a challenge commonly addressed through Continual Learning (CL) techniques. However, existing CL methods face a critical limitation: real-world data streams are rarely fully labeled, making annotation cost a major practical constraint. This paper investigates Active Class-Incremental Learning (ACIL) for multivariate time series, where a model must sequentially learn new classes while selectively querying labels under a fixed annotation budget. We present a systematic evaluation of a wide range of query strategies combined with multiple rehearsal-based approaches, assessing their impact on plasticity, stability, and label efficiency across four benchmark datasets. Our analysis reveals the limitations of uncertainty-based and distribution-aware methods in achieving strong performance under constrained labeling budgets. To address these shortcomings, we propose TypiCore, a novel hybrid query strategy that alternates between typicality-based and diversity-based sample selection across active learning cycles, enabling the construction of memory buffers that are both representative and diverse. Evaluated on the TSCIL benchmark, TypiCore delivers statistically significant improvements over all baselines and matches or surpasses fully supervised continual learning performance on multiple datasets while requiring a fraction of the available labels.

</details>


### [151] [Selectivity Matters: Source Node Influence Pruning for Unsupervised Graph Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17668)
*Ridong Han,Yawen Shen,Zhongnian Li,Tongfeng Sun,Xinzheng Xu,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SNIP框架，通过量化源节点结构差异并剪枝不兼容节点，实现数据级精炼，从而在无监督图域适应中超越全图训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UGDA方法默认所有源节点都对特征对齐有正向贡献，但由于结构偏移，存在结构偏差的源节点（如结构离群点）在目标图中缺乏语义对应，强行对齐会引入噪声并导致负迁移。

Method: 提出SNIP（Source Node Influence Pruning），一种模型无关的数据中心精炼框架。通过集成多种中心性度量量化单个源节点与目标域的结构差异，为每个源节点分配影响分数；采用基于秩的归一化机制消除不同度量间的尺度变化，筛选并过滤掉低影响分数的结构不兼容节点，构建更有利于后续对齐的精炼"子源图"。

Result: 在5个真实世界数据集的8个迁移场景上，SNIP始终优于竞争基线，显著提升适应性能，验证了选择性节点利用优于全图训练。

Conclusion: 无监督图域适应的研究范式应从特征级对齐转向数据级精炼，选择性利用源节点比全图训练更有效。

Abstract: Unsupervised Graph Domain Adaptation (UGDA) aims to facilitate knowledge transfer from a labeled source graph to an unlabeled target graph by mitigating cross-domain distribution shifts. Existing methods primarily focus on node-level feature alignment in latent spaces, relying on the implicit assumption that all source nodes contribute positively to the alignment. However, this assumption often fails because a node's semantic information is intrinsically coupled with its topological graph structure. Due to structural shifts, source nodes with severe structural deviations (e.g., structural outliers) lack semantic counterparts in the target graph, and forcing alignment on them introduces severe noise and causes negative transfer. To bridge this gap, we argue that selective source node utilization is superior to full-graph training, thereby shifting the research paradigm from feature-level alignment to data-level refinement. To this end, we propose Source Node Influence Pruning (SNIP), a novel model-agnostic, data-centric refinement framework. Specifically, SNIP quantifies the structural discrepancy between individual source nodes and the target domain by integrating multiple centrality measures, assigning each source node an influence score. A rank-based normalization mechanism is further employed to eliminate scale variations across different measures, allowing SNIP to effectively identify and filter out structurally incompatible nodes with low influence scores. As a plug-and-play method, SNIP constructs a refined "sub-source" graph that is inherently more beneficial for subsequent alignment. Comprehensive experiments across eight transfer scenarios on five real-world datasets demonstrate that SNIP consistently outperforms competitive baselines and significantly enhances adaptation performance, validating the superiority of selective node utilization over full-graph training.

</details>


### [152] [GeneSpeak-FP: Target and Compound Retrieval from Observed Cell-Level Perturbation Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17671)
*Kseniia Vaniushkina,Jeongmin Lim,Jinyong Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Transformer的检索模型，用于从单细胞扰动图谱中逆向检索与观察到的转录响应最一致的目标和化合物。在Tahoe-100M数据集上，模型在目标检索Recall@10达0.408，化合物检索Hit@1达0.129，证明单一多任务模型能从细胞级响应中恢复目标注释和化合物身份，但对未见化合物和细胞背景的泛化性尚待验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模单细胞扰动图谱的构建使得逆向问题成为可能：给定观察到的转录响应，如何从固定库中找出与之最一致的注释目标和化合物。现有方法缺乏一个统一的、可端到端训练的检索模型来解决该问题。

Method: 提出Transformer检索模型，输入为单细胞级扰动特征（处理细胞与细胞系特异性DMSO参考对比得到），编码器将特征映射为目标检索向量和分子嵌入向量，通过有监督目标损失和结构-转录组对齐联合训练。采用Tahoe-100M数据集的379种化合物，以化合物内分层90/10条件对划分进行训练和验证。

Result: 目标检索Recall@10为0.408，Recall@20为0.544；化合物检索Hit@1为0.129，Hit@10为0.343，平均倒数排名0.205。消融实验显示模型显著优于随机向量基线和后验基因袋控制。诊断评估结果与主模型几乎一致。

Conclusion: 单一多任务Transformer模型能够从观察到的细胞级响应中恢复靶点注释和化合物身份，但泛化到未见化合物和细胞背景的能力仍待建立。

Abstract: Large-scale single-cell perturbation atlases make it possible to ask an inverse question: given an observed transcriptional response, which annotated targets and compounds in a fixed library are most consistent with that response? We present \model, a Transformer retrieval model for this closed-library setting. Each input is a cell-level perturbation signature formed by contrasting one treated cell with a cell-line-specific mean DMSO reference. The encoder maps the signature to a target-retrieval vector and a molecular-embedding vector, trained jointly with supervised target losses and structure--transcriptome alignment. We evaluate on Tahoe-100M conditions with mapped target annotations using a within-compound stratified 90/10 condition-pair split of 10,505 training and 1,168 validation drug--cell-line pairs. Because compounds and cell lines can occur in both partitions, the experiment measures held-out condition-pair retrieval rather than generalization to unseen compounds or cellular contexts. In a Monte Carlo evaluation over 38,400 sampled validation cells, \model\ achieved target Recall@10 of 0.408 and Recall@20 of 0.544, together with compound Hit@1 of 0.129, Hit@10 of 0.343, and mean reciprocal rank of 0.205 over a 379-compound bank. A separate diagnostic evaluation produced nearly identical values for the main model and large gains over a random-vector control and post-hoc bag-of-genes controls. These results demonstrate that a single multi-task model can recover both mapped target annotations and recorded compound identities from observed cell-level responses in the evaluated Tahoe-100M closed-library setting. Generalization to unseen compounds and cellular contexts remains to be established.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Objective Expressivity: Geometry Preservation in Multimodal Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17673)
*Tillmann Rheude,Roland Eils,Benjamin Wild*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态对比学习中编码器Jacobian条件数的重要性，提出通过正则化、LeakyReLU激活和残差路径等几何保持编码器（GPE）方法改善条件数，从而提升检索和线性探测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习正从双模态（图像-文本）向三模态及以上扩展，但高阶多模态对齐面临优化和表征挑战，现有研究缺乏对编码器几何性质（如Jacobian条件数）影响的系统理解。

Method: 识别编码器Jacobian条件数为关键因素，提出几何保持编码器（GPE），通过Jacobian正则化直接改善条件数，并结合LeakyReLU激活和残差路径等简单修改恢复几何优势。在合成基准和四个真实世界数据集（含缺失模态场景）上进行验证。

Result: 改善Jacobian条件数在多种对比学习目标下均提升了检索和线性探测性能；表达能力强的对比目标在线性探测上收益有限。Jacobian条件数差会导致奇异值谱坍缩或放大，破坏多模态对齐。

Conclusion: 多模态对比学习的性能不仅取决于目标函数的表达能力，还依赖于底层编码器的几何和优化性质，Jacobian条件数是其中的关键因素。

Abstract: Contrastive learning is increasingly moving toward settings with three or more modalities instead of image-text pairs. Yet, extending models from pairwise to higher-order multimodal alignment can introduce optimization and representation challenges. We identify encoder Jacobian conditioning as a key factor in trimodal contrastive learning: poorly conditioned encoders exhibit collapsing or amplified singular-value spectra, leading to exploding Jacobian condition numbers and degraded multimodal alignment. We introduce geometry-preserving encoders (GPEs) by directly conditioning the Jacobian through regularization and demonstrating that simple modifications like LeakyReLU activations and residual paths recover these geometric benefits. Across a synthetic benchmark and four real-world datasets including missing modalities, improving Jacobian conditioning boosts retrieval and linear probe performance across multiple contrastive objectives, whereas expressive objectives yield little benefit in linear probes. More broadly, our results show that multimodal contrastive learning depends not only on objective expressivity, but also on the geometric and optimization properties of the underlying encoders.

</details>


### [154] [Uncovering Latent Reasoning Strategies in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17674)
*Awni Altabaa,John Lafferty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将预训练语言模型的响应分布分解为策略条件化表示的方法，通过变分目标函数解决后验坍塌问题，使潜在策略编码与不同推理策略对齐，同时保持原始响应分布不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在推理任务中会隐式地学习多种策略，但这些策略纠缠在模型的响应分布中，难以显式分离和解释。现有方法无法有效分解这些隐式策略，导致无法对模型的推理行为进行结构化的理解与控制。

Method: 提出潜在变量分解框架，将分布分解为路由器（router）和生成器（generator）；设计新的变分目标函数，基于相对于基础模型的信息增益，对高困惑度token施加更强的重建压力，从而鼓励潜在变量编码策略相关的响应变化，避免后验坍塌。

Result: 在多个多策略算法任务基准上，该方法能够恢复与不同参考策略对齐的潜在编码，同时保持基础模型的响应分布不变。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解耦了语言模型中的隐式推理策略，为理解和控制模型的多种推理路径提供了结构化表示方法。

Abstract: A language model $p_θ(y \mid x)$ trained on reasoning tasks learns to solve problems via multiple distinct strategies, yet these strategies are implicit and entangled within the model's response distribution. We study the problem of decomposing the response distribution of a given pretrained language model into a structured, strategy-conditioned representation. Specifically, we learn a latent-variable factorization $p_θ(y \mid x) \leadsto (r_φ(z \mid x), g_φ(y \mid x,z))$, where a router $r$ maps each input to a distribution over latent strategies $z$ and a generator $g$ produces the response conditioned on that strategy. A key challenge is that the generator, initialized from the base model, already represents $p_θ(y \mid x)$ without using $z$. Standard variational inference therefore gives the model no incentive to route information through $z$ and can yield a severe form of posterior collapse. To address this, we propose a variational objective that measures fractional information gain relative to the base model's response loss and concentrates reconstruction pressure on tokens with high base model surprisal, encouraging $z$ to encode strategy-relevant response variation. We introduce a benchmark of multi-strategy algorithmic tasks and show that this objective recovers latent codes aligned with distinct reference strategies while preserving the base model's response distribution.

</details>


### [155] [Planning with Transformers: Chain of Computation and Structured Context Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17710)
*Ehsan Futuhi,Nathan R. Sturtevant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对LLMs在规划任务上的理论与实际表现差距，本文提出Chain of Computation (COC)架构——将基于Transformer的语言模型置于迭代循环中，结合结构化上下文窗口(SCW)，使LM能学习规划策略、预测世界模型并执行算术运算。实验表明，在仅追加SCW配置下，小规模LM即可在BlocksWorld和Pancake puzzle上达到99.89%以上成功率，并可解决多达20个圆盘的汉诺塔问题（需超百万动作），通过符号算术支持或确定型下推自动机(DPA)公式化可大幅降低训练数据需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在理论上具有图灵完备的计算能力（Transformer已被证明是图灵完备的），但在实际规划任务中表现不佳，存在理论能力与实证性能之间的显著差距。本文旨在弥合这一差距，探索如何让LLM在规划任务中发挥其理论计算能力。

Method: 提出Chain of Computation (COC)架构：(1) 将Transformer-based LM置于迭代循环中，利用其作为模式匹配系统的优势；(2) 设计结构化上下文窗口(SCW)，提供恒定大小的上下文窗口并支持每步规划中选择使用哪个窗口；(3) 在此架构中，LM学习规划策略、预测世界模型并执行规划所需的算术运算；(4) 对汉诺塔问题提出两种改进方案：符号算术支持或确定型下推自动机(DPA)公式化SCW。

Result: (1) 在BlocksWorld和Pancake puzzle上，使用仅追加SCW的小规模从头训练LM即可达到99.89%以上成功率；(2) 可解决多达20个圆盘的汉诺塔问题，需超过100万个动作；(3) 通过符号算术支持或DPA公式化，大幅降低了训练数据需求；(4) 失败案例分析表明汉诺塔问题失败源于算术运算错误或遇到未见过的token。

Conclusion: 通过COC架构设计，理论上图灵完备的Transformer可以在实践中有效解决复杂规划问题，弥合了理论与实证之间的差距。选择合适的SCW结构（如符号算术支持或DPA公式化）能进一步提升LLM在长序列规划任务上的表现和样本效率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have had a remarkable impact across many areas of machine learning. However, recent studies have shown that they struggle to reliably solve planning problems. At the same time, theoretical results have shown that transformers, the core architecture underlying modern LLMs, are Turing-complete. In this work, we investigate this apparent gap between the theoretical computational power of LLMs and their empirical planning performance. We propose Chain of Computation (COC), a computational architecture that places a transformer-based LM inside an iterative loop, leveraging its strength as a pattern-matching system. The COC uses a Structured Context Window (SCW) which provides a constant-sized context window with support for choosing which window is used at each planning step. Within this architecture, the LM is able to learn a planning policy, predicts the world model, and performs the arithmetic operations required during planning. We show that, when given an append-only SCW (resembling a Turing Machine tape), even relatively small LMs trained from scratch can learn planning policies and generalize from a small number of training instances within each planning domain, achieving success rates above 99.89\% on BlocksWorld and the Pancake puzzle. Our analysis of failure cases in Tower of Hanoi (TOH) reveals that they arise from arithmetic operations or from encountering previously unseen tokens. We show that COC can solve TOH problem instances with up to 20 disks, requiring over 1 million actions, while requiring substantially less training data by either (1) planning with symbolical support for arithmetic or by (2) using a deterministic pushdown automaton (PDA) formulation for the SCW.

</details>


### [156] [MXSens: Sensitivity-Aware Mixed-Precision Quantization for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17733)
*Simla Burcu Harma,Danila Mishin,Zhengyuan Su,Ayan Chakraborty,Elizaveta Kostenok,Dongho Ha,Babak Falsafi,Martin Jaggi,Yunho Oh,Amir Yazdanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MXSens，一种基于敏感度引导的混合尾数位宽分配方法，利用MXINT的块结构实现对LLM的4比特量化，在保持硬件效率的同时显著提升了量化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4比特量化虽能高效推理，但异常值导致精度严重下降；数据旋转或混合精度整数量化方法依赖软件管理的缩放和频繁反量化，开销大；MXINT等微缩放格式虽将缩放编码到硬件中，但与基于旋转的方法不兼容，缺乏对异常值差异性和量化敏感度的细粒度考虑。

Method: MXSens首先分析发现异常值的严重程度（从罕见极端到频繁轻微偏差）及量化敏感度在层和列间分布不均；据此提出一种无需训练的方法，基于列级和层级的敏感度分析，为不同列和层分配混合尾数位宽（4/6/8），并天然兼容MXINT的块状结构。

Result: 在W4A4KV4设置下，MXSens在LLaMA-2-70B和LLaMA-3-8B上分别达到3.77和7.63的困惑度，在WikiText-2上显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文建立了LLM量化中精度与资源效率的新平衡点，证明敏感度引导的混合精度策略能够有效克服微缩放格式与异常值处理之间的不兼容性问题。

Abstract: 4-bit quantization enables efficient LLM inference, but suffers from significant accuracy degradation due to outliers. Prior work addresses this problem via data rotation or mixed-precision integer quantization, but often relies on software-managed scaling and frequent dequantization, incurring substantial overhead. Microscaling formats, such as MXINT, eliminate these inefficiencies by encoding scales in hardware, yet remain incompatible with rotation-based methods. Our analysis reveals that outliers vary in severity, from rare extremes to frequent mild deviations, and that quantization sensitivity is unevenly distributed across layers and columns. These insights motivate a fine-grained, sensitivity-guided approach. We introduce MXSens, a training-free method that assigns mixed mantissa bitwidths (4/6/8) based on column- and layer-wise sensitivity, naturally leveraging the block-wise structure of MXINT. MXSens outperforms state-of-the-art quantization methods across a range of models and tasks. Under the W4A4KV4 setting, MXSens achieves perplexities of 3.77 and 7.63 on LLaMA-2-70B and LLaMA-3-8B, respectively, substantially improving over existing baselines on WikiText-2. Our work establishes a new balance between accuracy and resource efficiency for LLM quantization.

</details>


### [157] [Towards Reliable Zero-Shot Crowd Forecasting: Evaluating Time Series Foundation Models for Special Event Pedestrian Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17758)
*Ziteng Li,Yanan Xin,Tina Comes,Serge Hoogendoorn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了预训练时间序列基础模型在特殊活动人群密度预测中的零样本概率预测能力，重点评估其在数据稀缺、分布异质条件下的不确定性量化效果，并以SAIL2025活动为案例给出实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模特殊活动的人群管理需要实时可靠的行人流预测，但监督学习方法受限于历史数据稀缺、数据分布异质以及事件内观测窗口短等问题，难以有效支持运营决策。概率不确定性量化对捕捉突发波动和尾部风险至关重要。

Method: 采用预训练时间序列基础模型作为轻量级零样本概率预测方法，无需大量局部重训练；使用面向短时事件的决策导向指标，以SAIL2025活动为案例对两种时间序列基础模型进行综合评估。

Result: 通过决策导向指标评估了两种时间序列基础模型在人群预测上的表现，明确了零样本预测在何种条件下在运营上仍然可靠，并提炼出面向人群管理者的实践洞见。

Conclusion: 预训练时间序列基础模型可在数据稀缺的特殊活动场景下实现有效的零样本概率预测，为人群管理者提供何时依赖零样本预测的操作性指导。

Abstract: Managing massive crowds during infrequent special events requires reliable real-time pedestrian-flow forecasting to ensure public safety and operational efficiency. However, supervised forecasting methods face limitations in these contexts due to scarce historical data, heterogeneous data distributions, and short in-event observation windows. To effectively support operational decision-making, forecasts should provide not only accurate point estimates but also informative predictive uncertainty. Probabilistic uncertainty quantification plays a critical role in this aspect, particularly capturing sudden volatility and tail risks. This paper investigates pretrained time series foundation models as a lightweight approach for zero-shot probabilistic forecasting without extensive local retraining. Using decision-oriented metrics tailored to short events, we conduct a comprehensive assessment of two time series foundation models on crowd forecasting, with the SAIL2025 event as a use case. We then distill practical insights for crowd managers, specifying when zero-shot forecasts remain operationally reliable.

</details>


### [158] [Generalize and Guide: Decomposing Rewards for Few-Shot Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17760)
*Ziyi Liu,Grace Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多任务鉴别器邻近引导逆强化学习（MPG），通过可泛化的鉴别器和邻近函数，从少量目标任务演示和充足多任务数据中学习新任务，在导航和操作任务上平均成功率81.2%，比最强基线高24.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实任务常存在自然变异（如抓取不同形状杯子），难以收集覆盖所有场景的目标任务演示。虽然目标任务演示有限，但容易获取异构但相关的行为数据集，因此需要解决小样本多任务演示逆强化学习问题。

Method: 提出MPG框架，学习两个互补的奖励组件：（1）可泛化的鉴别器，跨相关任务传递共享结构以识别新任务中的专家行为；（2）邻近函数，测量状态偏离专家行为的程度，在探索时提供纠正性引导。

Result: 在多种具有显著变异的导航和操作任务上（如物体配置、台面布局和初始机器人位姿变化），平均成功率达81.2%，比最强的单任务基线平均高出24.7个百分点。

Conclusion: MPG方法有效解决了小样本多任务逆强化学习问题，通过分离共享结构学习与任务特定指导，在有限目标任务演示下实现了对新任务的泛化学习。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a powerful framework for learning from demonstrations. However, real-world tasks often exhibit substantial natural variations (e.g., picking up mugs with varying shapes), making it impractical to collect demonstrations that fully specify a new task under every possible scenario. In practice, while demonstrations for the target task are limited, it is often easier to obtain datasets of heterogeneous but related behaviors. This motivates the problem of few-shot IRL with multi-task demonstrations (FM-IRL), where an agent must learn a new task with substantial variations from only a limited number of target-task demonstrations, together with sufficient demonstrations of related tasks and online agent experience. To do so, we must both recover the expert distribution of the new task and provide guidance when the agent deviates from it. We introduce Multitask discriminator Proximity-Guided IRL (MPG), which learns two complementary reward components: (1) a generalizable discriminator that transfers shared structure across related tasks to identify expert behavior in a new task, and (2) a proximity function that measures how far a state deviates from expert behavior and provides corrective guidance during exploration. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on multiple challenging navigation and manipulation tasks under significant variations (e.g., object configurations, table layouts, and initial robot poses), achieving an average success rate of 81.2%, outperforming the strongest per-task baseline by an average of 24.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [159] [FIFA World Cup 2026 as a Contamination-Free Benchmark for LLM Forecasting Agents: Four Models, a Bookmaker, and 104 Matches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17765)
*Jiacheng Ding,Cong Guo,Jason Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了WC2026-Agents基准和数据集，用于评估LLM作为自主预测智能体对2026年世界杯104场比赛的预测能力。四个前沿模型（Claude Opus 4.8、ChatGPT GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Grok Expert Mode）执行相同的搜索-行动-反思循环，与博彩市场赔率进行对比。结果显示：四个模型92%的比赛给出相同的首选预测，但均未超越市场的Brier分数；然而在决策质量上差异显著，投资回报率从-18%到+10%不等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估缺乏对模型作为自主预测智能体的真实未来事件预测能力的系统评测，且存在数据污染问题。需要构建一个无污染的基准，同时评估预测准确性、决策质量和自我认知能力。

Method: 选取2026年世界杯104场比赛，四个前沿模型执行相同的搜索-行动-反思循环：使用网络工具收集证据、提交1X2（主胜/平/客胜）概率分布和100美元虚拟投注、赛后仅根据最终比分进行反思。所有比赛均在模型训练截止日期之后进行，确保无污染。同时收集博彩市场赔率作为经济基线。

Result: 四个模型92%的比赛首选预测相同，但均未超越市场的Brier分数。市场偏好上的简单平注收益超过所有模型。模型间决策质量差异显著：投资回报率-18%至+10%，引用市场的预测比例12%至100%，错误预测的自我报告率36%至86%。

Conclusion: WC2026-Agents基准评估了LLM在校准、决策质量和自我认知三个维度的能力——这些维度即使模型预测结果相似时也会表现出差异。当前前沿模型在预测准确性上均未超越市场，但在决策行为上存在显著差异。

Abstract: We introduce WC2026-Agents, a benchmark and dataset for evaluating large language models (LLMs) as autonomous forecasting agents on real, future events. For every one of the 104 matches of the 2026 FIFA World Cup, four frontier models -- Claude Opus 4.8, ChatGPT (GPT-5.5, high reasoning), Gemini 3.1 Pro, and Grok (Expert Mode) -- ran an identical search-act-reflect loop: gather evidence with a web tool, commit to a 1X2 (team-A win / draw / team-B win) distribution and a virtual 100-USD bet, and, after the match, reflect given only the final score. Because every match kicked off after the models' training cutoffs, the benchmark is contamination-free by construction. Crucially, we pair the four agents with a fifth competitor drawn from the same information environment -- the pre-match betting market -- collected as per-match 1X2 odds, giving an economically grounded baseline and letting us score not just what an agent predicts but what it does with money. The release contains 416 forecasts and 414 reflections with verbatim reasoning, ground truth (including penalty shootouts), odds, and a reproducible evaluation suite. A reference evaluation surfaces findings that raw accuracy hides: the four agents issue an identical top pick in 92% of matches and none beats the market's Brier score; indeed, a naive flat stake on the market favorite out-earns all four agents. Yet the agents diverge sharply as decision-makers: betting return-on-investment ranges from -18% to +10%, fading the market is unprofitable for all four, the share of forecasts that cite the market ranges from 12% to 100%, and self-reported error rates on wrong picks range from 36% to 86%. The benchmark thus measures calibration, decision quality, and self-knowledge -- axes on which frontier models differ even when their predictions do not. Data and code: https://github.com/graphuofm/FIFA2026LLM

</details>


### [160] [Feature Attribution-Based Explainability Analysis of Deep Learning Models in Predictive Process Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17783)
*Kseniya Sahatova,Rafael Seidi Oyamada,Xuefei Lu,Johannes De Smedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对深度神经网络在预测性流程监控中的局部事后可解释性方法，通过控制流感知的分段算法将轨迹划分为有意义的片段，并计算片段级SHAP解释，以识别影响预测的关键轨迹部分和变更点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在预测性流程监控中表现优异，但其黑箱特性限制了信任和实际应用。现有特征归因方法存在困境：事件级归因对长轨迹计算复杂度过高，而基于聚合轨迹表示的解释无法捕捉底层控制流动态。

Method: 提出一种控制流感知的分段算法，将轨迹划分为有意义的片段，并支持计算片段级SHAP解释，从而识别影响预测的轨迹部分和变更点。

Result: 在具有已知流程逻辑的合成数据集上验证了分段方法的有效性（可明确验证变更点），并在贷款申请流程和荷兰市政行政流程的真实事件日志上展示了其实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在保持解释性的同时降低计算复杂度，通过控制流感知分段平衡了事件级和轨迹级解释的局限性，为预测性流程监控提供了有效的可解释性方案。

Abstract: Predictive process monitoring supports the optimization and control of operational business processes by forecasting the future state or outcome of ongoing cases. While deep neural networks have achieved strong performance for these tasks by modeling sequential dependencies in event logs, their black-box nature limits trust and practical adoption. Feature attribution methods are often used to address this, but applying them directly poses a dilemma: event-level attributions impose high computational complexity for long traces, while explanations based on aggregated trace representations often fail to capture the underlying control-flow dynamics. To address this issue, we propose a local post-hoc explainability method for deep neural networks in outcome prediction. The method relies on a control-flow-aware segmentation algorithm that partitions a trace into meaningful segments and supports the computation of segment-level SHAP explanations. This makes it possible to identify which parts of a trace influence a prediction and which change points steer the case toward the predicted outcome. We assess the proposed segmentation method on a synthetic dataset with known process logic, where meaningful change points can be explicitly verified, and we demonstrate its usefulness on real-world event logs from a loan application process and an administrative process of a Dutch municipality.

</details>


### [161] [The Concept of Representation in ML: Beyond Plato and Aristotle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17800)
*Gilad Landau,Aviv Keren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从心灵哲学中关于心理表征的辩论视角，审视了“柏拉图式表征假说”——即不同AI模型的表征趋向收敛是因为共享了现实的统一结构。作者认为哲学资源有助于澄清此类主张的实质，并指出仅凭对齐证据不足以得出强烈的形而上结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来随着AI模型规模扩大、能力接近人类水平，关于模型表征收敛性的讨论从工程语境滑向了更具哲学意味的“心理表征”领域。作者认为需要厘清这些主张的哲学前提，特别是《柏拉图式表征假说》中“收敛源于现实统一结构”的核心论点。

Method: 引入心灵哲学中关于心理表征的经典辩论（如表征的意向性、内容确定性问题等），分析《柏拉图式表征假说》的论证逻辑，检验其从“表征对齐”到“现实结构统一”的推理链条。

Result: 哲学分析表明：（1）表征对齐证据本身不足以支持关于现实结构的强烈形而上学结论；（2）哲学资源可以澄清这类主张中的概念混淆和隐含假设；（3）为未来研究提供了更严谨的思考框架。

Conclusion: 跨AI模型的表征收敛现象需要更谨慎的哲学审视，不能简单归因于现实的统一结构；心灵哲学的辩论历史表明，表征问题远比单纯的对齐度量复杂，AI研究应借鉴这些哲学洞见以避免概念上的混淆和过度承诺。

Abstract: Representation is a central concept in modern machine learning, where it usually refers to internal encodings that support learning and generalization. As models scale and their capabilities become increasingly human-level, this representational language sometimes shifts from an engineering context into the more philosophically loaded domain of mental representation. We argue that this is the case for recent claims about the convergence of representational properties across different AI models. In particular, we assess the arguments developed in The Platonic Representation Hypothesis, according to which this convergence is driven by a unified structure of reality. We examine this claim by introducing arguments and ideas from debates about mental representation in the philosophy of mind. We argue that these philosophical resources can clarify what is at stake in such claims, explain why alignment evidence alone is insufficient for strong metaphysical conclusions, and suggest directions for future research.

</details>


### [162] [Phasor Attention: Mean Root Square Normalization for Phase Manifold Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17822)
*Sungwoo Goo,Hwi-yeol Yun,Sangkeun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出均值根平方归一化(MRSNorm)，通过将通道配对为二维相量并反转传统归一化计算顺序(先局部L2再全局L1平均)，在减半可学习参数的同时，利用三角恒等式实现内置梯度裁剪，防止数值爆炸，确保梯度同质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RMS归一化依赖标量平方的二次累加，存在三个固有问题：异常值引发的数值不稳定、梯度饥饿以及各向异性相位失真。作者试图通过几何约束从根本上解决这些问题。

Method: MRSNorm将通道结构性地配对为2D相量，先计算局部L2幅度(Root Square)，再通过全局L1平均(Mean)聚合。这种操作反转迫使激活值约束在相量流形上，保持共形不变性，且相量分量共享单一仿射权重从而减半参数。Pythagorean恒等式提供内置三角梯度裁剪器。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上的ResNet实验中，尽管参数量减半，MRSNorm在极端超参数设置下成功防止梯度爆炸，保持稳定优化轨迹，而标准归一化则出现梯度发散。

Conclusion: 无约束的空间缩放是标准归一化中的有害冗余，MRSNorm证明了向基于相量的深度表示学习范式转变的可行性。

Abstract: While Root Mean Square Normalization has become the de facto standard for accelerating modern sequence models, its reliance on the quadratic accumulation of independent scalars ($\sum x^2$) inherently triggers outlier-induced numerical instability, gradient starvation, and anisotropic phase distortion. We introduce Mean Root Square Normalization (MRSNorm). By structurally pairing channels into 2D phasors, MRSNorm mathematically inverts the traditional scaling paradigm: it computes the localized $L_2$ magnitudes (Root Square) before aggregating them via a global $L_1$ average (Mean).
  This operational inversion strictly constrains activations to a phasor manifold, preserving conformal invariance. By sharing a single affine weight across phasor components, MRSNorm halves the total number of learnable parameters, proving that unconstrained spatial scaling in standard norms is a harmful redundancy. We analytically demonstrate that this geometric constraint yields a built-in, trigonometric gradient clipper governed by the Pythagorean identity, unconditionally equalizing the local gradient norm to ensure Gradient Homogeneity.
  Empirical evaluations on a ResNet with CIFAR-100 show that despite halved parameters, MRSNorm provides critical structural stability under rigorous stress tests. Under extreme hyperparameter settings where standard normalizations suffer from gradient divergence, MRSNorm successfully prevents numerical explosion and secures stable optimization trajectories. Our findings propose a fundamental paradigm shift toward phasor-based deep representation learning. The implementation of MRSNorm is available at Appendix C.

</details>


### [163] [Theoretical Foundations of $\max$@$k$ Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17823)
*Riccardo Poiani,Martino Bernasconi,Andrea Celli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对有限时域强化学习中的max@k目标进行理论分析，证明了该问题与标准期望回报最大化存在本质差异：马尔可夫策略通常不足够，需要状态增广来恢复最优性，且学习max@k最优策略在统计上比标准RL更困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大推理模型（如代码生成、定理证明）常采用生成K个响应并基于retry-aware指标（如max@k）评估性能，但该准则下的理论基础十分有限。

Method: 通过理论分析刻画max@k优化与标准期望回报最大化的区别，证明马尔可夫策略的不足，提出一种紧凑的状态增广方法恢复最优性，并设计一个达到最优样本复杂度的高效算法。

Result: 揭示了历史依赖策略与非历史依赖策略之间的性能差距；证明了学习max@k最优策略在统计上比标准RL更困难；提供了达到最优样本复杂度的算法。

Conclusion: max@k学习问题在有限时域RL中具有独特的理论特性，需通过状态增广和历史依赖策略来实现最优，且其统计复杂度高于标准RL。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning is a cornerstone technique for modern large reasoning models. Usually, for difficult tasks such as code generation and theorem proving, the agent is evaluated by generating $K$ responses rather than sampling a single response, and performance is then measured using a retry-aware metric such as $\max$@$k$. Despite their practical importance, the theoretical foundations of learning under such criteria remain limited. In this work, we provide a theoretical study of the $\max$@$k$ learning problem in finite-horizon reinforcement learning. We show that optimizing the $\max$@$k$ objectives is fundamentally different from standard expected-return maximization. In particular, we prove that Markovian policies are in general insufficient, identify a compact state augmentation that restores optimality, and explicitly characterize the performance gap that can arise between history-dependent and non-history-dependent policies. Moreover, we show that learning $\max$@$k$-optimal policies is statistically harder than standard reinforcement learning and provide an efficient algorithm that achieves the optimal sample complexity rate.

</details>


### [164] [Mobius Learning: Cyclic Depth Folding in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17843)
*Tongtian Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Mobius Learning训练架构，基于循环深度折叠（cyclic depth folding）让不同数据流使用循环移位后的block顺序，使同一block组同时承担浅层和深层角色（深度角色叠加）。实验表明，在GPT-2 small模型上，该方法在更大Transformer block序列轮次下取得比固定顺序循环Transformer更低的验证损失，且天然适合内存受限的分布式训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统观点中Transformer浅层和深层block必须按固定有序深度轴分配角色的设定，探索同一block组能否同时承担浅层和深层角色。

Method: 提出Mobius Learning训练架构，核心是循环深度折叠：不同数据流遵循循环移位的block顺序，同一block组在不同数据流中分别处于序列的浅层和深层位置，实现深度角色叠加（depth-role superposition）。

Result: 在四worker实验（modded GPT-2 small 124M模型，2.5B FineWeb tokens，Muon优化器）中，Mobius Learning在更大的Transformer block序列轮次下取得了比固定顺序循环Transformer更低的验证损失。

Conclusion: Transformer block组不必固守固定的浅层或深层角色，循环深度折叠开辟了新的设计空间；且该方法天然适合内存受限的分布式训练（原始数据保持本地，每个worker仅存储一组block而非完整block栈）。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models organize computation along an ordered depth axis, where shallow and deep blocks often develop distinct representational roles. We challenge the conventional view that these roles must remain tied to a block's position in the ordered sequence. We introduce Mobius Learning, a training architecture based on cyclic depth folding, in which different data streams follow cyclically shifted block orders. The same block group is therefore applied early in the block sequence for some data streams and late for others, so it is optimized in both shallow and deep roles, a phenomenon we call depth-role superposition. Surprisingly, in four-worker experiments with a modded GPT-2 small (124M) model trained on 2.5B FineWeb tokens using Muon, Mobius Learning achieves lower validation loss than a fixed-order looped Transformer at larger numbers of Transformer block-sequence passes. This counterintuitive result shows that a block group need not remain confined to one fixed shallow or deep role within the block sequence and opens a new design space based on cyclic depth folding. Crucially, this structure makes Mobius Learning particularly well suited to memory-constrained distributed training: raw training data remain local, while each worker stores one block group rather than the complete Transformer block stack.

</details>


### [165] [Distributional Soft Bellman Operator under the Cramér Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17897)
*Keru Wang,Yixin Deng,Yao Lyu,Stephen Redmond,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布性软策略迭代（DSPI）中固定策略的分布性软贝尔曼算子在Cramér几何下的压缩性质，证明了该算子是√γ-压缩算子，存在唯一不动点，并给出了相应的CDF级数收敛性条件，进一步将该问题等价转换为谱域Hilbert空间表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布性强化学习与最大熵控制结合的DSPI框架中，策略评估步骤依赖于分布性软贝尔曼算子，需要合适的概率度量来保证贝尔曼更新的可控性。本文旨在探究Cramér几何下该算子是否具有压缩性质及唯一不动点。

Method: 直接对可容许的CDF域进行操作，构建CDF层面的分布性软贝尔曼算子；在Cramér度量下证明其√γ-压缩性质；通过均匀一阶矩条件（而非分别假设奖励和熵的有界性）导出有限Cramér域性质；并利用共轭变换将问题等价转移到谱域Hilbert空间表示。

Result: 证明了固定策略下的分布性软贝尔曼算子是√γ-压缩算子，存在唯一不动点，且迭代策略评估收敛；该性质由组合的一步奖励熵偏移的均匀一阶矩条件保证；在谱域得到了等价的Hilbert空间表示。

Conclusion: 这些结果确定了DSPI策略评估步骤在Cramér几何下的贝尔曼不动点，为研究DSPI类算法中的近似评论员、评估误差和评论员损失设计提供了参考基准。

Abstract: Distributional soft policy iteration (DSPI) provides an important framework for combining distributional reinforcement learning (DRL) with maximum-entropy control, in which the policy evaluation step is governed by a distributional soft Bellman operator acting on entropy-regularised returns. Theoretical analysis of such an evaluation step requires a probability metric under which Bellman updates can be controlled, typically by showing that the operator contracts the distance between any two candidate return-distribution estimates. In this paper, we focus on the Cramér geometry, a cumulative distribution function (CDF)-based metric with an $L^2$ structure, and study whether the fixed-policy distributional soft Bellman operator has this contraction property and hence a unique fixed point under this metric. Working directly on an admissible CDF field domain, we formulate the CDF-level distributional soft Bellman operator, prove that it is a $\sqrtγ$-contraction, and obtain the corresponding unique fixed point together with convergent iterative policy evaluation. The CDF formulation also shows that this finite-Cramér-domain property follows from a uniform first-moment condition on the combined one-step reward entropy shift, rather than from separate uniform boundedness assumptions on the reward and entropy terms. We then transport the same evaluation problem to the spectral domain by conjugation, obtaining an equivalent Hilbert-space representation of the same decision process. Taken together, these results identify the Cramér-geometric Bellman fixed point associated with the policy-evaluation step of DSPI, providing a reference point for studying approximate critics, evaluation error, and critic-loss design in DSPI-style algorithms.

</details>


### [166] [The Art of Not Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17944)
*Ashmith Atmuri,Akshay Kumar,Yashaswini Rao Bhogarajula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CMP架构，一种无反向传播的局部稀疏学习网络，在15个文本域的增量学习任务中，其向后迁移比使用EWC的Transformer好15-19倍，验证了局部稀疏学习本身能抵抗灾难性遗忘的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证一个具体假设：灾难性遗忘本质上是反向传播的信用分配方式带来的结构性后果，而非仅训练时的问题，因此局部且稀疏的学习规则应能天然抵抗遗忘，无需重放或正则化等修补手段。

Method: 提出CMP架构：输入编码为稀疏关系码，存储于双层竞争记忆网络，完全通过局部无梯度更新学习，整个网络无反向传播。在15个文本域的域增量协议下进行三播复现实验，并与在线EWC训练的同等规模Transformer对比。

Result: CMP的向后迁移比使用EWC的Transformer好15-19倍，且该结果在域顺序控制下稳健（范围+0.24至+0.44）。同时报告了与Transformer基线存在显著准确率差距、在视觉基准上的零结果，以及尝试结合提升准确率机制时的失败案例。

Conclusion: 中心主张是狭义且可证伪的：在精确规定的条件下，局部、稀疏、非反向传播的学习在抵抗灾难性遗忘方面显著优于带标准修补（EWC）的反向传播。

Abstract: We introduce CMP (Cognitive Memory Primitive), an architecture that represents inputs as sparse relational codes, stores them in a two-tier competitive memory, and learns entirely through local, gradient-free updates, with no backpropagation anywhere in the network. We use this architecture to test a specific hypothesis: that catastrophic forgetting, usually treated as a training-time defect to be patched with replay or regularization, is instead a structural consequence of how backpropagation assigns credit and that a learning rule that is local and sparse by construction should resist it without a patch. On a controlled domain-incremental protocol across 15 text domains, three-seed replicated, CMP's backward transfer is 15-19x better than a matched-size Transformer trained with online EWC, and the result survives a domain-order control (reported as a range, +0.24 to +0.44, rather than a single figure). We report this alongside a real, substantial accuracy gap versus the Transformer baseline, a null result on a recognized vision benchmark, and a diagnosed, unresolved failure attempting to combine this architecture with a separate mechanism that improves raw accuracy, disclosed because an honest negative result is more useful than an omitted one. The central claim is narrow and falsifiable: local, sparse, non-backpropagation learning measurably resists catastrophic forgetting better than backpropagation with its standard fix, under conditions we state precisely.

</details>


### [167] [A Geometric Perspective on Stabilizing Value Conflict Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17946)
*Saket Reddy,Andy Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了链式思维（CoT）推理如何通过平滑损失景观的最陡方向，解决基于RLHF的标量奖励在价值冲突场景下的优化不稳定问题，并提出一种面向价值冲突的CoT设计来提升道德推理能力，推动LLM的多元对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在使用RLHF的压缩标量奖励进行训练时，难以处理价值冲突场景下的优化不稳定问题。

Method: 从几何角度分析了CoT推理对损失景观最陡方向的平滑作用，并设计了面向价值冲突的新型CoT策略。

Result: CoT与损失景观最陡方向的进一步平滑相关，缓解了标量奖励的优化不稳定性；面向价值冲突的CoT在道德推理相关下游基准上表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 明确修改和改进推理动态设计是提升模型在复杂价值冲突场景下性能的有效途径，有助于推进LLM的多元对齐。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often struggle to navigate value conflicts when trained with the compressed scalar rewards of Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF). To address this challenge, we investigate how chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning can help improve performance in this domain. Geometrically, we show that CoT correlates with further smoothing the model's loss landscape in its sharpest direction, helping resolve the optimization instability of traditional scalar rewards. We also demonstrate via relevant downstream benchmarks that value conflict-focused CoT may generalize to different kinds of moral reasoning, demonstrating that this CoT has the potential to be an effective mechanism for better moral reasoning. To capitalize on this potential, we create a new value conflict-focused CoT design that further smooths the sharpest direction of the loss landscape and increases moral reasoning performance. This finding shows that explicitly modifying and improving the design of reasoning dynamics offers a promising avenue for improving model performance on user requests with complex value conflicts, advancing pluralistic alignment in LLMs.

</details>


### [168] [DiFA: Inference-Time Forward-Process Alignment for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17972)
*Shigui Li,Delu Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiFA，一种无需训练、基于前向过程对齐的扩散模型推理框架。它将数据预测重新定义为序列状态估计问题，借鉴卡尔曼滤波聚合历史预测，并通过偏差引导机制保留细节，在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上显著提升了FID、IS和FD-DINOv2等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型推理框架将生成视为数值积分问题，将模型当作精确估计器，忽略了去噪过程中固有的统计不确定性。

Method: 提出DiFA，这是一个无需训练的框架，将推理时的数据预测精炼重新定义为序列状态估计问题。受卡尔曼滤波启发，根据结构一致性和噪声水平兼容性聚合历史预测，并引入偏差引导机制以保留细节。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上，FID、IS和FD-DINOv2等指标均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 将推理过程与前向统计结构对齐能够显著提升扩散模型的生成保真度。

Abstract: The prevailing inference framework for diffusion models formulates generation fundamentally as a problem of numerical integration. This perspective casts the model as an exact estimator, neglecting the inherent statistical uncertainty of the denoising process. In this work, we propose Forward-Process Aligned Diffusion prediction (\textbf{DiFA}), a training-free framework that reframes inference-time data prediction refinement as a sequential state estimation problem. Rather than reusing past outputs solely for numerical integration, DiFA treats iterative data predictions along the reverse trajectory as correlated observations to build a forward-aligned temporal consensus. Inspired by Kalman filtering, this consensus aggregates historical predictions according to structural consistency and noise-level compatibility. To counteract the over-smoothing tendency of temporal consensus, we introduce a deviation guidance mechanism to adaptively preserve residual details. Empirically, DiFA yields significant improvements on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet across the evaluated metrics, including FID, IS, and FD-DINOv2, demonstrating that aligning inference with the forward statistical structure substantially improves generative fidelity.

</details>


### [169] [Harness Engineering for LLM-Driven GPU Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17979)
*Yue Shui,Chenyu Ma,Hangfei Xu,Shengzhao Wen,Yanpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以评估框架为中心的LLM驱动GPU内核优化系统，在MLSys 2026 FlashInfer竞赛的NVIDIA Blackwell B200 GPU上，将评估框架与基于性能分析的控制解耦，结合人工编写的技能约束与Codex/Claude Code代理生成候选内核，在五个算子定义上实现了1.12倍至29.68倍不等的加速比，并证明专家经验对可靠优化仍至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可以辅助GPU内核生成，但其实际有效性取决于能否对生成代码进行可靠的约束、验证、性能分析和选择。现有方法缺乏一个系统化的框架来确保这些环节的闭环控制。

Method: 提出一个以评估框架（harness）为中心的系统，解耦为两个部分：1) 评估框架负责编译、正确性验证、官方对齐的计时和构件归档；2) 基于性能分析的优化控制器将分析器和负载证据转化为有界的候选生成决策。人工编写的技能规则捕获算子约束、参考实现、性能分析流程和提升规则，Codex和Claude Code代理在此约束内生成候选内核。

Result: 在五个算子定义上，官方对齐的构件相比FlashInfer基线分别实现了1.62倍、18.05倍、29.68倍、1.12倍和13.70倍的加速比。Agent-Assisted（代理辅助+专家经验）内核在所有算子定义上均优于Full-Agent（完全代理生成）的构件。

Conclusion: 专家提供的优化方向、高质量参考实现和负载上下文对于可靠的AI驱动内核优化仍然至关重要。将人工专家知识与LLM代理生成能力相结合的混合方法，比完全依赖LLM代理的方法效果更优。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can assist GPU kernel generation, but their practical effectiveness depends on whether generated code can be reliably constrained, validated, profiled, and selected. This paper presents a harness-centered system for LLM-driven GPU kernel optimization in the MLSys 2026 FlashInfer AI Kernel Generation Contest on NVIDIA Blackwell B200 GPUs. The system separates an evaluation harness from a profile-backed optimization controller: the harness enforces compilation, correctness, official-aligned timing, and artifact archival, while the controller turns profiler and workload evidence into bounded candidate-generation decisions. Human-authored skills capture operator constraints, references, profiling procedures, and promotion rules, while Codex and Claude Code agents generate candidate kernels inside those constraints. Across five operator definitions, the retained official-aligned artifacts achieved mean-latency speedups over supplied FlashInfer baselines of 1.62x, 18.05x, 29.68x, 1.12x, and 13.70x. The Agent-Assisted kernels outperform the Full-Agent artifacts across the evaluated definitions, indicating that expert-provided optimization directions, high-quality references, and workload context remain critical for reliable AI-driven kernel optimization.

</details>


### [170] [Information-Based Exploration via Random Features for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17981)
*Waris Radji,Odalric-Ambrym Maillard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于随机傅里叶特征的贝叶斯核方法RFIG，用于信息增益近似和探索奖励计算，在保持理论可解释性的同时实现了与深度探索方法竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习中的表示学习方法尽管拓展了经典探索策略，但往往使算法变得复杂且难以建立理论保证，特别是基于神经网络的不确定性估计缺乏可解释性。

Method: 利用贝叶斯核方法理论，采用随机傅里叶特征近似信息增益，在不可数状态空间中计算探索奖励，并给出了信息增益近似的误差界。

Result: 在多种控制与导航任务上，RFIG在性能上与成熟的深度探索方法相当，同时提供了更优的理论解释性。

Conclusion: RFIG成功地将探索策略的理论可解释性与深度RL的可扩展性相结合，为乐观探索提供了非黑箱的替代方案。

Abstract: Representation learning has enabled classical exploration strategies to be extended to deep Reinforcement Learning (RL), but often makes algorithms more complex and theoretical guarantees harder to establish. We introduce Random Feature Information Gain (RFIG), grounded in Bayesian kernel methods theory, which uses random Fourier features to approximate information gain and compute exploration bonuses in non-countable spaces. We provide error bounds on information gain approximation and avoid the black-box aspects of neural network-based uncertainty estimation, for optimism-based exploration. We present practical details that make RFIG scalable to deep RL scenarios, enabling smooth integration into standard deep RL algorithms. Experimental evaluation across diverse control and navigation tasks demonstrates that RFIG achieves competitive performance with well-established deep exploration methods while offering superior theoretical interpretation.

</details>


### [171] [fSRD: Fuzzy Spectral Region Decomposition -- Automated Multi Operator Koopman Representations via an Adaptive Spectral Learning Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17990)
*Charles Bokor,Mark Cary,Denise Morrey,Fabrizio Bonatesta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模糊谱区域分解（fSRD），一种基于多Koopman算子的全自动学习框架，通过模糊树模型自适应构建局部不变嵌入，实现对非线性混沌动力系统的精确线性重构，兼具可解释性与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度非线性的混沌动力系统在复杂度、表达性和数据效率之间存在根本性权衡。现有的Koopman方法寻求全局有效的算子，但难以在有限维度下识别有用的谱嵌入，且常依赖先验知识或精心策划的数据。

Method: 提出模糊谱区域分解（fSRD），一种全自动学习框架。通过多个Koopman算子估计有限维表示，利用全局模糊树模型自适应构建局部不变嵌入（称为不变分解），受模糊神经架构启发，优先追求简约解。

Result: 在典型混沌系统（Lorenz、Duffing）和高维真实数据上，fSRD在数据丰富和有限的数据场景下均展现出强预测精度、可解释性和鲁棒表达性。

Conclusion: fSRD弥合了可解释的算子理论模型与表达性数据驱动序列学习之间的鸿沟，验证了该方法在非线性动力学建模中的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Highly nonlinear chaotic dynamical systems remain difficult to model due to fundamental trade-offs between complexity, expressivity, and data efficiency. Modern machine learning methods achieve strong predictive performance but often rely on a-priori system knowledge or curated data with limited interpretability. Koopman operator theory offers a promising direction via linear representation in an infinite-dimensional observable space. However, many data-driven Koopman methods seek globally valid operators for which useful finite-dimensional spectral embeddings remain difficult to identify under these constraints. To overcome associated limitations, we introduce Fuzzy Spectral Region Decomposition (fSRD), a fully automated learning framework for estimating finite Koopman representation via multiple operators. The proposed method realizes a data-adaptive framework for assembling locally invariant embeddings, termed Invariant Decomposition. fSRD achieves highly accurate linear reconstructions of nonlinear systems while learning finite-dimensional representations of their induced evolution operators, bridging interpretable operator-theoretic models with expressive data-driven sequence learning. These embeddings are adaptively constructed via a global fuzzy tree model, drawing inspiration from fuzzy neural architectures to learn the induced dynamics while prioritizing parsimonious solutions. Empirical results across canonical chaotic systems (e.g., Lorenz and Duffing) and high-dimensional real-world data demonstrate strong predictive accuracy, interpretability, and robust expressivity across data-rich and data-limited regimes, highlighting the method's generality.

</details>


### [172] [MADA-RL: Multi-Agent Debate-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Parameter-Efficient Reasoning in Compact Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18006)
*Martino M. L. Pulici,Cuong Xuan Chu,Evgeny Kharlamov,Zifeng Ding,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MADA-RL提出一种轻量级后训练框架，通过辩论感知的对抗性优势信号和LoRA微调，将紧凑模型（≤4B参数）拆分为生成器和评论家角色协同训练，在数学推理任务中以16倍更少的可训练参数实现性能提升，并分析了与强基线的差距来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理能力强但训练成本高，紧凑模型（≤4B参数）在有限预算下训练尤为困难。现有方法缺乏针对紧凑模型的高效后训练方案，且静态均值奖励归一化无法为评论家提供精确的信用分配信号。

Method: 提出MADA-RL框架：(1) 将紧凑模型拆分为生成器和评论家两个角色；(2) 通过辩论感知信号训练，关键创新是反事实评论家优势——将评论家优势定义为自身奖励减去生成器集成的每实例准确率，而非静态均值；(3) 仅用LoRA适配器微调少量参数；(4) 部署时通过轻量级多轮协议组合两个角色。

Result: 在5个数学推理基准上，MADA-RL将DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B准确率从39.9%提升至41.9%（+2.0点，p<0.001），可训练参数比全微调基线少16倍，位于准确率-可训练参数帕累托前沿；但未超越在更大数据集上训练的强基线DeepScaleR和STILL-3。

Conclusion: 反事实优势信号是性能提升的核心来源：受训评论家学会纠正生成器错误而非模仿它们，达到了所有评估模型中最高的评论家改进率。MADA-RL为紧凑模型在有限预算下提供了一种高效的后训练范式，但其与大数据量训练基线之间的差距仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Large language models achieve strong reasoning performance, but often at prohibitive training cost - a challenge that is especially acute for compact models ($\leq 4 \, \mathrm{B}$ parameters) trained under limited budgets. We introduce MADA-RL, a post-training framework that specializes compact models into generator and critic roles and trains them with a debate-aware learning signal, fine-tuning only a small subset of parameters via LoRA adapters. Our central contribution is a counterfactual critic advantage: a dynamic, role-conditioned baseline that redefines the critic's advantage as its reward minus the generator ensemble's per-instance accuracy. This explicitly optimizes critics to improve over generator consensus rather than to merely reproduce a correct answer, yielding more targeted credit assignment than static mean-reward normalization. At deployment, the specialized agents are composed in a lightweight multi-round protocol. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, MADA-RL raises the accuracy of the DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B model from $39.9 \, \%$ to $41.9 \, \%$ ($+2.0$ points, $p < 0.001$) using $16$ times fewer trainable parameters than fully fine-tuned baselines, placing it on the accuracy-trainable-parameter Pareto front. It approaches, but does not surpass, the strongest baselines (DeepScaleR, STILL-3), which are trained on substantially larger datasets; we analyse this gap and the associated inference-time cost directly. A controlled study isolates the source of MADA-RL's gains: the counterfactual advantage produces the highest critic improvement rate of any model evaluated, indicating that trained critics learn to correct generator errors rather than to imitate them.

</details>


### [173] [FlashPDE: A Drop-in Fused Triton Operator Library for Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18020)
*Peiyu Zang,Bosen Xie,Ruoxiang Xu,Yongqiang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashPDE是一个即插即用的融合算子库，通过可微分的Triton内核替代碎片化的PyTorch有限差分执行，在网格化科学机器学习中实现高达37倍的内存降低和2.30倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络(PINNs)在大规模问题中受限于自动微分的显存开销和基于网格的PDE算子执行效率低下。

Method: 提出FlashPDE，一个可微分的融合算子库，采用Triton内核集成融合模板计算、解析离散伴随反向传播和边界梯度修正，通过统一的torch.autograd.Function接口提供14个可微PDE算子（覆盖1D-3D椭圆、抛物线和Navier-Stokes系统）。

Result: 在A100 GPU上，峰值显存降低最高37.0倍，CUDA内核启动减少最高3.5倍，端到端加速最高2.30倍，内核级加速最高19.2倍，数值精度与PyTorch有限差分参考一致。

Conclusion: FlashPDE在PyTorch生态中构建了连接可微PDE求解器与GPU优化数值计算的硬件高效执行层。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) solve PDEs by incorporating physical constraints into neural-network training, but large-scale problems are limited by automatic-differentiation memory overhead and inefficient execution of grid-based PDE operators. We present FlashPDE, a drop-in fused operator library for grid-based scientific machine learning. FlashPDE replaces fragmented PyTorch finite-difference execution with differentiable Triton kernels. Each operator integrates fused stencil evaluation, an analytic discrete-adjoint backward pass, and boundary-gradient correction within a unified torch.autograd.Function interface. The library provides 14 differentiable PDE operators covering 17 configurations across 1D--3D elliptic, parabolic, and Navier--Stokes systems, while remaining independent of neural architectures and training strategies. Experiments on an NVIDIA A100 GPU show that FlashPDE reduces peak memory usage by up to 37.0x compared with coordinate-based automatic differentiation and reduces CUDA kernel launches by up to 3.5x compared with eager PyTorch finite-difference implementations. Across six representative PDE benchmarks, FlashPDE achieves up to 2.30x end-to-end time-to-solution speedup and up to 19.2x kernel-level acceleration while maintaining numerical agreement with PyTorch finite-difference references. FlashPDE provides a hardware-efficient execution layer that bridges differentiable PDE solvers and GPU-optimized numerical computation within the PyTorch ecosystem.

</details>


### [174] [L1 Augmented Attention as an Improved Vector Similarity Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18027)
*Kurt Godden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出L1增强注意力机制，通过在点积注意力中引入可学习的L1距离项，融合方向对齐与坐标偏差惩罚，在WikiText 2上实现高达14.5%的困惑度降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准缩放点积注意力将方向对齐与向量幅值混为一谈，限制了其作为Transformer模型中相似度度量的有效性。

Method: 在点积分数中减去一个可学习的、头特定的查询-键L1距离；为降低计算成本，将查询和键投影到低维子空间中，其参数专门保留信息性L1结构。

Result: 在紧凑型Transformer上，WikiText 2困惑度降低高达14.5%，优于原始Transformer基线和RBF L2核。分析显示不同层和头存在独特的几何角色与强特异性分工。

Conclusion: 用L1几何丰富注意力机制为现代语言模型中的相似性计算提供了原理性且有效的改进，兼具精度提升与并行效率优势。

Abstract: Scaled dot product attention conflates directional alignment and vector magnitude, limiting its effectiveness as a similarity metric in Transformer models. We introduce L1 augmented attention, a simple and computationally parallelizable modification that subtracts a learned, head specific L1 distance between queries and keys from the dot product score. This hybrid similarity captures complementary geometric information. Dot product rewards directional alignment, while L1 penalizes coordinate deviations. To reduce the cost of L1 computation, we project queries and keys into low dimensional subspaces whose parameters specialize to preserve informative L1 structure. Evaluated on WikiText 2 using a compact transformer, L1 augmented attention achieves up to a 14.5% reduction in perplexity over the original transformer baseline and outperforms an RBF L2 kernel. Analysis of norm variance and learned L1 weights reveals distinct geometric roles across layers and strong head level specialization. These results demonstrate that enriching attention with L1 geometry provides a principled and effective improvement to similarity computation in modern language models, with practical benefits for both accuracy and parallel efficiency.

</details>


### [175] [Adaptive Mamba Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18043)
*Zeyuan Song,Zheyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应Mamba神经算子（AMO），通过将再生核融入状态空间模型（SSM）并构建Takenaka-Malmquist系统，在任意几何和多种网格上求解偏微分方程（PDE），并在多个基准问题上取得优于现有方法的相对L²误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意几何和多种网格上精确求解偏微分方程是科学与工程应用中的重要任务，现有方法存在局限性。

Method: 提出AMO框架，将再生核融入SSM而非使用核积分公式，通过构建Takenaka-Malmquist系统实现与自适应傅里叶分解理论的对齐，从而在点云、结构化网格、规则网格和不规则域上近似PDE解流形。

Result: 在流体物理、固体物理和金融等多个领域的挑战性基准PDE问题上，AMO在相对L²误差指标上持续优于现有最先进求解器。

Conclusion: 本工作为设计可解释的神经算子框架提供了新范式。

Abstract: Accurately solving partial differential equations (PDEs) on arbitrary geometries and a variety of meshes is an important task in science and engineering applications. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Mamba Neural Operators (AMO), which integrates reproducing kernels for state-space models (SSMs) rather than the kernel integral formulation of SSMs. This is achieved by constructing Takenaka-Malmquist systems for the PDEs. AMO offers new representations that align well with the adaptive Fourier decomposition (AFD) theory and can approximate the solution manifold of PDEs on a wide range of geometries and meshes. In several challenging benchmark PDE problems in the fields of fluid physics, solid physics, and finance on point clouds, structured meshes, regular grids, and irregular domains, AMO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art solvers in terms of relative $L^2$ error. Overall, this work presents a new paradigm for designing explainable neural operator frameworks.

</details>


### [176] [SEE: Structure-aware Exploring \& Exploiting for Long-horizon GUI Agent Trajectory Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18046)
*Zhuohang Fan,Beichen Zhang,Yuanfa Li,Changqiao Wu,Wei Liu,Jian Luan,Weigang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SEE框架，通过两阶段数据合成（高效探索构建UI转移图 + 基于图的轨迹合成）来生成高质量、长程、高覆盖率的GUI交互轨迹，用于训练GUI智能体，在真实应用中取得了更好的任务成功率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体数据集存在覆盖不全、长程轨迹稀缺的问题；现有数据收集依赖昂贵人工演示或on-policy方法，导致常见流程过采样而稀有转换和复杂多步流程缺失。

Method: 两阶段数据合成框架：(i) 高效探索阶段，构建屏幕和元素层面的显式UI转移图；(ii) 基于图的合成阶段，通过规划和受控采样组合多样化的多步轨迹，防止虚假循环并支持长程组合。

Result: 在多个真实应用中，SEE生成的轨迹平均长度为14.8步且无虚假循环；基于SEE微调的智能体在任务成功率和未见屏幕泛化方面均有提升。

Conclusion: SEE框架能够可复现、可解释地生成高质量GUI轨迹数据，有效解决了现有数据合成方法覆盖不全和长程能力不足的问题，且代码和数据集将公开。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents powered by vision-language models hold promise for automating real-world mobile tasks. However, progress is limited by the lack of high-coverage, long-horizon interaction trajectories collected from element-rich and rapidly evolving apps. Existing pipelines often rely on costly human demonstrations or on-policy framework, which tends to over-sample common flows while missing rare transitions and complex multi-step procedures. To address this problem, we propose SEE, a two-stage data synthesis framework consisting of (i) an efficient exploration stage that builds an explicit UI transition graph over screens and elements, and (ii) a graph-based synthesis stage that composes diverse multi-step trajectories via planning and controlled sampling. This design yields reproducible and explainable data generation, while explicitly preventing spurious cycles and enabling long-horizon composition. Across multiple real-world apps, SEE produces trajectories with an average length of 14.8 steps while avoiding spurious loops, and agents fine-tuned on SEE achieve improved task success and generalization to unseen screens. We will publicly release our synthesis code and dataset.

</details>


### [177] [SGN: A Similarity-based Generative Network for Data Generation under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18072)
*Jiaqi Zhu,Xincheng Chen,Yuncheng Wu,Zhaojing Luo,Beng Chin Ooi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种相似性生成网络（SGN），该网络在源域上一次性训练，无需参数更新即可适配新目标域，通过标签诱导的相似性结构和编码器-解码器架构，利用目标域少量标注代表集生成具有目标域特征且保持类别一致性的样本，用于目标域数据增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在源域训练后，面对分布偏移的目标域时样本对齐效果差，限制了其在目标域数据增强中的有效性；现有的目标域适配方法需要额外优化和域特定参数，缺乏可重用性。

Method: 提出SGN框架，包含：1）学习由标签诱导的成对相似性结构化的潜在空间；2）通过编码器-解码器架构保留重建信息；3）生成时，将目标域少量标注代表集编码后在该潜在空间中组合，使生成样本继承目标域特征同时保持类别一致性。

Result: 在图像和表格数据集上的实验表明，SGN在源域到目标域分布偏移下，能有效进行目标引导的数据增强。

Conclusion: SGN是一种可重用的、无需参数更新的生成框架，通过相似性结构化的潜在空间实现跨域分布对齐，有效解决了目标域数据增强中的分布偏移问题。

Abstract: Generative models trained on a source domain often produce samples that are poorly aligned with shifted target domains, limiting their effectiveness for target-domain data augmentation. Although target-specific adaptation can reduce this mismatch, it typically requires additional optimization and domain-specific parameters. We propose a Similarity-based Generative Network (SGN), a reusable framework that is trained once on labeled source data and applied to new target domains without parameter updates. SGN learns a latent space structured by label-induced pairwise similarities while preserving reconstructive information through an encoder-decoder architecture. At generation time, a small labeled representative set from the target domain is encoded and combined in the learned latent space, allowing the generated samples to inherit target-specific characteristics while maintaining class consistency. We further analyze the realizability and dimensionality requirements of the proposed similarity structure. Experiments on image and tabular datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SGN for target-guided data augmentation under source-to-target distribution shifts.

</details>


### [178] [Sobek: Streaming Equivariant Tensor Product Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18074)
*Vladimir Chorošajev,Cédric Bény*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sobek，一种基于流式执行的等变图神经网络计算框架，通过重新关联径向投影、球谐耦合和图聚合操作，将边缘局部乘积直接消费到接收端状态，消除了传统实现中随图规模和算子宽度快速增长的工作空间和内存流量，在75项性能对比中全部取得1.2倍至49.7倍加速，峰值内存降低高达99%，并能执行超出OpenEquivariance容量两个数量级的工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的等变图神经网络在实现中需要物化边缘特定的权重、消息和伴随矩阵，导致张量积工作空间和内存流量随图规模和算子宽度快速增长，限制了可行的工作负载，并使大规模问题无法充分利用GPU资源。作者旨在证明这些边缘大小的中间变量并非等变算子本身的固有需求，而是执行调度的产物。

Method: 通过重新关联径向投影、球谐耦合和图聚合操作，将边缘局部乘积直接消费到有界接收端状态中，形成流式计算公式。该流式公式保留了全连接的多重性混合，并扩展到前向、反向和双反向传播。在生成的CUDA后端Sobek中实现该公式。

Result: 在两个算子家族和所有三种微分阶数的75项容量匹配对比中，Sobek全部更快，加速比从1.2倍到49.7倍不等，峰值分配内存降低高达99%。同时能执行超出OpenEquivariance容量两个数量级的工作负载，同时保持接近峰值的吞吐量。

Conclusion: 边缘放大的张量积工作空间是传统执行调度的属性，而非等变卷积本身的固有需求。流式公式能够在保持计算等价性的同时大幅提升性能和内存效率。

Abstract: Equivariant graph neural networks repeatedly apply edge-conditioned tensor-product convolutions over graph edges.
