Conventional implementations materialize edge-specific weights, messages, and adjoints, causing tensor-product workspace and memory traffic to grow rapidly with graph size and operator width. This limits feasible workloads and can prevent larger problems from fully utilizing the GPU.
  We show that these edge-sized intermediates are artifacts of the execution schedule, not requirements of the equivariant operator. By reassociating radial projection, spherical-harmonic coupling, and graph aggregation, edge-local products can be consumed directly into bounded receiver-side state. The resulting streaming formulation preserves fully connected multiplicity mixing and extends through forward, backward, and double backward.
  We implement this formulation in Sobek, a generated-CUDA backend, and evaluate it across edge-scaling regimes and varied feature structures. Across two operator families and all three differentiation orders, Sobek is faster in all 75 capacity-matched comparisons, with speedups ranging from $1.2\times$ to $49.7\times$, and reduces peak allocated memory by up to 99\%. It also executes workloads up to two orders of magnitude beyond OpenEquivariance's capacity while retaining near-peak throughput. These results show that edge-scaled tensor-product workspace is a property of the conventional schedule, not of equivariant convolution itself.

</details>


### [179] [Generalised Bellman recurrence and three dualities in sequential decision-making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18077)
*Fernando E. Rosas,David Hyland,Daniel Polani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Bellman方程成立所需的三个核心条件（动态分解、回报递归分解、不确定性聚合兼容性），并展示了当这些条件不满足时可通过状态增广或变形恢复可解性，同时发现了三重对偶性统一了强化学习、控制论和决策理论中的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Bellman方程形式背后的根本原因，理解其成立所需的条件以及不成立时的补救措施，并揭示不同领域中相关方法之间的内在联系。

Method: 通过分析最优值函数的递归性质，识别出三个必要条件：动态通过充分统计量分解、回报递归分解、不确定性聚合与两者兼容。当条件不满足时，通过状态增广或对回报/动态进行变形来恢复可解性。进一步利用对偶性分析统一不同领域的方法。

Result: 发现三个核心条件共同作用产生了Bellman方程；揭示了概率与回报、回报与聚合、聚合与概率之间的三重对偶性；展示了这些对偶性来源于单一构造，统一了强化学习、控制和决策理论中的方法。

Conclusion: Bellman方程的形式源于三个条件的互洽性，当条件不成立时可通过状态增广或变形恢复可解性，三重对偶性框架为理解不同领域的方法提供了统一视角。

Abstract: What gives the Bellman equation its form? We show that the recursive properties of optimal value functions follow from three conditions: that the dynamics decomposes through sufficient statistics, that the return decomposes recursively, and that the aggregation of uncertainty is compatible with both. When all three conditions hold on a common state, the Bellman equation arises from their mutual consistency; when one fails, tractability can often be recovered by augmenting the state or by deforming return or dynamics. The same conditions are shown to give rise to three dualities: one between probability and return, one between return and aggregation, and one between aggregation and probability. Our framework reveals these dualities as arising from a single construction, unifying methods developed separately across reinforcement learning, control, and decision theory.

</details>


### [180] [SelectInfer: Selective Neuron Loading and Computation for On-Device LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18081)
*Huzaifa Shaaban Kabakibo,Eric Schniedermeyer,Artem Burchanow,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SelectInfer是一种面向边缘设备的LLM推理优化框架，通过选择性神经元加载和计算，在保持任务性能的同时显著降低内存占用和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在资源受限的边缘设备上部署面临高计算和内存需求的挑战，现有粗粒度剪枝或量化方法会损害精度或需要重训练。

Method: 提出SelectInfer框架，包含离线LLM分析器以识别任务特定和通用神经元，并实现两种优化：选择性加载（仅加载最重要的神经元子集以降低内存）和选择性计算（运行时仅动态计算最相关神经元）。

Result: 在多个数据集上的评估表明，SelectInfer在显著降低内存占用和计算量的同时，保持了任务性能。

Conclusion: SelectInfer为在边缘设备上部署LLM提供了一种实用且有效的方案，通过细粒度的神经元级优化实现了效率与性能的权衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across a range of Natural Language Processing (NLP) tasks, but their high computational and memory demands pose significant challenges for deployment on resource-constrained edge devices. Existing approaches to model compression and optimization often rely on coarse-grained pruning or quantization, which can compromise accuracy or require re-training and fine-tuning. In this work, we introduce SelectInfer, a neuron-level optimization framework that enables efficient LLM inference on edge devices through selective neuron loading and computation. By profiling and identifying both task-specific and general-purpose neurons using an offline LLM profiler, SelectInfer implements two key optimizations: selective loading, which reduces memory footprint by selectively loading a subset of neurons that were identified to be most important during the offline stage, and selective computation, which dynamically computes only the most relevant neurons at runtime. Evaluation across multiple datasets shows that SelectInfer achieves significant reductions in memory footprint and computation while preserving task performance, making it a practical step towards enabling LLM deployment on edge devices

</details>


### [181] [Enhancing Rubric-based RL via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18082)
*Mingxuan Xia,Yuhang Yang,Chao Ye,Shuai Zhu,Shenzhi Yang,Guangcheng Zhu,Yuhang Zhang,Cheng Peng,Haobo Wang,Siqing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CriPO方法，通过自蒸馏同时解决基于Rubric的RL中未探索标准(UC)和被抑制标准(SC)问题，不引入训练-推理不匹配，在医学和科学基准上以约2倍少的优化步骤实现更强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Rubric的RL在开放任务中提升LLMs表现良好，但存在UC（无探索标准无优化信号）和SC（被满足但标量奖励聚合导致非正优势而丢失信号）两类失败模式，现有方法仅关注UC且引入训练-推理不匹配。

Method: 提出CriPO：针对UC，构建标准注入自教师并计算局部前向KL损失以注入缺失行为；针对SC，采用反事实自教师定位负优势rollout中标准相关token并将其优势翻转为正值，避免有用模式被抑制。

Result: 在医学和科学基准上，CriPO一致优于基于Rubric的RL，以约2倍少的优化步骤实现更强最终性能。

Conclusion: CriPO通过自蒸馏同时处理UC和SC且无训练-推理不匹配，显著提升了基于Rubric的RL的效率与效果。

Abstract: Rubric-based RL has recently shown promise in improving LLMs on open-ended tasks. A widely recognized limitation of rubric-based RL is limited exploration: criteria that no rollout manages to satisfy (Unexplored Criteria, UC) receive no optimization signal. Recent methods address this by incorporating rubric information as external guidance during rollout, yet they introduce a train-inference mismatch: the policy is optimized on rollouts produced under external guidance while this guidance is absent at inference time, causing error accumulation through autoregressive decoding. Moreover, these exploration-focused approaches overlook a fundamentally different failure mode that we term Suppressed Criteria (SC) -- criteria that are satisfied by some rollouts yet whose learning signals are lost during optimization because scalar reward aggregation assigns them non-positive aggregate advantages. Our analysis reveals that SC are remarkably prevalent: over 57% of samples exhibit this failure mode throughout training, with an average of 1.8 SC per sample. To simultaneously address both UC and SC without introducing training-inference mismatch, we propose Criterion-Distilled Policy Optimization (CriPO), which enhances rubric-based RL via on-policy self-distillation. For UC, CriPO constructs a criterion-injection self-teacher and computes a localized forward-KL loss to inject missing behaviors into the policy. For SC, CriPO employs a counterfactual self-teacher to locate criterion-relevant tokens in negative-advantage rollouts and flips their token-level advantages to positive values, preserving useful patterns that would otherwise be suppressed. Experiments on medicine and science benchmarks demonstrate that CriPO consistently outperforms rubric-based RL, achieving stronger final performance with approximately $2\times$ fewer optimization steps.

</details>


### [182] [The Label Complexity of Class-Conditional Coverage under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18088)
*Weijia Han,Lisha Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分类器在分布偏移下逐类覆盖率的失效问题，证明了无标签方法无法同时实现有效且高效的逐类覆盖，量化了恢复逐类有效性所需的标签量，并通过骨架动作识别和自然图像损坏基准实验展示了边界效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准识别系统的评估中，训练集和测试集通常存在分布偏移，导致分裂保形预测的边缘覆盖率看似正常（~90%），但逐类覆盖率严重不均衡（最差类别仅~70%，10/60类低于80%）。本文旨在刻画恢复逐类有效性的代价与条件。

Method: 通过理论分析建立三个结果：(1) 不可行性定理：当偏移联合作用于协变量和标签时，无标签方法无法实现有效且高效的逐类覆盖；(2) 量化所需标签量：仅需逐类有效性需少量目标标签，同时保证有效性和效率的标签数随效率容差平方反比和对数类数增长；(3) 评估预测驱动推断族：即使最优利用伪标签，效率提升也受限于小常数因子。实验基于骨架动作识别和自然图像损坏基准。

Result: (1) 无标签方法无法同时实现逐类覆盖有效性和效率；(2) 逐类有效性单独只需每类少量目标标签，而同时兼顾效率需更多标签（与效率容差平方成反比、与类数对数成正比）；(3) 利用分类器伪标签对效率提升有限；真实数据实验显示源域逐类校准可恢复大部分逐类差距，直至边缘覆盖率本身开始失效。

Conclusion: 分布偏移下逐类覆盖率的失效是系统性问题，恢复需要目标标签且存在理论下限。源域校准在边缘覆盖率失效前能有效恢复逐类覆盖，该现象在骨架数据和自然图像数据上均成立。

Abstract: Standard evaluation of many recognition systems contains distribution shift by construction, since benchmarks place disjoint conditions in the training and test splits. Under such a shift, split conformal prediction keeps marginal coverage near the nominal level while per-class coverage fails silently: on a real cross-subject skeleton benchmark, marginal coverage stays near ninety percent, the worst action class is covered about seventy percent of the time, and ten of the sixty classes fall below eighty percent coverage.
  We characterize the cost of restoring per-class validity. First, an impossibility: once the shift acts jointly on the covariates and the labels, the target class-conditional score law is unidentified from source labels and an unlabeled target sample, so no label-free method attains per-class coverage that is at once valid and efficient. Second, we make the cost precise: per-class validity alone needs only a handful of target labels per class, while the label count necessary and sufficient for validity together with per-class efficiency grows as the inverse square of the efficiency tolerance and the logarithm of the number of classes, with matching upper and lower bounds. Third, within the evaluated prediction-powered inference family, even the most favorable use of the classifier's own pseudo-labels on an unbounded unlabeled target pool improves efficiency by at most a small constant factor where coverage collapses.
  Skeleton action recognition is our real-data case study. A per-class calibration using source labels alone recovers a substantial share of the per-class gap while the shift preserves marginal coverage, and stops helping exactly when marginal coverage itself breaks. Three real shifts of increasing severity trace this boundary, and the same collapse and recovery appears on a natural-image corruption benchmark, beyond any single modality.

</details>


### [183] [Empowering On-Device Model Adaptation with an Edge AI Inference Accelerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18101)
*Mateusz Piechocki,Alessandro Capotondi,Marek Kraft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种异构适应管道，利用商用的边缘AI推理加速器(Hailo-8L)进行冻结主干网络的特征提取，仅对轻量级分类头在CPU上进行微调，在资源受限设备上实现比树莓派5 CPU基线高达15.4倍的训练加速，并降低单位样本能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧模型自适应对于实现资源受限硬件上的终身个性化至关重要，但计算、功耗和内存限制使得深度神经网络的端到端反向传播不切实际，因此需要一种更高效的片上模型适应方法。

Method: 将计算图分区：预训练主干网络量化至INT8并在Hailo-8L推理加速器上运行提取特征，仅轻量级FP32分类头在主机CPU上微调；关键步骤包括训练后量化恢复以保持加速器生成特征的质量。

Result: 相比树莓派5 CPU基线，训练速度快达15.4倍；在有利设置下具有竞争性吞吐量；持续降低单位样本能耗；训练后量化恢复对保持特征质量和缓解精度损失至关重要。

Conclusion: 提出了一种利用面向推理的边缘加速器进行高效端侧模型适应的实用方法，在资源受限设备上实现了显著的训练加速和能效提升。

Abstract: On-device model adaptation is essential to enable lifelong personalization on resource-constrained hardware, but compute, power, and memory limitations of such devices make end-to-end backpropagation impractical for modern deep neural networks. This work proposes a heterogeneous adaptation pipeline that repurposes a commercial edge AI inference accelerator, Hailo-8L, for frozen-backbone feature extraction during on-device training. The computational graph is partitioned so that the pre-trained backbone is quantized to INT8 and run on the accelerator, while only a lightweight FP32 classification head is fine-tuned on the host CPU, enabling frequent, energy-efficient in-field updates with most weights remaining fixed. Across multiple architectures and datasets, this pipeline achieves up to 15.4x faster wall-clock training time compared to a Raspberry Pi 5 CPU baseline, offers competitive throughput in favorable settings, and consistently reduces energy per sample. Post-training quantization restoration is shown to be crucial for preserving the quality of accelerator-generated features and mitigating accuracy loss in quantization-sensitive architectures. Overall, the results demonstrate a practical approach to efficient on-device adaptation using inference-oriented edge accelerators. The implementation is available at https://github.com/MatPiech/accelerator-training.

</details>


### [184] [LLM-as-a-Coach: Experiential Learning for Non-Verifiable Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18110)
*Tianzhu Ye,Li Dong,Guanheng Chen,He Zhu,Xun Wu,Shaohan Huang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出体验式学习（EL），将LLM裁判模型重构为教练模型，用高带宽、密集的文本反馈替代标量奖励信号，通过基于策略的上下文蒸馏使策略内化体验知识，在开放任务上超越传统强化学习，并缓解奖励破解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习在开放任务中将基于评分标准的评估压缩为标量奖励，丢弃了丰富的文本反馈，且无法区分质量不同的响应，导致学习信号信息量不足。

Method: 将LLM裁判模型重构为教练模型，教练模型将策略生成的响应评估提取为可迁移的体验知识，条件化教师模型，并通过基于策略的上下文蒸馏使策略内化这些知识，形成高带宽的反馈通道。

Result: 在两种策略族上，无论是使用策略自身的反馈还是专有模型的反馈，EL在保留和未见过的开放任务上均持续优于基于评分标准的RL，且分布外泛化能力更强，能有效缓解奖励破解。

Conclusion: 体验知识作为更丰富、更可泛化的学习信号，适用于非可验证任务的后训练阶段。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) on open-ended tasks compresses an LLM's rubric-based evaluation into a scalar reward, discarding rich textual feedback and conflating responses with distinct quality profiles. We propose Experiential Learning (EL), which repurposes the feedback model from an LLM-as-a-Judge into an LLM-as-a-Coach. The coach distills its assessment of each on-policy response into transferable experiential knowledge, which conditions a teacher model and is internalized by the policy through on-policy context distillation. Compared with scalar rewards, this higher-bandwidth feedback channel provides dense supervision and preserves fine-grained preferences among high-quality responses. Across two policy families, with feedback from the policy itself or a proprietary model, EL consistently outperforms rubric-based RL on held-out and unseen open-ended tasks. Notably, EL generalizes better beyond the training distribution, and mitigates reward hacking. These findings establish experiential knowledge as a richer and more generalizable learning signal for post-training on non-verifiable tasks.

</details>


### [185] [Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections for Parameter-Efficient Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18130)
*Valentijn Oldenburg,Floris de Kam,Bente Zuijdam,Lieve Eberson,Nicky van Zutphen,Stef de Wildt,Ivo Verhoeven*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究流形约束超连接（mHC）——残差连接的一种泛化形式——作为一种新型参数高效微调方法。实验表明，单独使用mHC效果不如LoRA，但在相同可训练参数预算下，mHC与LoRA结合能改善语言建模损失，并在1B和7B规模上带来任务相关的性能提升。残差路由被证明是一个有前景的PEFT新维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法大多关注权重或激活的适配，忽略了残差连接这一关键Transformer组件。作者希望探索残差连接的泛化形式（mHC）能否作为一种新型PEFT方法。

Method: 将冻结的OLMo-2骨干网络包裹可学习的残差路由模块（即mHC）。在微调设置中，将残差混合矩阵固定为单位矩阵通常会提升性能。并将mHC与LoRA结合使用。

Result: mHC作为独立PEFT方法未能始终优于LoRA。但在匹配可训练参数预算下，mHC+LoRA组合能改善语言建模损失，并在1B和7B规模上观察到任务相关的基准测试提升。

Conclusion: 残差路由（mHC）被识别为一个独特且有前景的新型PEFT维度，尽管单独使用时性能有限，但与LoRA结合能发挥互补优势。

Abstract: Most parameter-efficient finetuning (PEFT) methods adapt weights or activations, thus leaving one of the key Transformer components unchanged: residual connections. This paper investigates Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC), a generalisation of residual connections, as a novel PEFT approach, wrapping frozen OLMo-2 backbones with learned residual routing modules. We find that mHC can finetune frozen Transformers, but that its role differs fundamentally from the original pre-training setting: in finetuning, fixing the residual mixing matrix to identity often improves performance. As a standalone PEFT method, mHC does not consistently outperform LoRA. However, at matched trainable parameter budgets, mHC+LoRA combinations improve language-modelling loss and show task-dependent benchmark gains at both 1B and 7B scale. Overall, our results identify residual routing as a distinct and promising novel PEFT axis.

</details>


### [186] [Do Language Models Dream of Binding Molecules? Benchmarking LLMs under Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18144)
*Thomas MacDougall,Maksim Kuznetsov,Roman Schutski,Rim Shayakhmetov,Maxim Malkov,Vladimir Aladinskiy,Alex Aliper,Alex Zhavoronkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了通用大语言模型（LLM）在基于结构的药物设计中处理3D空间约束的能力，并与专用扩散模型进行对比。作者提出了3D-Fit基准测试策略，发现LLM虽落后于最先进方法，但能同时处理多种空间约束，具有扩展潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结构的药物设计中，扩散模型是主流范式，而LLM方法虽在分子设计中表现优异，但其在物理推理和3D空间环境理解方面的能力尚未被充分探索。本文旨在系统分析通用LLM是否能够处理复杂的3D约束条件。

Method: 提出了3D-Fit基准测试策略，用于评估LLM在多条件约束下的空间分子生成能力。约束条件包括蛋白质口袋、锚定片段、药效团点和强制性口袋-配体相互作用。将LLM与专用扩散模型进行对比分析。

Result: LLM在3D空间分子生成方面仍落后于最先进的扩散模型，但它们展现出处理多种空间约束的能力，且可扩展到异构设置。

Conclusion: 通用LLM在基于结构的药物设计中具有处理多条件空间约束的潜力，尽管目前性能不及专用扩散模型，但为未来LLM在3D分子生成领域的发展提供了方向。

Abstract: Structure-based drug design (SBDD) leverages the 3D structure of protein targets, often complemented by other spatial constraints, to generate candidate binding molecules. While diffusion models have dominated as a leading paradigm for high-quality 3D molecule generation, LLM-based methods are rapidly emerging in molecular design and have shown competitive performance in pocket-conditioned molecular generation. However, their ability to reason about physics and 3D spatial environments is largely underexplored. In this work, we systematically analyze whether current general-purpose LLMs are capable of navigating complex 3D constraints compared to established baselines such as specialized diffusion models. We consider 3D ligand generation conditioned on protein pockets together with ligand- and interaction-derived spatial constraints, including anchor fragments, pharmacophore points, and mandatory pocket-ligand interactions. To enable this evaluation, we introduce 3D-Fit - a token-efficient benchmarking strategy for assessing LLM performance on multi-conditioned spatial molecule generation. Our findings reveal a clear pattern in LLM spatial capabilities: while they still lag behind state-of-the-art approaches, they are promising and can handle multiple spatial constraints simultaneously, enabling scaling to heterogeneous setups.

</details>


### [187] [Totally Positive Matrices and the Highest-Order Coefficients of the Characteristic Polynomial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18148)
*Tiago Closs,Leandro Farina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究完全正矩阵是否可以通过其特征多项式的高阶系数来区分。利用神经网络分类器和特征归因方法，发现系数 (a_{n-1}, a_{n-2}, a_{n-3}) 在维度5、10、30下能有效分离完全正矩阵与非完全正矩阵，且不同结构化完全正矩阵族在三维系数空间中呈现不同的马氏椭球特征，分离度随维度增加而增大。据此提出关于结构化完全正矩阵族几何分离的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究完全正矩阵能否通过其特征多项式的高阶系数进行区分，并识别最具信息量的系数。

Method: 利用神经网络分类器与特征归因方法，基于正双对角矩阵乘积、Vandermonde矩阵、Cauchy矩阵等结构化完全正矩阵族构建数据集，在维度5、10、30下分析特征多项式最高阶系数的区分能力。

Result: 系数 (a_{n-1}, a_{n-2}, a_{n-3}) 包含强判别信息，能有效分离完全正矩阵与非完全正矩阵；分离呈显著非线性，可用三维系数空间中的马氏椭球自然描述；不同结构化族呈现不同椭球特征，分离度随维度增加而增大。

Conclusion: 提出猜想：结构化完全正矩阵族在由三个最高阶特征多项式系数确定的空间中可被几何分离。

Abstract: We investigate the extent to which totally positive matrices can be distinguished through the highest-order coefficients of their characteristic polynomials. To identify the most informative coefficients, we also employed neural-network classifiers together with feature-attribution methods. Using datasets built from several structured totally positive families, including products of positive bidiagonal matrices, Vandermonde matrices, and Cauchy matrices, we find that the coefficients (a_{n-1}, a_{n-2}, a_{n-3}) already contain strong discriminatory information for separating totally positive from non-totally positive matrices in dimensions 5, 10, and 30. The resulting separation is markedly nonlinear and admits a natural geometric description in the corresponding three-dimensional coefficient space by means of Mahalanobis ellipsoids. These ellipsoids enclose the totally positive samples while excluding most non-totally positive ones. Moreover, different structured totally positive families exhibit distinct ellipsoidal signatures, and the separation between these signatures increases with the dimension. These observations lead us to formulate a conjecture on the geometric separation of structured totally positive families in the space determined by the three highest-order characteristic coefficients.

</details>


### [188] [Differentiable Logic Gate Networks for Low-Latency EEG Classification on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18149)
*Shyamal Y. Dharia,Stephen D. Smith,Camilo E. Valderrama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将可微分逻辑门网络（Diff-Logic）应用于实时脑电图（EEG）分类，通过纯布尔电路替代传统浮点神经网络，在边缘设备上实现了接近恒定的推理时间，在痴呆检测任务中F1分数达到80.2%，比MLP基线高6.8%，同时模型规模缩小14倍、速度提升2.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络在边缘设备上进行实时EEG分类受限于浮点运算，存在延迟高、模型体积大、功耗受限等问题，亟需一种硬件原生、高效能的替代方案。

Method: 采用可微分逻辑门网络（Diff-Logic），将模型编译为纯布尔电路，通过位运算在CPU上执行；在两个分类任务（二分类痴呆检测和三分类情感识别）的四个EEG数据集上进行等参数实验，与同等容量的MLP和二值化神经网络（BNN）在四个复杂度层级（50k-500k参数）上进行比较。

Result: Diff-Logic在痴呆筛查中达到80.2%的Macro F1，比MLP基线高6.8%；在情感识别任务中MLP保持适度优势，但延迟高出2.3倍、模型大小高出14倍（在7W Nvidia Jetson Orin Nano CPU单核上部署）；Diff-Logic推理时间在模型规模扩大10倍时几乎保持不变，在最大复杂度层级获得2.9倍峰值加速。

Conclusion: 基于逻辑的神经架构为资源受限的脑机接口提供了一种实用范式，能够在满足便携式边缘部署的延迟和内存约束的同时，实现具有竞争力甚至更优的性能。

Abstract: Real-time EEG classification on edge devices is bottlenecked by the floating-point arithmetic of conventional neural networks. We investigated Differentiable Logic Gate Networks (Diff-Logic) as a hardware-native alternative that compiles models into pure Boolean circuits executable via bitwise CPU operations. Through rigorous iso-parameter experiments across four EEG datasets spanning two classification tasks, binary dementia detection and 3-class emotion recognition, we compared Diff-Logic against matched-capacity Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) and Binarized Neural Network (BNN) baselines at four complexity tiers (50k-500k parameters). On dementia screening, Diff-Logic achieved 80.2% Macro F1, outperforming the MLP baseline by 6.8%. On emotion recognition, the MLP retained a moderate performance advantage but incurred a 2.3$\times$ higher latency and 14$\times$ larger model size when deployed on a power-constrained (7W) Nvidia Jetson Orin Nano CPU (Single-core). Critically, Diff-Logic inference time remained nearly constant across a 10$\times$ increase in model scale, achieving a peak speedup of 2.9$\times$ over MLPs at the largest complexity tier. Our results establish logic-based neural architectures as a practical paradigm for resource-constrained brain-computer interfaces, achieving competitive or superior performance while natively satisfying the latency and memory constraints of portable edge deployment. Code is available on GitHub: https://github.com/Shyamal-Dharia/eeg-difflogic

</details>


### [189] [The Calibration Channel Determines the Bayes-Error Proxy: An Exact Law for Temperature-Induced Distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18162)
*Shreyas Pradeepkumar Khandale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格刻画了温度缩放（temperature scaling）如何扭曲软标签贝叶斯误差估计器β(z)=E[min(z,1-z)]。证明该估计量与温度严格单调，且温度与代理值之间存在连续双射，使得固定错误率的分类器可报告任意代理值。在高斯logits假设下推导出闭式二参数曲线。实验表明代理值在恒定测试误差下可变化56x至980x，闭式拟合误差<0.018，且校准温度与稳定代理值无对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ishida等人提出的软标签贝叶斯误差估计器β(z)=E[min(z,1-z)]在概率标签非真实后验时脆弱，即使完美校准的软标签也可能产生严重不准确估计。Ushio等人提出保序校准作为修正方案，但温度缩放作为最广泛使用的后验校准映射，其对代理估计量的扭曲机制尚未被精确刻画。

Method: (1) 证明无模型恒等式，将温度缩放后的代理估计量约化为分类器的边际分布；(2) 推导温度与代理值的严格单调性及连续双射关系；(3) 在高斯logits模型下推导代理-温度曲线的二参数闭式解；(4) 在CIFAR-10、Fashion-MNIST、SVHN的8个二分类任务上进行实证验证。

Result: 代理值在恒定测试误差下变化可达56倍至980倍；闭式二参数曲线与经验曲线拟合误差在0.018以内；使期望校准误差最小的校准温度与任何稳定代理值均不重合。

Conclusion: 精确刻画了温度缩放对软标签贝叶斯误差估计器的扭曲机制，提供了可预测的定量描述；强化了实践建议：代理值只有结合产生其概率的机制才有意义。

Abstract: The soft-label Bayes-error estimator beta(z) = E[min(z, 1-z)] of Ishida et al. estimates the irreducible error of a binary task directly from probability-valued labels. Recent work by Ushio et al. showed that this estimator is fragile when the probabilities are not the true posterior: even perfectly calibrated soft labels can yield a substantially inaccurate estimate, and they propose isotonic calibration as a consistent remedy. We complement that line of work by characterizing exactly how the most widely used post-hoc calibration map -- temperature scaling -- distorts the proxy. We prove an exact, model-free identity reducing the temperature-scaled proxy to the classifier's margin distribution, from which we obtain (i) strict monotonicity in the temperature and (ii) a continuous bijection from the temperature axis onto the open interval (0, 1/2), so that a fixed classifier -- with fixed decisions and fixed 0-1 error -- can be made to report any proxy value whatsoever. Under a Gaussian model of the logits we further derive a two-parameter closed form for the entire proxy-versus-temperature curve. Across CIFAR-10, Fashion-MNIST, and SVHN (eight binary tasks), the proxy varies by 56x to 980x at constant test error, the closed form reproduces the empirical curve to within 0.018, and the calibration temperature that minimizes the expected calibration error does not coincide with any stable proxy value. Our results give a precise, predictive account of the distortion whose existence motivates calibration-based remedies, and they reinforce the practical recommendation that a proxy value is meaningful only together with the mechanism that produced its probabilities.

</details>


### [190] [OR Else: A Differentiable Trust Region for Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18163)
*Chinmay Rane,Kanishka Tyagi,Michael Manry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了输出重置（OR）作为一种平滑单侧饱和规则，替代PPO和GRPO中使用的裁剪代理目标，用于大语言模型后训练。在Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct上的实验表明：基于GAE时，PPO-OR比PPO-clip平均奖励模型得分高0.305；基于组相对优势时，GRPO-OR虽未获得更高平均分，但展现出更小的跨度、接近零的终端OR残差和下降的过冲比例。两种对比中OR均改变了优化行为，但奖励效果存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO和GRPO中使用的裁剪代理目标存在有利方向饱和导致的标量目标导数突变问题，作者希望探索更平滑的替代方案——输出重置（OR），以改善大语言模型后训练的优化行为。

Method: 将PPO和GRPO中的裁剪策略项替换为OR平方边际损失（在rollout相对token log-ratio空间中），优势信号决定更新方向，token在越过有利边际后产生零直接OR残差。在Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct模型上，使用Anthropic hh-rlhf数据集和共享奖励模型，分别基于GAE和组相对优势进行比较，每个方法三个随机种子。

Result: GAE下：PPO-OR比PPO-clip平均奖励模型得分高0.305，但种子间跨度更大。组相对优势下：GRPO-OR未获得更高平均分，但跨度更小、终端OR残差接近零、过冲比例下降；两种组相对方法均表现出比GAE方法更大的rollout-to-current log-ratio位移，OR并未持续减小这一位移。在G=2时，GRPO-OR的诊断结果未转化为奖励分数提升。

Conclusion: 输出重置改变了两种匹配对比中的优化行为，但观察到的奖励效果存在差异。在组规模G=2时GRPO-OR的改进诊断未带来分数增益，更大组规模是否改变这一结果有待研究。所报告分数为训练时奖励模型测量值，非留出的人类偏好性能。

Abstract: PPO and the GRPO baseline studied here use clipped surrogate objectives whose favorable-direction saturation introduces an abrupt change in the scalar objective's derivative. We ask whether Output Reset (OR), a smooth one-sided saturation rule, offers a useful alternative for large language model post-training. PPO-OR and GRPO-OR replace the clipped policy term with an OR squared-margin loss in rollout-relative token log-ratio space; the advantage sign determines the update direction, and a token contributes zero direct OR residual after crossing the favorable margin. We compare PPO-clip with PPO-OR under generalized advantage estimation (GAE), and GRPO with GRPO-OR under group-relative advantages, using \texttt{Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct} on Anthropic \texttt{hh-rlhf} with one shared reward model and three seeds per method. Under GAE, PPO-OR has a mean final training-time reward-model score $0.305$ higher than PPO-clip, with a larger observed across-seed spread. Under group-relative advantages, GRPO-OR does not have a higher mean score, but shows a smaller observed spread, a near-zero terminal OR residual, and a declining overshoot fraction, while the matched GRPO clipped-objective trace remains variable. Both group-relative methods exhibit substantially larger rollout-to-current log-ratio displacement than the GAE methods, and OR does not consistently reduce it. Thus, OR changes optimization behavior in both matched comparisons, but the observed reward effect differs between them. At $G=2$, the GRPO-OR diagnostics do not translate into a reward-score gain. Whether larger groups change this outcome remains open. The reported scores are training-time reward-model measurements, not held-out human-preference performance.

</details>


### [191] [A Continual Validation, Updating, and Decision-Making Framework for Self-Adaptive Digital Twins via Robust Model Predictive Control: A Case Study in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18164)
*Yi-Ping Chen,Ying-Kuan Tsai,Vispi Karkaria,Seul Lee,Daniel Apley,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应数字孪生框架，通过Fisher分数漂移检测、LoRA高效微调（<1%参数）和Mann-Whitney U统计验证，在概念漂移下保持代理模型的预测精度和不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生依赖的代理模型在运行条件变化时会出现概念漂移，导致保真度下降。现有自适应框架缺乏原则性的漂移检测机制、从有限流数据高效更新的方法，以及对模型更新是否真正改善预测性能的统计认证。

Method: 提出三组件自适应框架：1）基于Fisher分数向量的多变量漂移检测器，监控代理模型置信度；2）低秩适配（LoRA）实现参数高效的持续学习，仅微调<1%参数；3）Mann-Whitney U检验对更新进行在线统计验证，确保预测性能确实提升后再部署。

Result: 在随机线性系统和定向能量沉积增材制造两个案例中，该方法能够以短延迟成功检测分布漂移，并在突增和渐进漂移下恢复预测精度和不确定性量化。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于神经网络的数字孪生在全生命周期内维持可信性提供了统计严谨且计算可行的途径。

Abstract: Digital Twins rely on surrogate models to mirror physical systems in real time, yet these models can degrade as operating conditions evolve, a phenomenon known as concept drift. Maintaining surrogate fidelity under drift, particularly when models must also capture aleatoric uncertainty, remains an open challenge. Existing adaptive frameworks lack principled mechanisms for detecting when updates are needed, for efficiently adapting models from limited streaming data, and for certifying that updates genuinely improve predictive performance. Here we present an adaptive Digital Twin framework that integrates a Fisher score--based multivariate drift detector, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for parameter-efficient continual learning, and a Mann--Whitney $U$ test for online statistical validation. The framework monitors surrogate-model confidence via Fisher score vectors, triggers targeted fine-tuning of fewer than 1% of model parameters upon drift detection, and statistically certifies predictive improvement before deploying the updated surrogate. Applied to a stochastic linear system and a directed energy deposition additive manufacturing process as case studies, the framework successfully detects distributional shifts with short delays and restores both predictive accuracy and uncertainty quantification under abrupt and incremental drift. These results establish a statistically rigorous and computationally tractable pathway for sustaining the trustworthiness of neural-network--based Digital Twins throughout their operational life cycle.

</details>


### [192] [FlashRT: Agent Harness for Guiding Agents to Deploy Real-Time Multimodal Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18171)
*Krish Agarwal,Zhuoming Chen,Yanyuan Qin,Zhenyu Gu,Atri Rudra,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashRT是一个智能体框架，通过链式编程范式将简单的参考实现自动转化为优化的多GPU部署，在多种多模态应用（视频世界模型、多模态LLM）上实现最高70倍延迟降低和3.6倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务系统和自动并行编译器局限于有限的变换和固定工作负载假设，导致针对新应用的高效部署需要手工实现。作者希望自动化这一过程，使开发者无需手动优化即可获得高性能多GPU部署。

Method: FlashRT采用链式编程范式，通过多轮变换过程：1) 将参考实现转化为中间表示(IR)以捕获数据依赖和持久状态范围；2) 通过顺序解释器验证IR；3) 进行静态分析识别候选变换；4) 在测量门控优化循环中迭代实现、验证和基准测试每个候选方案。

Result: 在NVIDIA B200 GPU上实现最高70倍延迟降低和2.8倍吞吐量提升；在AMD MI355X上实现同等峰值延迟降低且吞吐量提升增至3.6倍；在Qwen3-Omni文本转语音推理中，相比专家实现的vLLM-Omni方案，在AMD MI355X上响应延迟降低65%。

Conclusion: 基于智能体的优化方法在专业优化支持较弱的平台上更具可扩展性，FlashRT能够将简单参考实现自动转化为高效部署，显著超越专家手工优化的实现。

Abstract: Real-time multimodal applications, including voice agents and interactive video generation, compose heterogeneous models into pipelines whose efficient deployment requires application-specific decisions about placement, streaming, and intra-model parallelism. Existing serving systems and auto-parallelism compilers commit to limited transformations and fixed workload assumptions, so achieving high performance on a new application requires hand-crafting an efficient implementation. We present FlashRT, an agent harness that guides coding agents to lift simple developer-written reference implementations into optimized multi-GPU deployments that flexibly weigh target metrics like latency and throughput. Using a new chain-of-program paradigm, FlashRT directs a generic coding agent through a multi-pass transformation process where an agent transforms the reference into an intermediate representation (IR) to capture data dependencies and persistent-state scopes, validates this IR via a sequential interpreter, and performs static analyses to identify candidate transformations. Then, the agent iteratively implements, verifies, and benchmarks each candidate under a measurement-gated optimization loop to produce effective deployments that span different hardware budgets. Across various applications, including video world models and multimodal LLMs, FlashRT converts reference implementations into highly efficient deployments, delivering up to ~70x latency reduction and 2.8x throughput improvement on NVIDIA B200 GPUs. On AMD MI355X GPUs, FlashRT matches the peak latency reduction while increasing peak throughput improvement to 3.6x, demonstrating that agent-driven optimization can be more scalable on platforms with less mature expert optimization. In fact, for Qwen3-Omni text-to-audio inference, FlashRT reduces response latency by 65% compared to the expert vLLM-Omni implementation on AMD MI355X.

</details>


### [193] [Three-Body Scattering for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18198)
*Peng Sun,Zhenglin Cheng,Deyuan Liu,Jun Xie,Xinyi Shang,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三体散射建模（TBSM），通过分布能量诱导样本级运动，为一步生成器提供直接回归监督。在ImageNet-256上，像素空间PixelDiT-XL达到FID=2.23，潜空间DiT-XL达到FID=1.63（NFE=1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型依赖对抗性判别器、预设噪声到数据路径或自回归分解，缺乏一种能直接为一步生成器提供回归监督的分布能量方法。

Method: 将能量距离转化为恒定大小的每投射体相互作用：每个投射体被一个真实源吸引，被一个独立生成的源排斥。其条件期望等于1/2 D_E^2(P_θ,Q)的2-Wasserstein梯度流速度。通过在线追踪该条件期望降低场噪声，使用冻结图像特征中的散射训练一步生成器。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，像素空间PixelDiT-XL达到FID=2.23，潜空间DiT-XL达到FID=1.63（NFE=1）。

Conclusion: 建立了扩散相关监督、Drift-like动力学和GAN-like目标的设计映射，证明追踪散射是高维一步生成的有效途径。

Abstract: Modern generative models typically rely on an adversarial critic, a prescribed noise-to-data path, or an autoregressive factorization. Instead, we show that a proper distributional energy can induce sample-level motion and provide direct regression supervision for a one-step generator. Three-Body Scattering Modeling (TBSM) for generation turns the energy distance into a constant-size per-projectile interaction: each projectile is attracted toward one real source and repelled from one independently generated source. Conditioned on the projectile and its condition, its expectation equals the $2$-Wasserstein gradient-flow velocity of $\frac12D_E^2(P_θ,Q)$. A batch of $B$ frozen-target events yields $O(B)$ sample-level losses, each using one reference for its condition instead of the minibatch-wide all-pairs field used by methods such as Drifting Models. Tracking this conditional expectation online can reduce field noise. Using scattering in frozen image features, TBSM trains one-step generators on ImageNet-256, achieving FID${}=2.23$ with pixel-space PixelDiT-XL and FID${}=1.63$ with latent-space DiT-XL at NFE${}=1$. We provide a design map relating diffusion-related supervision, Drift-like dynamics, and GAN-like objectives. These results establish tracked scattering as a route to high-dimensional one-step generation. Code: https://github.com/sp12138/TBSM.

</details>


### [194] [Causal Discovery on Irregular Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18226)
*Martim Penim,Ricardo Ribeiro Pereira,Jacopo Bono,Hugo Ferreira,Mário A. T. Figueiredo,Pedro Bizarro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PCMCI+的扩展方法，通过聚合时间窗口内的因果影响，使得原本依赖固定时间滞后的因果发现方法能够处理不规则采样的时间序列数据，在合成不规则事件流上显著优于标准PCMCI+。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法（如PCMCI+）依赖规则且离散的滞后结构，只能处理规则采样数据，而现实中的传感器流、医疗数据和金融交易等任务往往面临不规则采样的事件流，亟需能处理不规则时间序列的因果发现方法。

Method: 扩展PCMCI+方法，将固定的时间滞后依赖关系建模改为在预定义的时间窗口内聚合因果影响，从而适应不规则采样数据。

Result: 在已知因果结构的合成不规则事件流上，在不同信噪比下均能一致地恢复底层因果图，且显著优于标准PCMCI+在不规则采样数据上的表现。

Conclusion: 通过时间窗口聚合机制有效扩展了PCMCI+，使其能够处理不规则时间序列的因果发现，在不规则采样场景下具有显著的性能优势。

Abstract: Causal discovery methods have shown strong performance in temporal systems, but they typically rely on regular and discrete lag structures, limiting their applicability to regularly sampled data. However, many real-world tasks require dealing with irregularly sampled streams of events, such as sensor streams, healthcare data, and financial transactions. In this work, we propose an extension of PCMCI+, a state-of-the-art method for causal discovery on regular multivariate time series, to allow for handling irregular time series. Instead of modelling causal relations through fixed-lag dependencies, our method aggregates causal influence over predefined temporal windows. We evaluate our method on synthetic irregular event streams with known causal structures under different signal-to-noise ratios, showing that it consistently recovers the underlying causal graph and substantially outperforms the standard PCMCI+ on irregularly sampled data.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Smoothed Two-Stage Decomposition Algorithm for Solving Large-Scale Transmission and Distribution AC-OPF Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16430)
*Juan Ospina,Manuel Garcia,Xinyi Luo,Andreas Wachter,David M. Fobes,Russell Bent*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种平滑两阶段分解优化器（StsDOpt），用于高效求解包含分布式能源的大规模输配电网AC最优潮流问题，通过主-子问题并行求解架构和光滑化技术，在不依赖近似或松弛的情况下实现了更快的求解速度和更好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式能源大量接入电网，传统将配电网视为静态负荷的输电网最优潮流方法已不再适用，而联合求解大规模、非凸的输配电网AC-OPF问题面临巨大挑战。

Method: 提出StsDOpt方法，将T&D AC-OPF问题分解为主问题-子问题结构以支持并行求解；利用光滑化技术使子问题对主问题的响应可微，结合原-对偶内点法的障碍问题特性，无需近似或松弛即可准确建模多相、不平衡、非线性的配电网系统。

Result: 集成于PowerModelsITD框架中，通过数值实验验证，该方法减少了墙钟求解时间，提升了可扩展性。

Conclusion: StsDOpt是一种鲁棒且可扩展的解决方案，能够有效解决大规模输配电网AC-OPF问题，促进分布式能源在复杂输配电网中的可靠集成。

Abstract: The integration of distributed energy resources (DERs) into the power grid has introduced new challenges to AC optimal power flow (AC-OPF) problems. Traditional OPF optimize consider transmission systems, treating distribution networks as static loads. However, the growing presence of DERs makes accurate distribution system modeling crucial for grid operations. Consequently, efficiently solving the resultant large-scale, nonconvex transmission and distribution (T&D) AC-OPF problem remains a significant challenge. This paper proposes a Smoothed Two-Stage Decomposition Optimizer (StsDOpt) to address these complexities by decomposing the T&D AC-OPF problem into a master-subproblem(s) structure, enabling parallel solving. Unlike traditional methods, StsDOpt does not rely on approximations or relaxations. It uses a smoothing technique to render the subproblems responses differentiable with respect to the master problem, leveraging the barrier problem properties inherent in primal-dual interior point methods. This approach is crucial for accurately modeling and solving distribution systems, which are multiphase, unbalanced, and nonlinear, distinguishing StsDOpt apart from other methods. Integrated into the PowerModelsITD framework, StsDOpt has been validated through numerical experiments, demonstrating reduced wall-clock solve time and increased scalability. Results highlight its efficacy as a robust, scalable solution for large-scale T&D AC-OPF problems, facilitating the reliable integration of DERs into complex T&D systems.

</details>


### [196] [Batteriesandthe BritishEnergy System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16526)
*Waqquas Bukhsh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文综述了电池在英国能源系统中的作用演变，特别是电动汽车电池的价值实现。文章指出，只有通过协调调度和更好整合到系统运营中，电动汽车电池的全部价值才能实现。无管理的充电可能给系统带来挑战，而非益处。释放这一价值需要改进市场设计、优化和控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着英国能源系统向2050年净零目标迈进，电池已成为现代能源系统的核心组成部分。作者希望审视电池角色的演变及其当前价值，特别是在交通领域的贡献，并论证只有通过协调调度和系统整合才能释放电动汽车电池的全部价值。

Method: 采用综述性视角分析，结合英国国家能源系统运营商开发的未来情景证据，对比管理充电与无管理充电对系统的影响。

Result: 电动汽车虽能提供大部分存储容量，但无管理的充电可能给系统带来挑战而非益处；释放价值需要改进市场设计、优化和控制策略。

Conclusion: 电池在英国能源系统中日益重要，电动汽车电池的全部价值需通过协调调度和系统运营整合实现，这要求市场设计、优化和控制策略的改进。

Abstract: Batteries are becoming a central part of modern energy systems, especially as electricity, transport and heat are decarbonised. In Great Britain, batteries already play an important role by providing flexibility and acting as a buffer for the system, and their importance will continue to grow as the system moves towards net zero by 2050. This perspective reviews how the role of batteries in Great Britain is evolving, their current value, and the growing contribution of batteries in the transport sector.Itarguesthatthefullvalueofelectricvehiclebatterieswillonlyberealisedthroughcoordinated dispatchandbetterintegrationintosystemoperation.Evidencefromfuturescenariosdevelopedbythe NationalEnergySystemOperatorshowsthatwhileelectricvehiclescouldprovidemostofthestorage capacity, unmanaged charging could create challenges for the system rather than benefits. Unlocking this valuewill require improvements in market design, optimisation, and control strategies.

</details>


### [197] [History-Dependent Recursive Preferences in Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16538)
*William B. Haskell*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对有限时域动态规划中具有历史依赖偏好的马尔可夫决策过程，提出了行为状态约简理论。通过公理和确定性等价条件，将全历史问题递归表示为时间与风险聚合器；构建了规范偏好增强状态（PA状态），证明其在可达递归分解中的极小性；在马尔可夫可行性等条件下，PA Bellman选择子能诱导最优全历史策略；通过矩形性和穷尽性条件，将偏好记忆重参数化为信念和品味坐标，得到分离表示与Bellman递归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限时域动态规划中，当决策者具有历史依赖偏好时，即使物理状态满足马尔可夫性，相关状态也可能是整个历史轨迹。现有方法缺乏系统性的状态约简理论来压缩这一高维状态空间，因此需要发展行为公理化的约简框架。

Method: 基于行为公理和确定性等价丰富条件，将全历史问题递归分解为时间聚合器和风险聚合器；通过商掉在共同延续计划下无差异且经共同一步扩展后仍等价的历史，构造规范偏好增强（PA）状态；进一步在矩形性和穷尽性条件下，将偏好记忆分解为信念和品味坐标。

Result: 规范PA状态是可达递归分解中的极小状态空间；PA Bellman选择子在马尔可夫可行性等条件下可诱导最优全历史策略；在额外条件下可获得分离表示的Bellman递归。

Conclusion: 本文为具有历史依赖偏好的马尔可夫决策过程提供了系统的行为状态约简理论，并通过分类示例展示了框架的广泛适用性。

Abstract: In finite horizon dynamic programming with history-dependent preferences, the relevant state may be the entire realized history, even when the physical state is Markov. This paper develops a behavioral state-reduction theory for such Markov decision processes. Under behavioral axioms and a certainty-equivalent richness condition, the full-history problem admits a recursive representation composed of time and risk aggregators. We then derive a canonical preference-augmented (PA) state by quotienting histories that have the same current physical Markov state, are indifferent under every common continuation plan, and remain equivalent after every common one-step extension. This canonical PA state is minimal among reachable recursive factorizations of the underlying preferences. Under Markov feasibility and standard dynamic-programming regularity, a PA Bellman selector induces an optimal full-history policy. With additional rectangularity and exhaustiveness conditions, we reparameterize the preference memory into distinct belief and taste coordinates, and obtain a separated representation and Bellman recursion. We give a taxonomy of examples to illustrate the scope of our framework.

</details>


### [198] [Near-Optimal Lower Bounds for Exact Zeroth-Order Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16558)
*Haihan Zhang,Chenheng Zhang,Zhiquan Qi,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文解决了零阶优化中精确函数值查询的Oracle复杂度下界问题，证明了对于非光滑L0-Lipschitz凸函数，在低精度区域（ε≥d^{-1/2}）下界为Ω(dε^{-2}/log(dε^{-2}))，在高精度区域（ε≤d^{-1/2}）下界饱和为Ω(d²/log(ed))，分别匹配了已知上界至多对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化中精确函数值访问的基本Oracle极限长期以来未被充分理解，即使是典型凸问题，维度d与精度ε的最优联合依赖关系也尚未解决。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用随机支撑函数构造困难函数族，并发展了一种针对自适应精确最大值观测的后验均值能量方法（posterior mean energy method），替代传统的一阶零链构造和基于噪声的转录不等式。

Result: 证明了对于任意自适应随机算法，最小查询次数T_ε满足下界：T_ε ≥ c·d·min{d, ε^{-2}} / log(e·min{d, ε^{-2}})。在低精度区域匹配O(dε^{-2})上界（差对数因子），在高精度区域匹配Õ(d²)上界（差多对数因子）。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了非光滑L0-Lipschitz凸函数在精确函数值查询下的Oracle复杂度问题，揭示了两个不同精度区域的下界行为，为理解零阶优化的理论极限提供了关键结果。

Abstract: The fundamental oracle limits of exact function value access remain poorly understood in zeroth-order optimization: even for canonical convex problems, the optimal joint dependence on dimension $d$ and accuracy $ε$ has remained unresolved. We resolve this question for arbitrary adaptive randomized algorithms minimizing nonsmooth $L_0$-Lipschitz convex functions over the $d$-dimensional Euclidean unit ball, where each query returns only the exact scalar value $f(\mathbf x)$ of a fixed objective. With universal $L_0>0$, let $T_ε$ denote the minimum number of queries required to return an $ε$-suboptimal point with probability at least $1/2$, uniformly over the function class. We prove that $$ T_ε\ge c\, \frac{ d\min\{d,ε^{-2}\} }{ \log\!\bigl(e\min\{d,ε^{-2}\}\bigr) }, $$ for all $d\ge d_0$ and $0<ε\leε_0$, for universal constants $c,ε_0>0$ and $d_0\in\mathbb N$. In the low-accuracy regime $ε\ge d^{-1/2}$, this matches the $O(dε^{-2})$ two-point exact value upper bound up to a logarithmic factor. In the high-accuracy regime $ε\le d^{-1/2}$, the lower bound saturates at $Ω(d^2/\log(ed))$, independently of $ε$, matching the $\widetilde O(d^2)$ evaluation oracle upper bound up to polylogarithmic factors. The proof uses a random support function hard family and develops a posterior mean energy method for adaptive exact max observations, in place of first-order zero chain constructions and noise based transcript inequalities.

</details>


### [199] [End-to-End Supply Chain Planning in the Paper Industry Via Column Generation and Benders Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16618)
*Changkun Guan,Amira Hijazi,Pascal Van Hentenryck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模造纸制造中的集成端到端规划问题，提出了一个两阶段混合框架（BDCG-DP），将列生成与精确动态规划结合Benders分解，在工业规模实例上相比传统方法降低总成本24.4%，并将求解时间从5小时以上缩短至1小时以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际生产中，生产调度、裁切决策、车辆装载和多周期履约通常被顺序优化，导致材料浪费、运输低效和服务水平下降。现有方法无法在工业规模下有效解决完全集成问题。

Method: 利用下游履约决策仅通过总供应可用性依赖于上游决策的结构特征，提出两阶段混合框架BDCG-DP：集成列生成（CG）与精确动态规划（DP）处理供应侧决策，结合Benders分解（BD）处理下游履约。

Result: 在北美大型造纸企业的专有实例上，BDCG-DP在八周规划问题上相比传统CG-DP降低总成本24.4%；四周规划问题中位数求解时间从5小时以上降至1小时以内；最复杂问题可在2.3至6小时内返回整数可行解。

Conclusion: 本文首次提出工业规模下集成生产、裁切、装载规划和多周期履约的精确模型，使规划人员能够在数小时内获得高质量可执行调度方案，此前在实际中无法实现。

Abstract: Problem definition: The paper studies an integrated end-to-end planning problem in large-scale paper manufacturing, where production scheduling, trimming decisions, vehicle loading, and multi-period fulfillment of make-to-order and make-to-stock demand must be coordinated over time. In practice, these decisions are often optimized sequentially, leading to material waste, inefficient transportation, and degraded service levels. Solving the fully integrated problem at industrial scale remains computationally challenging due to its combinatorial structure. Methodology/results: A key structural feature of the problem is that downstream fulfillment decisions depend on upstream production and logistics choices only through aggregate supply availability over time. By exploiting this structure, the paper develops an exact mathematical formulation and proposes a two-phase hybrid framework (BDCG-DP) that integrates column generation (CG) using exact dynamic-programming (DP) for supply-side decisions with Benders decomposition (BD) for downstream fulfillment. Computational experiments on proprietary instances from a major North American paper manufacturer show that BDCG-DP lowers total costs by 24.4% compared to a traditional CG-DP on challenging eight-week planning problems. Median runtime for four-week planning problems decreases from over five hours using CG-DP to under one hour using BDCG-DP. Managerial implications: This paper provides the first exact model that integrates production, trimming, load planning, and multi-period fulfillment at an industrial scale. The proposed approach returns integer-feasible plans within 2.3 to 6 hours for the most complex planning problems, enabling planners to access high-quality implementable schedules within hours, a capability that was previously unavailable in practice.

</details>


### [200] [Dynamic mean-variance portfolio selection with no-shorting constraints and unknown investment opportunity sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16625)
*Xun Li,Yutian Wang,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从强化学习视角研究禁止卖空且投资机会集未知的连续时间均值-方差投资组合选择问题。通过引入无熵辅助探索问题，证明合适探索方差下辅助问题最优高斯策略均值与原问题最优策略一致，并据此提出无需估计投资机会集的模型无关RL算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典均值-方差投资组合选择问题中，禁止卖空约束与未知投资机会集并存时，现有熵正则化探索方法因随机策略支持集约束导致高斯探索失效，理论分析困难。

Method: 引入无熵辅助探索问题，允许高斯策略样本违反禁止卖空约束但要求其均值满足约束；通过理论证明选择合适探索方差可使辅助问题最优高斯策略均值与原问题最优策略一致；基于此设计模型无关RL算法直接从轨迹数据学习。

Result: 证明了辅助问题最优高斯策略的均值与原问题最优策略等价；提出了无需估计投资机会集的模型无关RL算法；数值示例验证了算法有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入辅助探索问题巧妙规避了熵正则化框架下约束与高斯探索的冲突，为带约束的连续时间随机控制RL问题提供了新思路，且算法无需估计未知参数，具有实用价值。

Abstract: We study continuous-time mean-variance portfolio selection with no-shorting constraints and unknown investment opportunity sets from a reinforcement learning (RL) perspective. The problem is a constrained stochastic linear -- quadratic control problem for which the entropy-regularized exploratory formulation of Wang et al. (2020) leads to difficulty in theoretical analysis, because enforcing the constraint on the support of randomized policies nullifies the tractable Gaussian exploration. To tackle this challenge, we introduce an auxiliary exploratory problem without entropy in which exploratory policies are still Gaussian whose samples may violate the no-shorting requirement but their means satisfy it. We then prove that, for a suitable choice of exploration variance, the mean of the optimal Gaussian policy of the auxiliary problem coincides with the optimal policy of the original problem. Motivated by this theoretical result, we develop a model-free RL algorithm that learns the optimal policy of the auxiliary (and hence the original) problem directly from trajectory data without estimating the investment opportunity set. A numerical example demonstrates the performance of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [201] [Dynamical Optimal Transport with $\mathfrak{so}(d)$-Invariance: From Theory to Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16782)
*Kevine Meugang Toukam,Max von Renesse,Johannes Storn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种修正的Benamou-Brenier最优传输公式，该公式在动力学层面引入了欧几里得不变性，建立了Procrustes-Wasserstein距离，并在高斯情形下给出了闭合解，同时提出了一种原始-对偶数值方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传输理论中的Benamou-Brenier公式缺乏对欧几里得不变性的显式考虑，这在处理形状分析、图像配准等问题时存在局限。本文旨在通过引入动力学层面的欧几里得不变性，建立一种更具几何不变性的最优传输框架。

Method: 提出了修正Benamou-Brenier (MBB)公式，在动力学层面融入欧几里得不变性；通过变分方法证明最小元存在性并建立与静态Procrustes-Wasserstein距离的等价性；在高斯情形推导闭合表达式；设计原始-对偶离散化方案并通过抽象鞍点问题分析证明局部条件子序列收敛性。

Result: 证明了MBB公式下变分问题的最小元存在性及其与Procrustes-Wasserstein距离的等价性；高斯情形下得到闭合表达式，即协方差矩阵有序特征值平方根向量之间的欧几里得距离；数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文所提修正Benamou-Brenier公式有效融合了欧几里得不变性，理论完备且计算可行，为需要旋转等不变性的最优传输问题提供了新的理论工具和数值方法。

Abstract: We introduce a modified Benamou--Brenier (MBB) formulation of optimal transport that incorporates Euclidean invariance at the dynamical level. We establish existence of minimizers for the resulting variational problem and prove its equivalence to a static formulation defining the Procrustes--Wasserstein distance. In the Gaussian setting, we show that this distance admits a closed-form expression, reducing to the Euclidean distance between the vectors of square roots of the ordered eigenvalues of the covariance matrices. On the computational side, we formulate a primal--dual scheme for the discretized problem. We prove a local conditional subsequential convergence result through an abstract analysis of a class of parameter-dependent saddle-point problems and illustrate the method's performance numerically.

</details>


### [202] [Tight Conic Relaxations for Rank-one Doubly Nonnegative Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16796)
*Godai Azura,Sunyoung Kim,Makoto Yamashita*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究秩一双非负矩阵补全的二次约束二次规划（QCQP）的紧锥松弛，针对稀疏模式中未指定元素，证明了特定稀疏模式（循环和边）下DNN与CP松弛的对偶等价性，给出了循环型稀疏模式下SDP和DNN松弛紧的显式充分条件（基于局部比值界和累积差条件），并表明添加边可放宽紧性所需的比值条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏QCQP中，提升的矩阵变量包含未直接出现在目标或约束中的元素，需要研究这些未指定元素能否被正确恢复（即秩一补全性质），从而为锥松弛的紧性提供理论保证。

Method: 将紧性解释为未指定元素的秩一补全性质；针对由循环和边构成的块状稀疏模式，证明DNN与CP松弛的对偶等价性；针对循环型稀疏模式，推导基于局部比值界和累积差条件的显式充分条件。

Result: 证明了循环和边块状稀疏模式下DNN与CP松弛对偶等价；给出了循环型稀疏模式下SDP和DNN松弛紧的显式充分条件；发现添加边可放松紧性所需的比值条件。

Conclusion: 为锥松弛恢复秩一QCQP最优解提供了可处理的证书（tractable certificates），加深了对稀疏结构下矩阵补全问题紧松弛的理论理解。

Abstract: We study tight conic relaxations for a quadratically constrained quadratic programming (QCQP) formulation of rank-one doubly nonnegative (DNN) matrix completion. Motivated by sparse QCQPs whose lifted matrix variables include elements not directly specified by the objective or constraints, we interpret tightness as a rank-one completion property for the unspecified elements. For sparsity patterns whose blocks consist of cycles and edges, we prove that the dual formulations associated with the DNN and completely positive (CP) relaxations are equivalent. For cycle-type sparsity patterns, we derive explicit sufficient conditions under which the semidefinite programming (SDP) and DNN relaxations are tight. These sufficient conditions are stated explicitly in terms of local ratio bounds and cumulative-difference conditions on a rank-one optimal solution. We also show that adding suitable edges to the sparsity pattern relaxes the ratio conditions required for tightness. The results provide tractable certificates for when conic relaxations recover a rank-one optimal solution of the underlying QCQP.

</details>


### [203] [Benchmarking Optimization Algorithms with Quality Profiles and Test Set Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16798)
*G. Fasano,C. Piermarini,M. Roma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出两种优化算法基准测试新工具：质量剖面（quality profiles）和测试集剖面（test set profiles），前者通过确定性比较过程强调求解精度而非计算开销，后者评估测试集的适当性。同时提供MATLAB代码，并在光滑/非光滑（无导数）优化中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试方法多关注计算负担，缺乏强调求解精度的比较工具；此外，文献中对测试集本身是否适合用于算法评估关注不足，亟需一种评估测试集适当性的方法。

Method: 提出质量剖面（quality profiles），采用确定性比较过程，以目标函数值（求解质量）为核心对算法进行评估与排序；提出测试集剖面（test set profiles），用于评估测试集对所选求解器的适当性，作为算法鲁棒性评估的补充。

Result: 通过大量数值实验，在光滑优化和非光滑（无导数）优化场景中展示了质量剖面的实用性；提供了可绘制两种剖面的MATLAB代码。

Conclusion: 质量剖面与测试集剖面的联合使用能够增强基准测试过程，尤其适用于同时包含精确方法和启发式算法的比较场景；测试集剖面填补了评估测试集适当性这一被忽视的研究空白。

Abstract: We propose a couple of novel tools for benchmarking optimization algorithms which possibly converge to different solutions on a test set: the quality profiles and the test set profiles. Their aim is to assess and compare algorithms in terms of quality (i.e. value of the objective function) of the obtained solutions, as well as to assess the consistency of the test set. A key distinguishing feature of the quality profiles we propose is its comparative deterministic procedure that emphasizes the accuracy of the solution, rather than the computational burden of solvers. In this regard, several test set--dependent approaches for both comparing and ranking algorithms have already been proposed in the literature, representing widely used benchmarking procedures. We believe that the joint use of such procedures, along with the novel quality profiles detailed here, should enhance the benchmarking process, in all those cases where the comparison encompasses exact methods as well as heuristics. Moreover, the literature on numerical optimization seems to have paid less attention, in the last decade, to determining how appropriate a test set used for benchmarking the selected solvers may be. This motivates the introduction of test set profiles, which assess the appropriateness of a test set and represent the flip side of evaluating the robustness of the solvers on that test set. This paper also includes extensive numerical experiments, showing the usefulness of quality profiles in both smooth and nonsmooth (derivative--free) optimization, along with the reference to a MATLAB code for plotting quality profiles and test set profiles.

</details>


### [204] [Treasure Search Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16863)
*A. Sharma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: TSO是一种将探索与利用任务分配给两类智能体的全局优化交互粒子方法：探索者持续探索，寻宝者进行利用。通过耦合跳跃-扩散随机微分方程建模，理论证明其稳态误差为O(1/α)，并能有效规避局部陷阱，在ODE约束和贝叶斯反问题中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统群智能优化方法在同一群体中平衡探索与利用，通常通过退化噪声、退火温度或调整参数来实现两者切换，这种设计存在局限。TSO旨在通过角色分离从根本上解决探索与利用的冲突问题。

Method: TSO采用两类智能体：多个探索者（Explorers）持续探索，单个寻宝者（Hunter）进行利用。寻宝者向探索者的目标加权平均漂移，并在降低目标函数时瞬移；探索者围绕寻宝者重新聚拢，形成搜索与捕获的反馈循环。动力学建模为耦合跳跃-扩散随机微分方程，寻宝者的跳跃作为共同噪声影响所有探索者，均值场极限为条件McKean-Vlasov跳跃-扩散SDE。

Result: 证明了均值场极限SDE的适定性；通过拉普拉斯逼近技术证明寻宝者以O(1/α)误差收敛到全局最小值附近；将共识漂移与平滑自由能关联，解释了群体规避局部陷阱的机制；展示了在ODE约束问题和低维贝叶斯反问题中的有效性。

Conclusion: TSO通过探索与利用的角色分离设计，提供了一种新型全局优化框架，具有理论保证和良好实践效果，且能通过后处理卡尔曼步骤量化反问题的不确定性。

Abstract: We introduce Treasure Search Optimization (TSO), an interacting particle method for global optimization. Most swarm methods balance exploration and exploitation within a single population, and typically switch between the two by degenerating the noise, annealing a temperature, or tuning a parameter. TSO instead splits these tasks across two kinds of agents. A swarm of explorers stays in exploration mode and a single treasure hunter performs exploitation. The hunter drifts toward an objective-weighted average of the explorers and may teleport to it when the move lowers the objective. The swarm then re-centers around the hunter, creating a feedback loop between search and capture. We model the dynamics as coupled jump-diffusion stochastic differential equations (SDEs). The hunter's jumps are shared by all explorers and act as a common noise. The mean-field limit is therefore a conditional McKean-Vlasov jump-diffusion SDE, whose well-posedness we prove. We also characterize the steady state and prove, via Laplace approximation techniques, that the hunter settles near the global minimum with error of order $1/α$, where $α$ is the weight parameter. Linking the consensus drift to a smoothed free energy, we explain why the swarm ignores spurious local traps and demonstrate how to quantify uncertainty in inverse problems using post-processing Kalman steps after TSO iterations. Numerical experiments on ODE-constrained problems and a low dimensional Bayesian inverse problem demonstrate the effectiveness of the TSO method.

</details>


### [205] [A Deep Reinforcement Learning Algorithm for the Vehicle Routing Problem with Stochastic Demands and Outsourcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16875)
*Mohsen Dastpak,Fausto Errico,Ola Jabali*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带随机需求和外判选项的车辆路径问题(VRP-SDO)，采用两层级迭代方法：第一层将客户划分为自营和外判子集，第二层利用基于图注意力网络的深度Q网络作为离线学习策略，快速估计自营子集的期望路由成本。实验表明，该方法相比现有最优方法降低路由成本19.6%，相比经典启发式方法降低29.6%以上，整体算法平均节省13.7%，并在数分钟内生成高质量决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物流服务提供商面临客户需求随机性和运营成本优化的双重挑战，现有方法未能同时处理需求不确定性、外判选项和路由决策的联合优化，且计算效率低下，难以在实际中快速做出高质量决策。

Method: 两层级迭代方法：第一层使用迭代局部搜索(ILS)确定客户划分（自营vs外判）；第二层将路由问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)，采用深度Q网络(DQN)求解，其状态由图注意力网络(GAT)聚合客户与车辆信息表示。离线训练策略适用于可变客户规模和位置分布，支持在线微调。

Result: 所提策略相比现有最优方法降低路由成本19.6%，相比经典启发式方法降低29.6%以上；整体算法平均节省13.7%（相较于无注意力表示的版本）；能在数分钟内生成高质量决策，而无离线训练估计器的基准方法需超过1小时。

Conclusion: 结合离线学习策略与图注意力网络表示，能显著提升随机需求车辆路径问题的求解效率与质量，为物流服务提供商在不确定性环境下做出快速、经济的运营决策提供了有效方法。

Abstract: We introduce the vehicle routing problem with stochastic demands and outsourcing options (VRP-SDO), in which a logistics service provider partitions customer requests into customers outsourced to a common carrier and customers committed to its fixed fleet. The latter induces a vehicle routing problem with stochastic demands (VRP-SD), solved dynamically. Demands are revealed upon visit; residual demand may be served by other vehicles or after restocking at the depot. Work beyond the regular shift incurs overtime costs, and the unit outsourcing cost decreases with the expected outsourced demand. The objective is to minimize expected travel, overtime, and outsourcing costs. We propose an iterative two-level methodology whose first level partitions customers into committed and outsourced subsets, while the second level estimates the expected VRP-SD routing cost. To avoid solving this problem from scratch at every iteration, we learn an offline routing policy that estimates costs almost instantly for any committed subset. An iterated local search establishes the first-level partitions. We formulate the second level as a Markov decision process and solve it with a deep Q-network whose state is represented by a graph attention network aggregating customer and vehicle information by relevance to the acting vehicle. Trained offline on instances with variable customer cardinality and locations, the policy applies to any daily customer realization; online fine-tuning improves the cost approximation. Experiments show that our policy reduces routing costs by 19.6% relative to a state-of-the-art method and by at least 29.6% over classical heuristics. Our overall algorithm saves 13.7% on average over the version without the attention-based representation and generates high-quality decisions within minutes, whereas benchmarks without an offline-trained estimator require over an hour.

</details>


### [206] [Entropic optimal transport need not select a zero-temperature limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16881)
*Maja Gwozdz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个反例，证明在紧致度量空间、无原子概率测度和有界Lipschitz代价下，熵最优传输的最小化序列P_ε在ε→0时不存在零温极限，其聚点集构成一个非退化区间。同时给出了收敛性与聚点集的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究熵正则化最优传输问题中，当正则化参数ε趋于0时（即零温极限），最小化序列P_ε是否收敛。已有理论表明在某些条件下收敛成立，但本文旨在揭示在一般条件下收敛可能失败。

Method: 通过构造一个紧致度量空间上的具体反例，其中最优传输问题的未正则化解关于μ⊗μ是奇异的，导致最优面上的熵恒为+∞。利用Bernton、Ghosal和Nutz的聚点定理以及ε↦π_ε的连续性，证明聚点集为紧弱连通子集，并给出局部和外部的收敛判据。

Result: 构造了使P_ε不收敛的反例，聚点集为{P_w: w∈[0,1]中非退化区间}，并计算了两个显式点w^-<w^+。此外证明了在Polish空间上若C∈L^1(μ⊗ν)连续且有下界，则零温聚点集为非空弱紧连通子集位于最优面上。

Conclusion: 紧致性、无原子性和代价函数的Lipschitz正则性不足以保证零温极限的收敛性；非收敛性只能通过最优计划的一个连通连续统实现。给出了局部和外部的收敛性一阶判据。

Abstract: We construct a compact metric space with an atomless probability measure and a bounded Lipschitz cost for which the entropic optimal-transport minimisers have no zero-temperature weak limit. More precisely, $P_\varepsilon$ does not converge as $\varepsilon\downarrow0$. In the example, every unregularised minimiser is singular with respect to $μ\otimesμ$, so that the entropy on the optimal face is identically $+\infty$. We describe the cluster set by \[ \operatorname{Clust}(P_\varepsilon)=\{P_w:w\in\mathcal W\}, \] where $P_w$ is the mixture of the two zero-cost graph couplings with weight $w$, and where $\mathcal W\subset[0,1]$ is a non-degenerate compact interval. We then compute two explicit points $w^-<w^+$ in this interval. This shows that compactness, atomlessness, and Lipschitz regularity of the cost do not imply zero-temperature convergence. We also present a compactness theorem for the general problem. If $C\in L^1(μ\otimesν)$ is continuous and bounded from below on Polish spaces, then the zero-temperature cluster set is a nonempty weakly compact connected subset of the optimal face. In the proof, we apply the cluster-point theorem of Bernton, Ghosal, and Nutz and the continuity of $\varepsilon\mapstoπ_\varepsilon$. Finally, we give local and exterior first-order criteria for full convergence and cluster membership. We show that nonconvergence is possible, but only through a connected continuum of optimal plans.

</details>


### [207] [Robust Control for Marked Point Processes under Transition-Rate Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16935)
*Sascha Desmettre,Philipp C. Hornung*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限状态空间非马尔可夫标记点过程下，在累积转移率有界不确定性范围内的稳健效用最大化问题，证明了鞅最优性原理和一类非标准最坏情况倒向随机微分方程的存在唯一性，并给出了稳健消费-保险问题的显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保险和金融领域，模型参数不确定性（特别是转移率不确定性）对最优决策有重要影响，但现有研究对非马尔可夫设定下路径依赖的累积转移率不确定性的处理尚不充分。

Method: 利用鞅最优性原理和一类非标准最坏情况倒向随机微分方程（BSDE）的存在唯一性结果，在路径依赖上下界约束的累积转移率类别中求解稳健效用最大化问题。

Result: 建立了最坏情况和最好情况下的寿险与健康险准备金的存在唯一性，并得到了具有幂效用偏好的稳健消费-保险问题的显式解。

Conclusion: 本文的非马尔可夫框架扩展了稳健保险决策理论，为处理路径依赖的转移率不确定性提供了严格的数学基础和解的显式形式。

Abstract: We consider a novel robust utility maximisation problem under bounded cumulative transition rate uncertainty within the class of non-Markovian marked point processes on a finite state-space. Utility is maximised over the class of admissible controls, while Nature chooses a worst-case biometric scenario from the class of admissible, path-dependent cumulative transition rates restricted by path-dependent upper and lower bounds. We prove a martingale optimality principle and a novel existence and uniqueness result for a non-standard worst-case backwards stochastic differential equation, which allows us to establish existence and uniqueness of worst-case and best-case prospective reserves of life and health insurance contracts with reserve-dependent payments. Finally, we find an explicit solution of a novel robust consumption-insurance problem with power utility preferences.

</details>


### [208] [Optimal Scheduling for Remote State Estimation over Hybrid Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16950)
*Manali Dutta,Rahul Singh,Shalabh Bhatnagar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个远程状态估计系统中的最优调度问题，该系统包含两个异构通信信道：快速不可靠（建模为Gilbert-Elliott信道）和慢速可靠。传感器根据估计误差相对于状态依赖阈值选择信道，问题被建模为连续状态空间的MDP，并证明最优策略具有阈值结构。针对参数未知情况，提出了一种利用该阈值结构的actor-critic学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际远程状态估计系统中，通信信道往往具有不同的延迟和可靠性特性，且信道丢包存在时间相关性（如突发丢包）。现有研究较少同时考虑异构信道特性和时间相关丢包模型下的最优调度策略，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将调度问题建模为连续状态空间的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），以无限 horizon 平均代价（估计误差平方+传输能耗）为优化目标。通过理论分析证明最优平稳策略的存在性，并刻画其阈值结构——策略取决于估计误差是否超过与GE信道状态相关的阈值。针对参数未知场景，提出利用阈值结构的actor-critic强化学习算法。

Result: 理论证明了最优策略的阈值结构：传感器根据估计误差与GE信道状态依赖的阈值比较来选择信道。数值实验表明，所提actor-critic算法能有效学习策略结构，其性能接近基于相对值迭代（RVI）计算的最优策略。

Conclusion: 本文成功将异构信道和时间相关丢包纳入远程状态估计调度框架，揭示了最优策略的阈值结构，并为参数未知场景提供了有效的学习算法，对实际通信系统设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: We study optimal scheduling for remote state estimation over a network with two heterogeneous communication channels: a fast but unreliable channel and a slow but reliable channel. To capture temporal correlations in packet losses, we model the unreliable channel as a Gilbert-Elliott (GE) channel. The remote estimation setup consists of a source, a sensor, and a remote estimator. The source evolves as a discrete-time autoregressive (AR) process, and the sensor decides at each time whether to use the fast unreliable channel or the slow reliable channel. We formulate the scheduling problem faced by the sensor as a Markov decision process (MDP) with a continuous state-space and consider minimizing the infinite horizon average cost criterion, where the cost consists of the squared estimation error and the transmission energy consumed. We establish the existence of an optimal stationary policy. We then characterize the structure of an optimal policy, and show that it has a threshold structure with respect to the estimation error. An optimal policy chooses from amongst the two channels based on whether the error exceeds certain thresholds, where the threshold value depends upon the GE channel state. When the system parameters are unknown, we propose an actor-critic (AC) learning algorithm that exploits the threshold structure of an optimal policy. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed AC algorithm learns the policy structure effectively and achieves performance close to that of the optimal policy computed using the relative value iteration (RVI).

</details>


### [209] [Structural Vibration Control of Offshore Wind Turbines Using Tuned Mass Damper Inerter in OpenFAST: Implementation, Validation, and Illustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16962)
*Hisham Tariq,Yuan Li,Flavia De Luca,Agathoklis Giaralis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过在OpenFAST中实现调谐质量阻尼惯性器（TMDI），扩展了其在风电机组多方向振动控制中的应用。研究验证了实现的准确性，并以IEA 15MW风机为例，证明TMDI在质量仅为传统TMD百分之一时仍能达到或优于其减振效果，且行程更小，适用于空间受限的风机环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明TMDI在风电机组中有效，但依赖简化结构模型，且尚未有研究在气动-水动-伺服-弹性耦合工具（如OpenFAST）中实现多方向TMDI，以支持塔筒、叶片和基础子结构等部件的独立振动控制。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在OpenFAST的结构控制（StC）模块中推导并实现TMDI的运动方程，支持任意连接方式和独立多方向配置。通过与独立数值模型对比验证准确性。以IEA 15MW风机为例，采用简化二自由度模型进行最优调谐，模态参数从OpenSeesPy有限元模型中提取。

Result: 质量仅为传统TMD百分之一时，TMDI在塔顶位移和加速度的峰值与标准差上达到或优于传统TMD的减振效果，且TMDI行程显著减小。

Conclusion: 轻量化TMDI在空间受限的风电机组振动控制中具有实用价值，OpenFAST实现为真实载荷条件下的性能驱动设计提供了工具支持。

Abstract: The tuned mass damper inerter (TMDI) is a passive vibration absorber that suppresses structural vibrations by leveraging the inertance property of inerter devices. Previous studies have demonstrated TMDI effectiveness in wind turbines, but relied on simplified structural models. To date, no study has implemented multi-directional TMDIs within aero-hydro-servo-elastic tools such as OpenFAST to enable independent vibration control across multiple turbine components, including the tower, blades, and substructure. This paper addresses this gap by implementing the TMDI in OpenFAST. The governing equations of motion are derived and implemented within the Structural Control (StC) Module, supporting arbitrary TMDI connectivity and independent multi-directional configurations across turbine components. Verification against independent numerical models confirms the implementation's accuracy across all supported configurations. An application study examines the IEA 15MW reference wind turbine equipped with a tower-top TMDI under combined wind-wave loading. Optimal tuning is performed using a simplified two-degree-of-freedom model, with modal properties extracted from an OpenSeesPy finite element model of the IEA 15MW. Results show that TMDIs with secondary mass up to 100 times smaller than a conventional TMD achieve matching or superior vibration suppression in the fore-aft and side-side directions, in terms of peak and standard deviation of tower-top displacement and acceleration. Notably, TMDI stroke is markedly reduced compared to the TMD, better suiting the spatially constrained wind turbine environment. These results support the practical merit of lightweight TMDIs for wind turbine vibration control, with the OpenFAST implementation enabling performance-driven design under realistic loading conditions.

</details>


### [210] [Relative Entropy-Bounded Ambiguous Chance Constraints for Robust Planning in Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16977)
*Trevor N. Wolf,Jay W. McMahon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对随机控制问题中的分布模糊性，提出了一种基于相对熵距离的分布模糊集定义方法，通过指数积分的变分表达式，在未知分布位于名义高斯参考分布的相对熵距离内时，对期望风险值进行边界约束，并应用于非线性协方差引导问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机会约束控制方法通常假设真实状态分布已知且服从高斯分布，这在许多实际工程应用中（如非线性系统动力学）不成立，需要处理分布模糊性。

Method: 1) 定义分布模糊集；2) 利用指数积分的变分表达式，在未知分布位于名义高斯参考分布的相对熵距离内时，对期望风险值进行边界约束；3) 提出确定相对熵距离的方法，该距离是参考协方差演化和二阶动力学截断误差的函数。

Result: 所提出的边界在零散度极限下恢复参考风险值；在随机航天器制导示例中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究为非线性协方差引导问题中处理分布模糊性提供了一个理论框架。

Abstract: We consider defining risk probability in stochastic control problems under distribution ambiguity. Current approaches for chance-constrained control typically assume that the true state distribution is known and Gaussian distributed. These assumptions are not amenable to many real-world engineering applications where system dynamics are nonlinear and only approximately modeled. In this work, we define a distribution ambiguity set and, with a variational expression for exponential integrals, bound the expected risk value under an unknown distribution that resides within a relative entropy distance of a nominal Gaussian reference distribution. Our bound recovers the reference risk value in the zero-divergence limit. A method is presented to determine the relative entropy distance defining the ambiguity set that is a function of the reference covariance evolution and second-order dynamical truncation errors. The resulting contributions provide a framework for handling distributional ambiguity in nonlinear covariance steering problems. A stochastic spacecraft guidance example is presented to demonstrate our contributions.

</details>


### [211] [Real-World, Large Scale, Multi-Period Log Truck Routing and Scheduling : Application to Canadian Forestry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16978)
*Abdelhakim Abdellaoui,Issmail El Hallaoui,Loubna Benabbou,François Aubé,Mouloud Amazouz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对林业多阶段原木运输车辆路径问题（LTRSP），提出了一种改进的混合整数线性规划（MILP）精确公式化，并采用基于Relax&Fix和Fix&Optimize的元启发式分解策略进行求解。在加拿大林业公司历史数据上的实验表明，该方法能在实际计算时间内获得接近最优的结果，显著降低运输成本和温室气体排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 林业运输成本占总运营成本的三分之一以上，而林业车辆路径问题长期以来缺乏能够整合全部运营约束的精确且可扩展的公式化方法，这是林业运筹学领域一个长期悬而未决的开放问题。

Method: 首先分析林业特有的业务规则并构建反映实际运营的路网，然后提出一个包含所有已知林业运营约束的改进MILP公式化，并对时间离散化等关键建模选择进行详细论证；最后将最先进的求解器与基于Relax&Fix和Fix&Optimize的元启发式分解策略相结合，以解决大规模多周期实例的组合复杂性。

Result: 在加拿大林业公司历史数据上的计算实验表明，该方法能在实际计算时间内获得接近最优的结果，实现了显著的财务收益和温室气体排放减少。

Conclusion: 该研究成功回答了林业车辆路径问题中如何建模并求解包含全范围运营约束的精确公式化这一长期开放问题，为林业运输优化提供了可扩展的实用解决方案，兼具经济与环境效益。

Abstract: This paper addresses the multi-period log-truck routing and scheduling problem ($\mathcal{LTRSP}$), a key operational activity in the forestry industry, where transportation accounts for more than one-third of total operational costs. The Canadian forestry sector faces significant logistical difficulties driven by vast geographic distances, seasonal variability, volatile markets, and environmental considerations. Our research tackles a long-standing open question in the forestry operations literature, namely the absence of an exact and scalable formulation of the forestry vehicle routing problem integrating the full range of operational constraints \cite{ronnqvist2015operations}: \textit{How can we model and solve an exact formulation of the forestry VRP problem?} In response, we analyze business rules specific to the forestry sector to construct a routing network reflecting real operational practices, and then propose a comprehensive improved mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation incorporating all known forestry operational constraints, with a detailed justification of key modeling choices such as time discretization, along with a decomposition-based solution methodology tailored for large-scale, multi-period industrial instances. To address the inherent combinatorial complexity, we combine state-of-the-art solvers with a metaheuristic decomposition strategy based on \textit{Relax\&Fix} and \textit{Fix\&Optimize}. Computational experiments on historical data from a Canadian forest company demonstrate near-optimal results within practical computation times, with significant financial gains and reduced greenhouse gas emissions.

</details>


### [212] [Robust Chance-Constrained Optimization using a Continuous Parameter Space Wasserstein-2 Ambiguity Set of Gaussian Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17018)
*Shibshankar Dey,Sanjay Mehrotra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Bures-Wasserstein度量的分布鲁棒机会约束优化框架，用于处理高斯混合模型下的不确定性。与传统有限支撑分布鲁棒方法不同，该方法允许最坏情况分布内生决定混合分量的数量、均值与协方差，并设计了自适应切割曲面算法保证有限步收敛。在电动汽车充电站能量分配案例中，该方法能实现任意可靠性目标，并诱导出与名义解不同的结构性能量分配变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限支撑分布鲁棒（FDR）方法仅对经验混合支撑点进行鲁棒化，主要检验名义混合的拟合质量，当服务可靠性依赖于混合支撑参数的结构性误设定时，这种处理方式并不充分。因此需要一种更灵活的模糊集描述方式，允许最坏情况分布内生确定混合分量的位置与数量。

Method: 1) 利用Bures-Wasserstein度量构建Wasserstein-2模糊集，该度量定义在具有有限二阶矩的概率测度上；2) 推导内层最坏情况机会约束问题的强对偶性及其半无限重构；3) 设计自适应切割曲面算法，内生产生混合分量位置及高斯分布的均值与协方差，并采用块交替局部搜索发现新分量。

Result: 算法在有限次迭代内达到任意预设最优性间隙。在电动汽车充电站能量分配案例中，提出的连续分布鲁棒（CDR）方法能够实现任意可靠性目标，且其能量分配方案与名义解存在结构性差异，而FDR方法则保持接近名义解。

Conclusion: 所提方法突破了传统有限支撑分布鲁棒优化中支撑点预置的限制，提供了更灵活且理论上严谨的分布鲁棒机会约束求解框架，在实际应用中具有更优的可靠性与适应性。

Abstract: We study distributionally robust linear chance-constrained problems in which uncertainty is modeled by a Gaussian mixture model (GMM). Finite-support distributionally robust (FDR) formulations, widely used in data-driven robust optimization, robustify over empirical mixture support points and therefore primarily stress-test the fitted nominal mixture. This can be insufficient when service reliability depends on structural misspecification of the nominal mixture-support parameters. To address this limitation, we describe the ambiguity set of distributions by developing a novel formulation of a Wasserstein-2 metric that uses the Bures-Wasserstein (BW) metric over probability measures with finite second moments. Unlike FDR, which generally sets finitely many empirical support points a priori, the proposed ambiguity set allows the worst-case distribution to endogenously determine both how many mixture components receive mass and where their means and covariances lie within a continuous support. For the resulting ambiguity set, under mild regularity conditions, we prove strong duality for the inner worst-case chance-constraint problem and derive its semi-infinite reformulation. We then develop an adaptive cutting-surface algorithm, which endogenously determines the locations of mixture components receiving mass, and the mean and covariances of the Gaussian distributions at these locations. The algorithm attains any prescribed optimality gap in finitely many iterations, while a block-alternating local search identifies new components. A case study using the electric-vehicle charging-station energy-allocation problem demonstrates the framework's practical value in achieving any reliability targets. CDR also induces structural changes in energy allocations, unlike FDR, whose allocations remain close to the nominal solution.

</details>


### [213] [Optimal Combinatorial Testing with Constraints: The Balancing Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17083)
*Thiago Serra,Changkun Guan,Hunter Gehman,Sumit Dhar,Mikey Ferguson,John Hooker,Marcel Schoppers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了二元参数组合测试中的成对覆盖问题，对比了无约束和有约束两种情况，提出了基于整数规划的最优算法和更快的启发式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际软件测试中，穷举所有配置组合不可行，但大多数bug仅由少数组件交互引起，因此成对覆盖测试是有效方法。然而，当存在约束（禁止某些成对组合）时，问题从易解变为NP难，需要高效的求解方法。

Method: 对比分析无约束与有约束二元参数成对覆盖测试的性质差异；提出基于整数规划（Integer Programming）的精确算法，以及一种更快的启发式算法。

Result: 精确算法能求得最优解，启发式算法通常也能产生最优解，两者均优于或可与基线方法竞争。

Conclusion: 在有约束情况下，保持参数平衡（即按钮开/关次数相近）仍是好策略，有时甚至是最优的。提出的算法有效解决了约束组合测试的优化问题。

Abstract: Imagine that you are in front of a cockpit with several on-off buttons. If you were to thoroughly test it, you would need to try a prohibitive number of configurations. But since most bugs in practice can be isolated to interactions among few components, having tests that cover every possible pairwise configuration is a good start. However, this is a problem that goes from easy to NP-hard as soon as some pairwise configurations are forbidden. In this paper, we revisit unconstrained combinatorial testing with pairwise coverage on binary parameters and contrast it with the constrained case, showing and conjecturing properties that either are upheld or change from one to the other. In particular, we discuss the extent to which it remains a good idea -- and sometimes indeed optimal -- to have every button almost as many times on as off to minimize testing. We propose the first exact algorithm based on integer programming and a faster heuristic that often produces optimal solutions, both outperforming or competitive with their baselines.

</details>


### [214] [Grid-Interactive Operation of Solar-Integrated Data Centers for Coordinated Local and System-Level Decarbonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17089)
*Mingxu Yang,Weiqi Zhang,Hui Geng,Jiaze Ma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种滚动时域优化框架，发现数据中心自建太阳能虽然提升了能源自给率并降低了自身排放，但会限制其从电网吸收低价低碳电力的能力，揭示了企业可持续目标与电网整体脱碳之间的根本矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估数据中心自建太阳能发电策略的真实影响，探究企业级可持续目标与电网级脱碳之间可能存在的冲突。

Method: 提出了一个滚动时域优化（RHO）框架，协调独立数据中心的作业调度、电网交互和现场太阳能发电。

Result: 太阳能集成提高了能源自给率并降低了数据中心整体排放，但同时也限制了数据中心吸收电网中低成本、低碳电力的能力，存在一个关键悖论。

Conclusion: 企业个体可持续目标与系统级电网脱碳之间存在根本性张力，单纯依靠自建可再生能源可能并非最优策略。

Abstract: The exponential growth of AI is accelerating the deployment of data centers (DCs), placing unprecedented strain on power infrastructures. In response, major IT corporations are increasingly adopting on-site solar generation to reduce grid dependence and meet sustainability targets.
