However, the true impacts of this strategy remain ambiguous. While DCs are flexible assets capable of temporal load-shifting, anchoring them to self-generated power may inadvertently constrain their grid responsiveness. To evaluate these trade-offs, we propose a receding-horizon optimization (RHO) framework coordinating job scheduling, grid interactions, and on-site solar generation for a stand-alone DC. Our findings reveal a critical paradox: although solar integration increases energy self-sufficiency and reduces overall DC emissions, it inherently limits the facility's capacity to absorb low-cost, low-carbon electricity from the grid. This implies a fundamental tension between individual corporate sustainability goals and system-wide grid decarbonization.

</details>


### [215] [Dynamic Pricing for a Two-Sided Data Market Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17119)
*Lijun Bo,Dongfang Yang,Yijie Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据平台在连续时间动态定价问题中，如何向隐私敏感的数据提供者购买原始数据并向消费者销售数据产品，通过控制收购价格和销售价格，并建模数据积累与市场参与之间的反馈机制，最终推导出HJB方程并证明了价值函数的粘性解存在性和最优反馈策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据平台在运营中面临双边市场定价问题，尤其是数据供给方（提供者）对隐私敏感，数据积累与市场参与之间存在相互影响的动态反馈机制，现有研究对此类连续时间动态定价问题的理论分析尚不充分。

Method: 采用连续时间随机控制框架，将提供者和消费者的到达过程建模为依赖于当前数据存量的点过程，数据状态过程为跳-扩散过程，推导非线性积分-微分HJB方程，利用粘性解理论证明值函数的存在唯一性，并在适当条件下建立经典正则性，验证最优反馈定价策略。

Result: 证明了价值函数是HJB方程的唯一粘性解，在适当条件下具有经典正则性，并验证了最优反馈定价策略的存在性。数值分析揭示了模型参数对最优定价策略的影响。

Conclusion: 本文为数据平台在双边市场中考虑数据积累反馈效应的动态定价问题提供了严格的理论框架和求解方法，数值结果可为平台定价实践提供指导。

Abstract: We study a continuous-time dynamic pricing problem for a data platform that purchases raw data from privacy-sensitive providers and sells data products to consumers. The platform controls both the acquisition price offered to providers and the selling price charged to consumers. Provider and consumer arrivals are modeled by point processes whose intensities depend on the platform's current data stock, capturing feedback between data accumulation and market participation. We formulate the platform's problem as an infinite-horizon stochastic control problem with a jump-diffusion state process and derive the associated nonlinear integro-differential HJB equation. We prove that the value function is the unique viscosity solution, establish classical regularity under suitable conditions, and verify the optimal feedback pricing policy. Finally, we conduct numerical analyses to examine the influences of model parameters on the optimal pricing policies.

</details>


### [216] [A Parallel Evolutionary Algorithm Framework for Graph $k$-CUT Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17158)
*Sihong Shao,Chuan Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的并行进化算法框架（PEAF），用于解决两类图划分问题（MaxGCP和MinGCP），通过结构继承交叉算子、分层变异机制和多样性保持选择策略，在G-set数据集上对九个代表性k-CUT问题取得了优于Gurobi的性能，并揭示不同模型之间的结构关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图k-CUT问题的算法大多针对单一模型设计，缺乏跨模型的可迁移性；需要一种统一框架来高效求解多种相关图划分问题。

Method: 将图划分问题分为MaxGCP和MinGCP两类；提出PEAF框架，包含结构继承交叉算子、基于多重变异启发式（MMH）和辅助割变异启发式（ACMH）的分层变异机制，以及多样性保持选择策略。

Result: 在G-set上对k∈{2,3,4,5}的九个k-CUT问题中，PEAF-ACMH一致优于Gurobi；对于MaxGCP，改进了Max-k-Cut（k≥3）的多个最优解；揭示了不同模型间的结构亲缘性和平衡性差异。

Conclusion: PEAF既是统一的高效求解器，也是揭示图k-CUT模型结构性质的有用工具。

Abstract: Graph k-CUT problems include many important variants whose objectives combine cut value, volume, and cardinality terms in different ways. Most existing algorithms are designed for individual formulations, which limits their transferability across related models. In this paper, we organize a broad family of graph partitioning problems into two classes, MaxGCP and MinGCP, according to their optimization orientation and balance-related structure. Based on this classification, we propose a unified Parallel Evolutionary Algorithm Framework (PEAF). This framework combines structure-inheriting crossover operators, a hierarchical mutation mechanism based on the Multiple Mutation Heuristic (MMH) and the Auxiliary Cut Mutation Heuristic (ACMH), and a diversity-preserving selection strategy. Extensive experiments on G-set with k \in\{2, 3, 4, 5\} show that PEAF-ACMH consistently outperforms Gurobi on nine representative k-CUT problems. For MaxGCP, PEAF-ACMH improves several best-known solutions for Max-k-Cut with k \geq 3, and through numerical bounds derived from its relation to Max-k-Cut, verifies the high quality of the obtained solutions for Judicious-k-Partition and AntiCheeger-k-Cut. The results further indicate that Judicious-k-Partition usually yields more balanced partitions than AntiCheeger-k-Cut. For MinGCP, theoretical and computational comparisons show that Cheeger-k-Cut and Sparsest-k-Cut produce more balanced partitions than Normalized-k-Cut and Ratio-k-Cut, respectively. PEAF-ACMH also obtains highly similar partitions for Min-k-Cut and MinMax-k-Cut within short running times, providing numerical evidence for their structural affinity. These results demonstrate that PEAF is both an effective unified solver and a useful tool for revealing structural properties of graph k-CUT models.

</details>


### [217] [A Principal-Agent Mean-Field Game Model of Insurance with Risk Interdependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17214)
*Asaf Cohen,Ruolan He,Mingyan Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究道德风险、内生参与和战略风险相互依赖下的保险合同设计问题，通过异质平均场博弈近似N-agent博弈，证明了低层平均场纳什均衡的存在性与唯一性，并将其嵌入 insurer 的上层Stackelberg优化问题，最终扩展到有限合同菜单并提供数值证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N-agent博弈存在维度灾难，难以直接求解；需要有效方法处理代理人之间的战略风险相互依赖以及内生参与决策问题。

Method: 采用异质平均场博弈近似战略互动；通过可测选择论证和Kakutani不动点定理证明低层平均场纳什均衡存在性；利用L1-Lipschitz连续性和压缩映射证明均衡唯一性；将平均场响应嵌入上层Stackelberg优化问题，通过一般性能包络处理潜在多重均衡；扩展到有限合同菜单。

Result: 证明了上层ε-最优合同的存在性，在唯一性条件下证明了精确Stackelberg均衡的存在性；数值实验表明多合同筛选在相互依赖风险环境下能提高委托人的期望收益。

Conclusion: 提出的异质平均场博弈框架能有效处理战略风险相互依赖下的保险合同设计问题，多合同筛选机制优于单一合同。

Abstract: We study an insurance contract-design problem under moral hazard, endogenous participation, and strategic risk interdependence. Because the resulting $N$-agent game suffers from the curse of dimensionality, we approximate the strategic interactions via a heterogeneous mean-field game. We rigorously establish the existence of a lower-level mean-field Nash equilibrium using measurable selection arguments and the Kakutani fixed-point theorem. By proving the $L^1$-Lipschitz continuity of the aggregate participation threshold, we further establish equilibrium uniqueness via a contraction mapping. We then embed this mean-field response into the insurer's upper-level Stackelberg optimization problem. We formulate the objective through general performance envelopes to accommodate potential equilibrium multiplicity, proving the existence of upper-level $\varepsilon$-optimal contracts, and demonstrating the existence of an exact Stackelberg equilibrium under the uniqueness regime. We conclude by extending the model to finite contract menus, providing numerical evidence that multi-contract screening improves the principal's expected payoff in interdependent risk environments.

</details>


### [218] [Uniform Exponential Stability Analysis of Impulsive Linear Time-Invariant Systems on Banach and Hilbert Spaces: Non-Coercive and Coercive Stability Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17260)
*Corentin Briat,Francesco Ferrante,Christophe Prieur*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Banach或Hilbert空间上无穷维脉冲系统的一致指数稳定性，通过结合混合系统理论和无穷维系统方法，得到了基于算子的稳定性条件，并给出了固定脉冲序列及各类驻留时间下的充要条件。线性切换系统作为特例被重新表述为脉冲系统，并继承了相应的稳定性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无穷维脉冲系统的传播算子是时变的演化族，这使得稳定性分析变得困难。现有研究缺乏针对这类系统在Banach和Hilbert空间上的统一稳定性分析框架。

Method: 结合混合系统理论与无穷维系统方法，利用非强制和强制Lyapunov泛函，提出基于算子的稳定性条件，并通过凸规划进行解析或数值检验。

Result: 得到了固定脉冲序列以及任意、常数、最小和区间驻留时间下的一致指数稳定性的充要条件；并将结果推广到Hilbert空间上的二次Lyapunov泛函情形；证明了线性切换系统可精确地重新表述为脉冲系统的特例。

Conclusion: 所提方法为无穷维脉冲系统的稳定性分析提供了统一的理论框架，且线性切换系统作为特例自然继承相关稳定性条件。理论分析和数值示例验证了方法的有效性，尤其适用于时滞系统的采样数据控制。

Abstract: We consider the uniform exponential stability analysis of infinite-dimensional impulsive systems defined on a Banach or Hilbert space, whose flow is governed by a fixed $C_0$-semigroup generator and whose jumps occur at a prescribed time sequence. While the flow and jump maps are themselves time-invariant, the time-triggered impulses render the propagator a genuinely time-varying evolution family, which is the source of the analysis difficulty addressed here. We combine ideas from hybrid systems theory and infinite-dimensional systems to produce operator-based stability conditions, which can be analytically or numerically checked via convex programming. Necessary and sufficient conditions for the uniform exponential stability of impulsive systems on Banach spaces are obtained in the context of a fixed impulse-times sequence but also of arbitrary, constant, minimum, and range dwell-times using both non-coercive and coercive Lyapunov functionals. Some of those results are then adapted to systems on a Hilbert space and quadratic Lyapunov functionals. As an application, linear switched systems are shown to be an exact special case: reformulated as impulsive systems with unit-norm selector jumps, they inherit non-coercive and clock-dependent dwell-time stability conditions on both Banach and Hilbert spaces. Theoretical and numerical examples are given for illustration, notably on the sampled-data control of time-delay systems.

</details>


### [219] [Norm Minimisation Problems Involving Distances to Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17276)
*Doan Huu Hieu,Nguyen Duy Cuong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究涉及凸集距离的积空间范数最小化问题，利用凸分析和泛函分析工具建立了完全的对偶必要充分最优性条件，并证明整个解集可由给定解处的对偶向量构造。进而研究了Fermat-Torricelli问题、Chebyshev中心问题及p-Fermat-Torricelli问题的推广版本的最优性条件和解集描述，并通过有限维和无限维空间中的实例进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决涉及凸集距离的积空间范数最小化问题中缺乏完整对偶最优性条件和解集构造方法的空白，为广义Fermat-Torricelli问题、Chebyshev中心问题等经典问题的推广版本提供统一的理论框架。

Method: 运用凸分析和泛函分析的标准工具，建立对偶必要充分最优性条件；通过给定解处的对偶向量构造整个解集；将理论结果应用于广义Fermat-Torricelli问题、Chebyshev中心问题及p-Fermat-Torricelli问题的分析。

Result: 给出了完整对偶必要充分最优性条件；证明了从单一解处的对偶向量可构造整个解集；为多个经典问题的推广版本提供了最优性条件和解集描述；通过有限维和无限维空间中不同范数下的实例验证了结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 建立的框架统一并推广了涉及凸集距离的积空间范数最小化问题的最优性理论，为相关优化问题的求解和解集刻画提供了系统方法。

Abstract: The paper studies product-space norm minimisation problems involving distances to convex sets. Using standard tools from convex and functional analysis, we establish complete dual necessary and sufficient optimality conditions and show that the entire solution set can be constructed from the dual vectors arising from the optimality conditions at a given solution. As a consequence, we study optimality conditions and solution set descriptions for generalised versions of the Fermat-Torricelli problem, the Chebyshev centre problem, and the p-Fermat-Torricelli problem. Comparisons with existing results are provided whenever applicable. Examples in finite and infinite dimensional spaces equipped with different norms are presented to illustrate the results.

</details>


### [220] [Solving Highly Constrained Multi-Objective Decision Problems with the IMAP-IGS Preference-Guided Metaheuristic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17304)
*L. J. K. Timp,N. Yorke-Smith,A. R. M Wolfert*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出IMAP框架及其求解器IMAP-IGS，将利益相关者偏好聚合直接嵌入进化算法的适应度函数中，以解决高度约束下多目标优化中偏好冲突问题。在DAS-CMOP、MO-VRPTW和HVASP基准测试中，IMAP在偏好冲突时显著优于加权和及基于Pareto的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度约束的多目标设计和决策问题因严格的可行性要求和冲突的利益相关者偏好而难以求解。现有进化算法将约束处理与偏好优化分离，基于Pareto的方法需要后处理选择偏好解，而标量方法会扭曲异构偏好。

Method: 提出IMAP框架，将偏好聚合直接嵌入进化算法的适应度函数中。设计约束违反偏好函数引导搜索朝向可行域，最终得分仅反映利益相关者偏好。提出IMAP-IGS求解器。实现两个变体：IMAP-BRKGA（组合优化）和IMAP-GA-II（连续优化）。

Result: 在DAS-CMOP、MO-VRPTW和HVASP基准测试中，当利益相关者偏好冲突时，IMAP始终优于加权和及基于Pareto的方法；当偏好一致时，优势基本消失。

Conclusion: IMAP的优势在于解决高度约束决策环境中的偏好冲突，而非偏好一致时的优化问题。

Abstract: Highly constrained multi-objective design and decision problems are difficult to solve because of strict feasibility requirements and conflicting stakeholder preferences. Evolutionary algorithms are widely used for these problems but typically separate constraint handling from preference optimisation. Pareto-based methods provide a set of trade-off solutions but require post-processing to select a preferred option, while scalarisation can distort heterogeneous stakeholder preferences. This paper introduces the Integrative Maximisation of Aggregated Preferences (IMAP) and its Inter-Generational Solver (IMAP-IGS), which embed preference aggregation directly into the evolutionary fitness function. A constraint-violation preference function guides the search toward feasibility, while final scores reflect only stakeholder preferences. The framework can be integrated into any evolutionary algorithm that uses scalar fitness evaluation. Two implementations are presented: IMAP-BRKGA for combinatorial optimisation and IMAP-GA-II for continuous optimisation. Testing on DAS-CMOP, MO-VRPTW, and HVASP benchmarks shows that IMAP consistently outperforms weighted-sum and Pareto-based approaches when stakeholder preferences conflict. These advantages largely disappear when preferences align, indicating that IMAP's strength lies in resolving preference conflicts in highly constrained decision environments.

</details>


### [221] [From a Scalar to a Matrix Setting for the Dai--Liao Parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17337)
*Saman Babaie--Kafaki,Morteza Kimiaei,Zohre Aminifard*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过均值不等式强化了Dai-Liao共轭梯度算法中两个经典自适应参数的最优性，并将其从标量形式扩展为矩阵形式，证明了著名的三项共轭梯度算法是该扩展类的一员。新方法具有更好的搜索方向矩阵条件数和更充分的二阶信息利用能力，数值实验验证了其理论合理性和计算可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dai-Liao共轭梯度算法的数值性能和理论性质高度依赖于其关键参数的调整，此前提出的两个标量自适应设置虽然有效，但其最优性缺乏严格的理论支撑。此外，标量参数形式限制了算法的灵活性和多样性，需要向更一般的矩阵形式拓展。

Method: 1) 利用调和-几何-算术-平方(Harmonic-Geometric-Arithmetic-Quadratic)均值不等式，证明两个经典Dai-Liao自适应参数通过缩小搜索方向矩阵奇异值区间来增强矩阵良定性；2) 对标量记忆less拟牛顿更新公式中的经典缩放参数进行类似分析；3) 将Dai-Liao参数从标量推广到矩阵形式，证明一个著名的三项共轭梯度算法是该扩展Dai-Liao类算法的特例；4) 在标准基准测试问题上进行数值实验验证。

Result: 1) 从理论层面严格证明了两类经典自适应参数的最优性；2) 建立了矩阵形式的Dai-Liao参数框架，将三项共轭梯度算法纳入该统一框架；3) 矩阵形式下的搜索方向矩阵具有良好的条件数，且能更显式地利用目标函数的二阶信息；4) 数值实验表明所提框架兼具理论健全性和计算可靠性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Dai-Liao参数矩阵化框架在理论上能够保障搜索方向矩阵的良定性并充分利用二阶信息，在实践上具有良好的数值表现，是对传统标量Dai-Liao方法的重要推广和改进。

Abstract: As is well known, both the numerical performance and the theoretical properties of the Dai--Liao conjugate gradient algorithm are highly dependent on the adjustment of its key parameter. Here, we first employ the well-known Harmonic--Geometric--Arithmetic--Quadratic mean inequality to reinforce the optimality of two previously proposed scalar adaptive settings of the Dai--Liao parameter. In other words, we show how these parameter choices are capable of enhancing the well-conditioning of the Dai--Liao search direction matrix by shrinking the intervals containing its singular values. A similar analysis is also carried out for scaled memoryless quasi--Newton updating formulas in order to further justify the optimality of two classical scaling parameters associated with these updates. Then, as the main contribution of this work, we move from the classical scalar setting of the Dai--Liao parameter to a matrix setting aimed at enhancing flexibility and diversity within optimization methods. In particular, we show that, under such a matrix formulation of the parameter, a well-known three-term conjugate gradient algorithm emerges as a member of the proposed extended Dai--Liao class of algorithms while enjoying several computationally attractive properties. Among these, the well-conditioning of the associated search direction matrix is especially noteworthy, as well as the ability to make more explicit use of the second-order information of the model. Finally, to provide practical evidence supporting the proposed matrix setting of the Dai--Liao parameter, we conduct a series of numerical experiments on standard benchmark test problems and report the results in detail. Generally speaking, the proposed framework is shown to retain both theoretical soundness and computational reliability.

</details>


### [222] [Supply Chain Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17491)
*Elioth Sanabria*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个量化框架，用于分析一般供应链网络中的系统性需求不确定性和风险传播级联。通过将随机网络嵌入报童模型，证明牛鞭效应是协调物流网络固有的拓扑属性，并利用Skorokhod反射问题建模库存消耗周期。通过引入非线性价格弹性机制和动态贸易关系再平衡，展示了分散理性行为与物理产能瓶颈如何共同加速系统性网络退化。最后通过全球石油贸易数据实验，验证了局部瓶颈冲击（如霍尔木兹海峡）如何引发非线性级联缺货和主权缓冲的重新配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将牛鞭效应归因于信息不对称或运营噪声，缺乏从网络拓扑本质属性角度理解系统性需求不确定性和风险传播的理论框架。此外，缺乏一个能同时刻画均衡态和瞬态、并内生化市场出清反馈的量化分析工具。

Method: 将随机网络嵌入报童模型构建多层级网络模型，分别分析均衡态和瞬态运营体制；将库存消耗周期建模为多维Skorokhod反射问题；引入非线性价格弹性机制和动态贸易关系再平衡来内生化市场出清反馈回路；通过全球石油贸易数据驱动的数值实验进行实证验证。

Result: 数学验证了牛鞭效应是协调物流网络不可逃避的固有拓扑属性，独立于运营噪声或信息可见性约束；局部瓶颈冲击（如霍尔木兹海峡产能冲击）会引发非线性级联缺货和系统性储备重新配置。

Conclusion: 系统性风险传播是供应链网络拓扑结构的固有属性，即使消除信息不对称也无法避免；局部冲击可通过网络拓扑结构非线性放大为全局系统性危机，需从网络层面设计韧性机制。

Abstract: This study provides a quantitative framework for analysis of systemic demand uncertainty and risk propagation cascades across general supply chain networks. By leveraging properties derived from stochastic networks embedded within a Newsvendor paradigm, we model multi-echelon networks under equilibrium and transient operational regimes. We mathematically validate that the systemic volatility behavior commonly referred to as the Bullwhip effect persists entirely as an unavoidable, inherent topological property of coordinated logistics networks, independent of traditional operational noise or information visibility constraints. Extending this paradigm to transient environments, we model inventory drawdown horizons as a multi-dimensional Skorokhod reflection problem. Crucially, we endogenize market-clearing feedback loops by incorporating non-linear price elasticity mechanisms and dynamic trade relation rebalancing, demonstrating how decentralized rational actions co-evolve with physical capacity bottlenecks to accelerate systemic network degradation. Finally, we operationalize the framework through a data-driven numerical experiment mapping global oil trade dynamics, showing how localized chokepoint disruptions, such as a capacity shock in the Strait of Hormuz, trigger non-linear cascading stockouts and systemic reallocation across sovereign buffers over time.

</details>


### [223] [CADMM-Prox: A Bi-level Consensus ADMM for Non-smooth Non-convex Distributed Consensus Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17495)
*Xu Du,Shuting Wu,Karl H. Johansson,Apostolos I. Rikos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非光滑非凸分布式优化问题，提出了一种双层一致性ADMM算法CADMM-Prox，通过引入近端机制保证全局收敛到Clarke稳定点邻域，并在相位恢复问题中验证了更稳定的收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习和信号处理等领域中广泛存在非光滑非凸优化问题，现有分布式优化算法在此类问题上的收敛性保障不足，亟需更稳定的求解方法。

Method: 提出CADMM-Prox算法，将经典一致性ADMM与近端机制结合，通过引入与外层变量相关的足够大的近端项，在局部目标函数满足半凸假设的条件下实现收敛性保证。

Result: CADMM-Prox能在半凸假设下全局收敛到Clarke稳定点的邻域；在相位恢复问题上的数值实验表明，该方法相比基线算法具有更稳定的收敛行为。

Conclusion: CADMM-Prox通过近端机制有效解决了非光滑非凸分布式优化中的收敛稳定性问题，为相关领域提供了理论保证更强的优化工具。

Abstract: Non-smooth and non-convex optimization problems are pervasive in machine learning, control, and signal processing, due to the need for sparse solutions and the inherently non-convex nature of many objective functions. In this paper, we study non-smooth and non-convex distributed optimization problems. We propose a novel bi-level Consensus Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) algorithm, termed CADMM-Prox. The proposed algorithm integrates classical Consensus ADMM with a proximal mechanism by introducing a sufficiently large proximal term associated with an outer-level variable. Under the mild assumption that the local objective functions are semi-convex, CADMM-Prox is guaranteed to converge globally to a neighborhood of a Clarke stationary point. Numerical experiments on a phase retrieval problem demonstrate that our proposed method exhibits more stable convergence behavior compared with baseline algorithm.

</details>


### [224] [Consumption and Investment in Incomplete Markets with Epstein-Zin Preferences in Infinite Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17557)
*Ho Man Tai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了不完整市场中最大化Epstein-Zin随机微分效用的最优消费-投资问题，通过有界域HJB方程解的极限刻画最优策略，并在三种市场模型中进行了数值实验，部分参数下得到了显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不完整市场（incomplete markets）中，投资者面临不可对冲的随机风险，传统方法难以直接求解。本文旨在为Epstein-Zin型随机微分效用下的最优消费-投资问题提供理论刻画和数值解法。

Method: 采用有界域中精心设计边界条件的HJB方程序列解的极限来逼近最优策略，并针对三种市场模型进行数值实验。

Result: 验证了最优策略可由有界域HJB方程解的极限刻画，并在特定参数条件下构造了显式解。

Conclusion: 本文提供的不完整市场下Epstein-Zin效用最优消费-投资问题的解法具有理论有效性和数值可行性。

Abstract: We solve the optimal consumption-investment problem in incomplete markets, where the investor aims to maximise an Epstein-Zin type stochastic differential utility from consumption over an infinite time horizon. We verify that the optimal strategies can be characterised by the limit of a sequence of solutions of the HJB equation in bounded domains with carefully designed boundary conditions. We also conduct numerical experiments for three market models. Explicit solutions are constructed under some parameter regimes.

</details>


### [225] [Monotonicity and Frank-Wolfe Dynamics in Atomic Splittable Congestion Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17684)
*Tobias Harks*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了原子可分拥堵博弈中的普适单调性和Frank-Wolfe稳定性，通过曲率条件刻画了变分不等式算子的单调性（包括严格和强单调性），并发现相同的曲率条件也决定了欧几里得正则化Frank-Wolfe动力学的局部与全局稳定性，此外还分析了单纯形博弈中内点均衡的局部指数稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为原子可分拥堵博弈建立普适单调性和Frank-Wolfe稳定性的理论刻画，揭示成本函数曲率与学习动力学稳定性之间的深层联系。

Method: 通过曲率不等式（涉及成本函数的前两阶导数和玩家数量）来表征变分不等式算子的单调性；利用相同的曲率条件分析正则化Frank-Wolfe动力学的稳定性；针对单纯形博弈进行特殊情形分析。

Result: 给出了普适单调性、严格单调性和强单调性的精确刻画；发现了单调性曲率条件与Frank-Wolfe动力学稳定性之间的等价关系；证明了在单纯形博弈中，即使不满足曲率条件，内点均衡仍是局部指数稳定的。

Conclusion: 原子可分拥堵博弈的成本函数曲率条件同时决定了变分不等式算子的单调性和正则化Frank-Wolfe动力学的稳定性，揭示了博弈论与学习动力学之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We study universal monotonicity and Frank--Wolfe stability properties for atomic splittable congestion games. Specifically, we characterize the largest resource cost class for which the associated variational inequality operator is monotone for every game. This characterization is given by a curvature inequality involving the first two derivatives of the allowable cost functions and the number of players. Our framework yields exact characterizations for universal monotonicity, strict monotonicity, and strong monotonicity; the strict and strong variants require corresponding stricter curvature conditions. We then draw a perhaps surprising connection to learning dynamics in atomic splittable congestion games. We show that the very same curvature condition also characterizes universal \emph{local and global stability} of the Euclidean-regularized Frank--Wolfe dynamics on arbitrary convex strategy spaces, provided the cost class is closed under positive affine transformations. Finally, we study games on simplices and show that an interior equilibrium of the regularized Frank--Wolfe dynamic is locally exponentially stable, even without the curvature condition.

</details>


### [226] [Limiting Stationarity of Regularized Gap-Function Reformulations for Bilevel Optimization with Unbounded Multipliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17772)
*Xiaoning Bai,Shangzhi Zeng,Jin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对双层优化中值函数型约束的退化问题（不满足标准约束规范），本文证明了正则化间隙函数重构的近似稳定点收敛到MPCC的C-稳定点（即使乘子序列无界），并提出了基于松弛的惩罚方法以保证M-稳定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 值函数型重构方法广泛用于双层优化，但其对应的值函数型约束本质上是退化的，不满足标准约束规范，导致乘子序列可能无界，从而基于有界乘子的收敛分析不适用。

Method: 1. 针对下层为约束凸规划的双层问题，采用正则化间隙函数重构；2. 证明近似稳定点的聚点为MPCC的C-稳定点（无需间隙函数约束的约束规范）；3. 提出基于松弛的两参数惩罚公式，保证精确乘子-松弛互补性；4. 设计非精确松弛惩罚方法，含自适应惩罚更新和可行性校正。

Result: 1. 在上下层约束系统的MFCQ和极限点的MPCC-MFCQ下，近似稳定点聚点为C-稳定点；2. 给出示例表明标准正则化间隙函数重构的近似稳定点可收敛到C-稳定点但非M-稳定点；3. 所提松弛惩罚方法在支配条件下保证M-稳定点。

Conclusion: 解决了值函数型约束退化导致的乘子无界问题，提供了无需间隙函数约束规范的收敛性分析，并通过松弛惩罚方法实现了更强的M-稳定点收敛保证。

Abstract: Value-function-type reformulations have generated a broad class of methods for bilevel optimization. However, the corresponding value-function-type constraints are inherently degenerate and generally fail to satisfy standard constraint qualifications, so the associated multiplier sequences may be unbounded and bounded-multiplier convergence analyses become inapplicable. We study this issue for the regularized gap-function reformulation of bilevel problems with constrained convex lower-level programs. We prove that accumulation points of approximate stationary sequences are C-stationary for the corresponding Karush-Kuhn-Tucker-based mathematical program with complementarity constraints (MPCC), even when the multiplier sequence associated with the regularized gap-function constraint is unbounded. The result holds under Mangasarian-Fromovitz constraint qualification (MFCQ) for the upper- and lower-level constraint systems and MPCC-MFCQ at the limiting MPCC point, without any constraint qualification on the regularized gap-function constraint itself. We further provide an example showing that approximate stationary points of the standard regularized gap-function reformulation may converge to a point that is C-stationary but not M-stationary. To guarantee M-stationarity, we introduce a slack-based two-parameter penalty formulation preserving exact multiplier-slack complementarity and establish M-stationarity under a domination condition on the penalty parameters. We develop an inexact slack-penalty method with adaptive penalty updates and feasibility correction, whose accumulation points are M-stationary under the stated assumptions.

</details>


### [227] [Thermal management optimization of Battery Electric Vehicles via hierarchical NMPC with CMO-trained Neural Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17892)
*Francesco Ripa,Diego Regruto,Pasquale Ceres,Christopher Strano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层NMPC框架，用于BEV热管理，通过数据驱动神经网络模型实现能耗与舒适性的平衡，在WLTC循环中相比工业规则式策略显著降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV续航里程受限，而热管理（座舱空调与电池热管理）是主要能耗来源之一，现有控制策略难以在能耗、乘客舒适度和电池热安全之间实现最优权衡。

Method: 提出分层NMPC框架：上层NMPC在长时域内权衡能耗与座舱温度跟踪，生成HVAC和BTM系统的最优作动器指令序列；下层NMPC引入局部修正以补偿扰动和模型不确定性，跟踪上层指定的座舱温度参考轨迹。两层均采用数据驱动神经网络模型作为预测模型。

Result: 在高保真BEV热仿真器（都灵理工与Stellantis合作开发）上，基于WLTC循环验证，所提方法相比工业规则式基线策略显著降低了能耗，同时满足舒适度和电池温度约束。

Conclusion: 分层NMPC框架结合数据驱动预测模型能有效降低BEV热管理能耗，是一种有前景的替代传统规则式控制的方法。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of energy-efficient thermal management in Battery Electric Vehicles (BEVs), with the goal of extending driving range while ensuring passenger comfort and battery thermal safety. A hierarchical Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) framework is proposed, in which the supervisory layer explicitly balances energy consumption and cabin temperature reference tracking over a long horizon and translates this trade-off into optimal actuator command sequences for the integrated heating, ventilation, and air conditioning (HVAC) system and the battery thermal management (BTM) system. These command sequences are then enforced by a lower-layer NMPC, which introduces local corrections to compensate disturbances and modeling uncertainty while tracking an externally specified cabin temperature reference trajectory. To enable predictive control of the highly nonlinear and strongly coupled thermal dynamics, data-driven neural network models are employed as prediction models within both control layers. The proposed approach is developed and validated using a high-fidelity BEV thermal simulator developed at Politecnico di Torino in collaboration with Stellantis N.V., and benchmarked against an industrial rule-based (RB) control strategy over the WLTC driving cycle. Simulation results demonstrate a significant reduction in energy consumption compared to the baseline strategy, while satisfying comfort and battery temperature constraints.

</details>


### [228] [Finding Fair Draws for Incomplete Round Robin Tournaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17901)
*Sten Wessel,Frits Spieksma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了不完全循环赛中因赛程差异导致的公平性问题，分析了在不同约束条件下（分组、协会归属、连通性要求等）是否存在公平抽签的复杂性，并通过实验展示了如何生成公平的不完全循环赛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不完全循环赛中，各队面对的对手集合不同，若忽略队伍实力差异，会产生公平性问题。本文旨在从计算复杂性角度探讨公平抽签的存在性及其条件。

Method: 通过计算复杂性理论分析不同场景下公平抽签的存在性问题，并结合实验方法生成公平的不完全循环赛。

Result: 确定了在多种约束（如分组、协会归属、连通性要求等）下判断公平抽签是否存在问题的计算复杂性；实验证明了生成公平不完全循环赛的可行性。

Conclusion: 不完全循环赛的公平性受多种因素约束，其存在性问题的计算复杂度因场景而异，且可通过实验方法生成公平的赛程。

Abstract: In an incomplete round robin tournament, teams play against different sets of opponents. Given varying strengths of the teams, this raises a fairness issue. We establish the complexity of determining whether a fair draw exists under various scenarios involving the presence of pots (i.e., strength based groups of teams), teams coming from the same association, connectivity requirements, and others. We also show experimentally how fair incomplete round robin tournaments can be generated.

</details>


### [229] [Reflected Schrodinger Bridge Problem over Sub-Riemannian Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17904)
*Daniel Owusu Adu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了反射亚黎曼薛定谔桥问题，针对有界域内欠驱动、退化扩散且具有硬状态约束的概率分布最小能量输运。通过引入与亚黎曼结构相容的内禀斜反射机制，在Hörmander和非特征边界假设下证明了退化参考过程存在光滑严格正转移密度。最优控制由带非对称边界条件的前向-后向PDE系统刻画，并发展了基于PDE的Sinkhorn迭代算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有薛定谔桥问题研究多针对完全可控系统或非退化扩散，而欠驱动系统（控制方向少于状态维度）中扩散退化且存在有界域硬约束时，标准的欧几里得法向反射会破坏水平子丛结构，导致反射方向不在容许控制与噪声场生成的子空间中。同时，参考测度可能不具有全支撑，使得传统方法失效。

Method: (1) 提出与亚黎曼结构相容的内禀斜反射机制替代欧几里得法向反射；(2) 在Hörmander条件与非特征边界假设下，证明退化参考过程存在光滑严格正转移密度；(3) 导出带非对称边界条件的前向-后向PDE系统（后向因子为斜Neumann条件，前向因子为法向无通量条件）；(4) 由于显式转移密度不可得，发展直接施加边界条件的基于PDE的Sinkhorn迭代算法。

Result: 建立了反射亚黎曼薛定谔桥问题的理论框架：证明了退化参考过程转移密度的光滑性与严格正性；推导了最优控制所满足的带非对称边界条件的FPB PDE系统；给出了基于PDE的Sinkhorn迭代求解方法，并提供了示例验证。

Conclusion: 本文成功将薛定谔桥问题推广到亚黎曼（欠驱动退化扩散）且有界域反射约束的设定，所提出的斜反射机制与PDE求解框架为解决此类几何退化最优输运问题提供了理论工具和数值方法。

Abstract: We formulate and solve the reflected sub-Riemannian Schrödinger bridge problem: minimum-energy transport of probability distributions on a bounded domain for underactuated dynamics with degenerate diffusion and hard state constraints. The main difficulties are geometric and degeneracy: Euclidean normal reflection is generally incompatible with the horizontal subbundle, since it may push the process in directions not generated by the admissible control and noise fields. Also, the reference measure may not have full support. We address these by introducing an intrinsic oblique reflection mechanism that is compatible to the sub-Riemannian structure. Under Hörmander and non-characteristic boundary assumptions, we prove that the reflected degenerate reference process admits a smooth, strictly positive transition density. The resulting optimal control is characterized by a forward--backward PDE system with asymmetric boundary conditions: an oblique Neumann condition for the backward factor and a normal no-flux condition for the forward factor. Since explicit transition densities are generally not available in this setting, we develop a PDE-based Sinkhorn iteration that enforces these boundary conditions directly. We illustrate the framework with an example.

</details>


### [230] [A Curvature-Aware Rank-Adaptive Distributed Augmented-Lagrangian Solver for Large-Scale SDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17933)
*Hongpei Li,Huikang Liu,Dongdong Ge,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: CARDAL是一种基于曲率感知的自适应秩分布式增广拉格朗日多GPU求解器，用于大规模半定规划，在Mittelmann基准测试中展现出比现有低秩GPU方法更强的鲁棒性，并在多GPU上实现最高4倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模半定规划（SDP）在实际应用中计算代价高昂，现有低秩GPU方法在鲁棒性和扩展性方面存在不足，亟需一种兼具理论保证和高效分布式实现的求解器。

Method: 采用自适应秩的Burer-Monteiro分解与增广拉格朗日方法；固定秩时使用带负曲率修正的矩阵自由L-BFGS方法逼近近似欧拉二阶稳定点；通过反向乘子移位将负对偶松弛方向转化为精确负曲率；设计小规模联合秩提升问题选择批量低秩修正；采用约束×秩×锥体设备网格的三轴分布式策略，通过设备局部操作和轴向上集体通信实现可扩展执行。

Result: 在Mittelmann基准测试中比现有低秩GPU方法具有更强鲁棒性；在机器人学、电子结构和Max-Cut的大规模SDP松弛实验中，三轴分布展现出互补的扩展区间，四块H100 GPU上观测到最高4倍墙钟加速。

Conclusion: CARDAL通过曲率感知的自适应秩策略和高效的三轴分布式架构，在理论保证和实际性能上均超越了现有低秩GPU求解器，为大尺度SDP问题的分布式求解提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We present CARDAL (Curvature-Aware Rank-Adaptive Distributed Augmented Lagrangian), a distributed multi-GPU solver for large-scale semidefinite programs (SDPs) based on a rank-adaptive Burer-Monteiro factorization and an augmented Lagrangian method. At fixed ranks, a matrix-free L-BFGS method with negative-curvature corrections targets an approximate Euclidean second-order stationary point of the factored augmented Lagrangian. A reverse multiplier shift turns a negative dual-slack direction into exact negative curvature after rank expansion, and a small joint rank-lift problem selects a batched low-rank correction. A verified slack lower bound provides an a posteriori approximate KKT certificate. Our analysis establishes generic global-optimality guarantees for heterogeneous products of PSD cones at per-block ranks near the Barvinok-Pataki scale, together with a finite-accuracy counterpart under blockwise cost smoothing. For scalable execution, CARDAL distributes constraint rows, factor columns, and PSD blocks over a Constraint x Rank x Cone device mesh. The primal residual, gradient, Hessian-vector products, and slack matrix-vector products are evaluated using device-local operations and axis-wise collectives. On the Mittelmann benchmark, CARDAL exhibits stronger robustness than existing low-rank GPU approaches under a uniform accuracy standard. Experiments on large-scale SDP relaxations from robotics, electronic structure, and Max-Cut demonstrate the complementary scaling regimes of the three distribution axes, with observed wall-clock speedups of up to 4x on four H100 GPUs.

</details>


### [231] [Long-time behavior and turnpike properties of linear-quadratic graphon mean field control problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18000)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Zhongyuan Cao,Jiamin Jian*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性二次型图均场控制（GMFC）问题的渐近行为和转向性质，在可稳定性和正定性条件下，建立了遍历控制问题的唯一可解性，推导了有限时域系统到其稳态极限的指数收敛估计，并证明了最优对的指数转向性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究图均场控制问题中有限时域与无限时域（遍历）最优控制之间的渐近关系，特别是转向性质（turnpike property）在异质交互网络系统中的成立性。

Method: 将有限时域和遍历GMFC问题分别刻画为Hilbert空间上的Riccati方程系统及广义微分/代数方程系统，在可稳定化条件和图诱导算子正定性假设下，通过分析方程解的指数收敛性建立结果。

Result: 建立了遍历控制问题的唯一可解性；获得了有限时域系统向其稳态极限的指数收敛估计；证明了最优对（控制和状态）的指数转向性质；证明了有限时域GMFC问题的时间平均价值函数收敛性。

Conclusion: 在线性二次型图均场控制框架下，当系统满足可稳定化和正定性条件时，有限时域最优控制/状态轨迹指数收敛至遍历稳态解，即转向性质成立，且时间平均价值函数收敛。

Abstract: We investigate the asymptotic behavior and turnpike properties of graphon mean field control (GMFC) problems in the linear-quadratic setting. We consider both a finite-horizon GMFC problem and its associated ergodic counterpart, in which the controlled dynamics are governed by a graphon mean field stochastic differential equation with heterogeneous interactions. The optimal controls and state trajectories for both problems are characterized by systems of Riccati equations together with systems of generalized differential and algebraic equations on suitable Hilbert spaces. Under a stabilizability condition and appropriate positivity assumptions on the graphon-induced operators, we establish the unique solvability of the ergodic control problem and derive exponential convergence estimates for the finite-horizon system to its stationary limit. As a consequence, we establish an exponential turnpike property for the optimal pair and prove the convergence of the time-averaged value function for the finite-horizon GMFC problem.

</details>


### [232] [Feedback Cycles in Exploratory Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18128)
*Chen-Hung Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间不一致随机控制中熵正则化导致的均衡策略灵敏度放大问题，发现低温下Gibbs响应会放大奖励和动力学误差，通过后向Volterra-抛物型预解式、块分解和路径-循环区分机制，揭示了温度、模型参数与估计误差之间的非线性过渡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间不一致随机控制中，熵正则化虽能平滑均衡策略，但在低温下同一Gibbs响应会强烈放大学习到的奖励和动力学中的误差，理解这种误差放大的机制对于鲁棒控制和学习至关重要。

Method: 利用后向Volterra-抛物型预解式控制探索性均衡的导数；通过对齐正模式建立指数阶下界；通过块分解区分因果路径（贡献1/tau的幂律）和正反馈循环（可产生指数增长）；利用局部均衡分支关于有限维模型参数的二次可微性导出函数值delta方法；在有界一致椭圆扩散中实现路径-循环区分；最后通过仿射模型在Lambert-W温度处给出精确非线性过渡。

Result: 闭合一个正循环将根n线性响应边界从幂律变为1/log n阶；沿循环Perron模式，右端点离散化在N tau^2趋于无穷时相对一致；仿射模型在beta T / W(beta T sqrt(n))处出现精确非线性过渡。

Conclusion: 路径和循环在误差放大中起截然不同的作用：因果路径贡献温和的幂律增长，而正反馈循环可导致指数级放大；温度、样本量和模型参数之间存在复杂的非线性过渡关系，需要谨慎选择正则化参数以保证估计的可靠性。

Abstract: Entropy regularization smooths equilibrium policies in time-inconsistent stochastic control. At low temperature, the same Gibbs response can strongly amplify errors in learned rewards and dynamics. We show that the derivative of an exploratory equilibrium is governed by a backward Volterra-parabolic resolvent. Along an aligned positive mode, a lower bound has the same exponential order. A block decomposition identifies the source of the amplification: causal paths contribute powers of 1/tau, whereas a positive feedback cycle can produce exponential growth.
  At fixed temperature, a local equilibrium branch is twice differentiable with respect to finite-dimensional model parameters, which yields a function-valued delta method. A bounded uniformly elliptic diffusion realizes this path-cycle distinction in every finite dimension. Closing one positive cycle changes the root-n linear-response boundary from a power law to order 1/log n; along the cyclic Perron mode, right-endpoint discretization is relatively consistent exactly when N tau^2 -> infinity. An affine model also gives an exact nonlinear transition at the Lambert-W temperature beta T / W(beta T sqrt(n)). Numerical calculations illustrate these rates.

</details>


### [233] [Improved Convergence Rate for Stochastic Multi-Gradient Descent: A Proof Discovered with AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18174)
*Lisha Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为光滑非凸随机多目标优化问题中的随机多梯度下降（SMG）算法建立了新的收敛速率：在恒定步长和线性增长的mini-batch下，输出点的平方Pareto平稳性（PS）测度达到$\widetilde O(T^{-1})$，显著优于此前Chen等人（2024）的$\widetilde O(T^{-1/4})$。关键改进在于利用PS测度的Lipschitz连续性替代了MGDA方向的(1/2)-Hölder连续性。该证明最初由ChatGPT 5.4 Thinking Extended生成，作者随后验证并重组了论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对光滑非凸随机多目标优化问题，现有SMG算法的收敛速率（$\widetilde O(T^{-1/4})$）存在提升空间，需要建立更紧的收敛界以更好地指导算法实践。

Method: 利用Pareto平稳性（PS）测度的Lipschitz连续性（定义为MGDA方向的范数），替代前人工作中使用的(1/2)-Hölder连续性假设；在恒定步长和线性增长mini-batch的设置下重新推导SMG的收敛分析。

Result: SMG算法输出点的平方PS测度在T次迭代后达到$\widetilde O(T^{-1})$，相比Chen等人(2024)的$\widetilde O(T^{-1/4})$实现了阶数级提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入更强的连续性假设（Lipschitz而非Hölder），可以显著改进SMG算法的理论收敛速率；同时记录了该证明由AI辅助生成的过程，反映了人机协作在数学证明中的新范式。

Abstract: For smooth nonconvex stochastic multi-objective problems, stochastic multi-gradient descent (SMG) computes an approximate steepest common descent direction of the objectives from stochastic gradients. With unbiased, variance-bounded stochastic gradients, this note establishes a new convergence rate for SMG in terms of the squared Pareto-stationarity (PS) measure. With a constant stepsize and linearly growing mini-batches, this measure at the algorithm's output is $\widetilde O(T^{-1})$ after $T$ iterations. This improves on the $\widetilde O(T^{-1/4})$ bound obtained by Chen et al. (2024) under the same setting, where $\widetilde O(\cdot)$ suppresses logarithmic factors. The key to the rate improvement is to exploit the Lipschitz continuity of the PS measure, defined by the norm of the multi-gradient descent algorithm (MGDA) direction, rather than the $(1/2)$-Hölder continuity of the MGDA direction used by Chen et al. (2024). The proof was discovered while the author was preparing homework for a graduate course: ChatGPT 5.4 Thinking Extended generated the initial proof strategy in response to an author-written homework-solution prompt; the author then verified and reorganized the resulting argument. The appendices document the prompt and summarize the student submissions.

</details>


### [234] [Risk-sensitive exit-time control for stochastic differential equations with path-dependent coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18192)
*David Criens,Fabian Fuchs*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了由路径依赖随机微分方程驱动的风险敏感退出时间控制问题的小噪声渐近性，建立了对数变换后的退出时间问题收敛到一个具有路径依赖系数的确定性控制问题，并给出了可计算示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究路径依赖型随机微分方程在风险敏感框架下的退出时间控制问题的小噪声渐近行为，填补该领域在路径依赖情形下的理论空白。

Method: 首先推导路径依赖系数下一般对数变换随机控制问题的变分表示，结合路径依赖偏微分方程理论和路径空间上的凸期望工具；其次使用概率方法分析变分公式的收敛性。

Result: 建立了对数变换后的退出时间问题收敛到具有路径依赖系数的确定性控制问题，并通过带记忆的随机微分方程示例刻画了极限问题及相应控制策略。

Conclusion: 成功将风险敏感退出时间控制问题的小噪声极限推广到路径依赖情形，提供了理论框架和可计算示例，为后续应用奠定基础。

Abstract: In this work, we study small-noise asymptotics of risk-sensitive exit-time control problems governed by stochastic differential equations with path-dependent coefficients. Our main result establishes the convergence of the $\log$-transformed exit-time problem to a deterministic control problem with path-dependent coefficients. For its proof, we first derive a novel variational representation for general $\log$-transformed stochastic control problems with path-dependent coefficients, combining tools from the theory of path-dependent partial differential equations and convex expectations on path spaces. In a second step, we use probabilistic methods to analyze the convergence of the resulting variational formulas. To illustrate the scope of our analysis, we consider a computable example for a stochastic differential equation with memory and characterize the limiting problem and associated control strategies.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [Rater State Bias in RLHF Preference Data: An Audit Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16195)
*Elena Kopteva,Vitaliy Hlynianyi-Zhuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文识别了RLHF中一种结构化混杂因素：标注者的状态漂移（如压力、情绪变化）会影响偏好标签，导致偏好数据不仅反映输出质量，还编码了标注者的状态信息。这种偏差不同于随机噪声，可能通过奖励建模和策略优化传播。作者提出了假设、审计框架、可验证预测及实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF偏好数据假设标签仅反映输出质量优劣，但忽略了标注者在标注过程中的状态变化（如压力、情绪）可能系统性地影响偏好判断，从而在奖励模型中引入结构化偏差。

Method: 定义了标注者状态漂移、标注者状态混杂、相关标注者状态偏差等概念；提出了情感真实性生存水平（survival level emotional authenticity）作为可测量响应模式，涵盖词汇、语用、话语和安全相关特征；推导出五个可证伪预测和效应量阈值；设计了适用于公开指令微调模型的审计协议和初步研究方案。

Result: 提出相关标注者状态偏差能够在数据聚合后存活并进入学习到的奖励信号；给出了效应量阈值和可证伪预测，为后续实证验证提供了具体检验标准。

Conclusion: 标注者状态漂移是RLHF偏好数据中一种合理且可检验的结构化偏差来源，需要被识别和审计以提升RLHF的可靠性。

Abstract: We identify a structured confound in Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF). Pairwise preference labels are intended to reflect the compared outputs, but they may also reflect the rater's state during annotation. Under sustained stressful or distressing conditions, raters' preferences may shift over time. As a result, preference data can encode rater state alongside judgments about response quality. These shifts differ from ordinary disagreement or random label noise. They are state dependent, can be shared across annotators working under similar conditions, and can propagate through reward modeling and policy optimization. We therefore propose rater state shift as a plausible and testable source of structured bias in RLHF preference data. This paper develops a hypothesis and an audit framework for studying this source of bias. We define rater state shift, rater state confound, and correlated rater state bias. We also define survival level emotional authenticity as a measurable response pattern using lexical, pragmatic, discourse, and safety related features. We analyze how correlated rater state bias can survive aggregation and enter learned reward signals. We derive five falsifiable predictions and effect size thresholds for an initial audit. Finally, we present an audit protocol and pilot study plan that can be applied to publicly available instruction tuned models. We do not infer the training history of any specific deployed model. Our goal is to isolate a plausible and testable source of structured bias in RLHF preference data.

</details>


### [236] [Design and Validation of a Lightweight 1D CNN for Affective Touch Classification in Soft Plush Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16196)
*Aleksandrs Vališevskis,Aleksandrs Okss,Inese Tīģere,Aleksejs Kataševs,Dina Bethere,Anete Hofmane,Airisa Šteinberga,Undīne Gavriļenko,Santa Meļķe,Lucie Matoušková*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套完整的开源MATLAB框架，用于开发轻量级深度学习模型以实现软体交互伴侣中的情感触觉识别。研究公开了一个包含1326个标注手势序列的FAIR兼容数据集，并通过468个CNN模型的系统探索，确定了13.2k参数的紧凑型扩张一维CNN为最优方案，在微控制器上可实现20 Hz实时运行。最终提出了启发式阈值过滤与CNN精细分类相结合的混合推理管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体传感器化伴侣虽然提供物理安全和情感直观的交互界面，但其可变形性和多通道触觉传感使得情感触觉的鲁棒解读变得复杂，需要轻量级且有效的计算解决方案。

Method: 构建了FAIR兼容的1326个标注手势序列数据集（25名参与者，涵盖儿童、青少年和成人）；系统探索了468个CNN模型的架构与超参数，确定了扩张一维CNN；设计了启发式阈值过滤与CNN精细分类相结合的混合推理管线；进行了理论推理时间分析和PC实时仿真验证。

Result: 13.2k参数模型在测试集上达75%准确率，留一法交叉验证达85%准确率；量化部署每窗口需3.2 MMAC，可在目标微控制器上实现20 Hz实时运行；CNN能识别之前启发式系统无法检测的微妙社交触觉，而高力度负面交互由阈值逻辑更可靠捕获。

Conclusion: 具有情感意义且保护隐私的触觉解读在计算上可行，可直接嵌入软体治疗伴侣中；混合推理管线被提出作为嵌入式部署策略，硬件集成将在后续研究中解决。

Abstract: Soft, sensorized companions offer a physically safe and emotionally intuitive interface for socially assistive technologies, yet their deformability and multichannel tactile sensing complicate the robust interpretation of human affect. This study presents a complete open-source MATLAB-based framework for the development and validation of compact deep learning models for affective touch recognition in soft interactive companions. As a primary contribution, a diverse FAIR-compliant dataset of 1326 labelled gesture sequences collected from 25 participants spanning children, teenagers, and adults is made publicly available, providing a reusable resource for future research in affective touch recognition. Through systematic architecture and hyperparameter exploration across 468 CNN models, the study identifies compact dilated one-dimensional convolutional neural networks (1D CNNs) as the most effective solution, with a 13.2k-parameter model achieving 75% test accuracy and 85% mean leave-one-subject-out cross-validation accuracy. Theoretical inference-time analysis shows that quantized deployment requires 3.2 MMAC per window, compatible with 20 Hz real-time operation on the target microcontroller. PC-based real-time simulation with the physical toy streaming sensor data demonstrates that the CNN resolves subtle social touches that the previous heuristic system failed to detect, whereas high-force negative interactions are captured more reliably by trivial threshold-based logic. The resulting hybrid inference pipeline - instantaneous heuristic filtering followed by CNN-based nuanced gesture classification - is proposed as the embedded deployment strategy. The study demonstrates that emotionally meaningful, privacy-preserving touch interpretation is computationally feasible for direct embedding within soft therapeutic companions, with hardware integration addressed in a forthcoming study.

</details>


### [237] [Some Large Language Models Exhibit Consistent Risk Attitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16197)
*Bowen Sun,Rui Min,Yuxi Wang,Brian Odegaard,Qi Wang,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个跨领域框架，将上下文风险信念与类别决策解耦，测试了六个大语言模型（LLM）和100名人类参与者在空间导航、临床分诊和财务分配任务中的风险态度。研究发现LLM表现出：任务内一致性、跨领域稳定性，以及风险态度分布收敛于较窄范围。这表明风险态度是LLM行为中一个稳定且此前未被表征的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险、开放性场景中部署AI系统时，感知风险如何转化为行动这一关键维度尚未得到测量。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究LLM是否在不确定性下表现出系统性和一致性的风险态度。

Method: 提出跨领域框架，将上下文风险信念（contextual risk belief）与类别决策（categorical decision）解耦，在空间导航、临床分诊和财务分配三个任务上，对六个代表性LLM和100名人类参与者进行测试。使用回归模型提取信念到决策的映射，量化风险敏感性和风险态度偏差。

Result: （i）LLM在固定任务领域内表现出稳健的任务内一致性；（ii）跨任务域保持风险态度的序数稳定性；（iii）相对于人类基线，LLM的风险态度分布收敛于更窄的范围。

Conclusion: 风险态度是LLM行为中一个稳定且此前未被充分表征的维度，这为评估和对齐AI系统在开放性决策中的行为奠定了基础，并激励进一步探究这些内在行为倾向的起源。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence systems are deployed in open-ended, high-stakes settings, a critical dimension remains unmeasured: how perceived risk is translated into action. We test whether large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic and consistent risk attitudes under uncertainty. We introduce a cross-domain framework that decouples contextual risk belief from categorical decision, and apply it to six representative LLMs and 100 human participants across spatial navigation, clinical triage, and financial allocation tasks. Using regression models, we extract each agents belief-to-decision mapping and quantify risk sensitivity and risk attitude bias. We find that most tested LLMs exhibit (i) robust intra-task consistency, indicating stable mappings from contextual belief to risk decision within a fixed task domain; (ii) cross-domain rank-order stability, preserving relative risk posture across tasks; and (iii) a convergence toward a restricted risk-attitude distribution relative to the broader human baseline. These results reveal risk attitude as a stable and previously uncharacterized dimension of LLM behavior, establishing a foundation for evaluating and aligning AI systems in open-ended decision-making and motivating further investigation into the origins of these intrinsic behavioral dispositions.

</details>


### [238] [A Survey on GNN-based Link Prediction: Techniques, Applications, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16198)
*Chengcheng Sun,Yajie Song,Cheng Zhai,Jiayun Tian,Jia Yang,Xiaobin Rui,Jian Zhang,Zhixiao Wang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从GNN架构和多样图结构的视角，系统综述了基于GNN的链接预测方法，提出了基于技术和应用的新分类法，涵盖GCN、GAE、GAT和GFormer等编码器架构，以及在知识图谱和推荐系统中的应用，并讨论了当前挑战和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述缺乏从GNN底层架构和多样图结构角度对链接预测的系统性探索，存在关键研究空白。

Method: 提出了一个创新的分类法，从技术角度（GCN-based、GAE-based、GAT-based、GFormer-based）和应用角度（知识图谱、推荐系统）对GNN链接预测方法进行系统分类与评述。

Result: 构建了一个涵盖技术和应用两个维度的GNN链接预测综述框架，分析了各类方法的优缺点及实际应用效果。

Conclusion: GNN-based链接预测在多个领域展现出重要价值，但仍面临挑战，未来有广阔的研究空间。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as the leading paradigm for link prediction, enabling the inference of missing connections and the anticipation of potential future links. However, existing reviews lack systematic exploration specifically targeting underlying GNN architectures and diverse graph structures. To address this critical gap, this paper provides a comprehensive review of GNN-based link prediction from a novel and dedicated GNN perspective. We propose an innovative taxonomy that categorizes recent advancements based on techniques and applications. From a technique perspective, we focus on key GNN encoder architectures, including GCN-based, GAE-based, GAT-based, and GFormer-based methods, discussing their strengths and limitations. From an application perspective, we highlight prominent use cases of link prediction in knowledge graphs and recommendation systems, demonstrating their real-world impact. In addition, we examine the current challenges and discuss promising future directions.

</details>


### [239] [PlanFlip: Attacking Multi-Agent LLM Systems via Planning-Phase Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16199)
*Yuhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanFlip框架针对多智能体LLM系统的规划阶段提出四种提示注入攻击（目标替换、优先级反转、上下文污染、角色混淆），发现更强模型（如GPT-5）反而更脆弱（ASR=0.68），同质化流水线存在关联智能体盲区，而推理增强模型（DeepSeek-R1）完全免疫，并提出异构模型多样性作为安全前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统的规划阶段是关键攻击面：单次注入到Planner的上下文即可引发级联放大，同时破坏所有下游子任务，而现有研究对此关注不足。

Method: 设计PlanFlip框架，包含四种规划阶段提示注入攻击（PF-1至PF-4），伪装成工具输出以绕过关键词过滤器。在9个前沿LLM上开展3,479轮实验评估。

Result: (1) 能力放大脆弱性：GPT-5攻击成功率最高(ASR=0.68)；(2) 同质化流水线存在关联智能体盲区：GPT-4o和Llama-3.3-70B的ASR近0但Stealth=1.00且StepShift>0，相同骨干网络的Critic报告对齐；(3) 推理增强模型抵抗注入：DeepSeek-R1在所有攻击下StepShift=0.00。提出D1和D2检测方法，检测率达1.00，在15/16个单元中优于基线。

Conclusion: 异构模型多样性是多智能体系统的安全前提，同质骨干网络中的冗余对规划阶段攻击不提供任何保护。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly rely on a Planner to decompose goals into sub-task sequences that downstream Executor and Critic agents execute and audit. We identify the planning phase as a critical attack surface: a single injection into the Planner's context achieves cascade amplification, corrupting all downstream sub-tasks simultaneously. We introduce PlanFlip, a framework comprising four planning-phase prompt injection attacks -- GoalSubstitution (PF-1), PriorityInversion (PF-2), ContextPollution (PF-3), and RoleConfusion (PF-4) -- each disguised as plausible tool outputs to evade keyword filters. Evaluating nine frontier LLMs across 3,479 episodes, we uncover three findings: (1) capability amplifies vulnerability -- GPT-5 achieves the highest attack success rate (ASR = 0.68), contradicting the assumption that stronger models are inherently more secure; (2) homogeneous pipelines exhibit a correlated-agent blind spot -- GPT-4o and Llama-3.3-70B show ASR near 0 yet Stealth = 1.00 and StepShift > 0, with attacks restructuring plans while the same-backbone Critic reports alignment (two independent judges confirm -0.20 to -0.32 semantic deviation, r = 0.943); (3) reasoning-augmented models resist injections -- DeepSeek-R1 achieves StepShift = 0.00 across all attacks. We propose GoalAnchorCheck (D1) and CrossAgentConsensus (D2), achieving detection rates up to 1.00 and outperforming same-backbone baselines in 15 of 16 cells. Our key insight: heterogeneous model diversity is a security prerequisite for multi-agent systems; redundancy within a homogeneous backbone provides no protection against planning-phase attacks.

</details>


### [240] [Deterministic Replay for AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16200)
*Rasheed Mudasiru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: agrepl是一个基于CLI的确定性重放框架，通过MITM代理拦截Agent系统的外部交互，实现严格隔离环境下的零网络执行重放，达到F=1.0的重放保真度，并将延迟降低98.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent系统因LLM采样方差、外部API状态变化、CDN基础设施头信息及执行环境噪声等因素而具有固有的非确定性，现有可观测性平台只能捕获执行日志，无法在隔离环境中复现某一执行过程。

Method: 在传输层通过MITM代理拦截所有外部交互，序列化为结构化执行轨迹；在严格隔离的零出站网络环境中重放；形式化定义了Agent执行模型、请求键匹配函数K(s)并证明了确定性不变量；提出噪声感知的差异比较算法，将HTTP头信息差异分为信号层和噪声层。

Result: 在五个工作负载上（n=250次重放实例）的实验评估显示，重放保真度F=1.0，每步延迟中位数降低98.3%。

Conclusion: agrepl以Go语言实现，以单一静态二进制文件分发，采用MIT许可证开源，为AI Agent系统的可复现调试提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: AI agent systems that couple large language models (LLMs) with external tools and APIs are inherently non-deterministic: LLM sampling variance, external API state, CDN infrastructure headers, and execution-environment noise collectively prevent any prior agent run from being faithfully re-executed. Existing observability platforms capture execution logs but cannot reproduce a run in isolation. We present agrepl, a developer-first CLI framework for deterministic replay of agent executions. agrepl intercepts all external interactions at the transport layer via a man-in-the-middle (MITM) proxy, serialises them as structured execution traces, and replays them in a strictly isolated environment with zero outbound network access. We formalise the agent execution model, define the request-key matching function K(s), and prove the determinism invariant. We introduce a noise-aware diff algorithm classifying HTTP header divergence into signal and noise tiers. Empirical evaluation across five workloads (n = 250 replay instances) demonstrates replay fidelity F = 1.0 and a median per-step latency reduction of 98.3%. agrepl is implemented in Go, ships as a single static binary, and is released under the MIT licence.
  Keywords: AI agents, deterministic replay, LLM debugging, reproducibility, MITM proxy, execution tracing, record/replay systems.

</details>


### [241] [Generative Ontology Induction: Domain-Agnostic Schema Discovery from Document Corpora Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16201)
*Sergei Sergienko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式本体归纳（GOI）框架，能从示例语料中自动诱导出包含实体、维度、属性、关系和约束的结构化本体，并导出为类型化图（6种节点、7种边）。通过四个不同领域本体验证，GOI覆盖率达95-100%，显著优于通用模板（最低仅52.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体工程仍是知识密集型AI系统的关键瓶颈，现有自动化方法依赖预定义模式、局限于窄领域或输出非结构化结果，无法适用于下游流水线。

Method: 提出GOI框架，是一种领域无关的归纳方法，从示例语料中生成包含实体、维度、属性、关系和约束的生成式蓝图，并导出为YAML/JSON格式的 typed graph（6种节点类型、7种边类型）。同时提出Node Coverage Score新评估指标，衡量生成输出中结构本体节点（类、属性、维度）的覆盖比例。

Result: 在四个对比本体（软件服务发票、自定义职位描述、疼痛管理临床记录、专业服务合同）上，GOI提示生成的结构骨干覆盖率达95-100%；而通用三字段模板在发票模式上达97.8%，但在职位描述本体降至52.2%，疼痛管理本体62.2%，专业服务合同本体78.3%。结构覆盖率与文档类型对模型的熟悉程度无关。

Conclusion: GOI框架在跨领域本体归纳中具有显著优势，能稳定保持高结构覆盖率，且不依赖模型对文档类型的先验熟悉度，为解决本体工程瓶颈提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Ontology engineering remains a critical bottleneck in knowledge-intensive AI systems. Existing automated approaches either depend on predefined schemas, operate within narrow domains, or produce unstructured outputs unsuitable for downstream pipelines.
  We introduce Generative Ontology Induction (GOI), a domain-agnostic framework that induces a generative blueprint - entities, dimensions, properties, relationships, and constraints - from a corpus of examples and exports it as a typed graph (six node types, seven edge types) in YAML/JSON. We introduce the Node Coverage Score, a novel evaluation metric that measures the fraction of structural ontology nodes (classes, properties, and dimensions) appearing in generated outputs.
  A controlled generative validation on four contrasting ontologies - a familiar Software Services Invoice schema, a custom Job Description Ontology, a confidential Pain-Management Clinical Visit Record Ontology, and a Professional Services Contract & Statement of Work Ontology - shows that GOI-prompted generation covers 95-100% of the structural backbone in every case; a generic three-field template holds at 97.8% on the invoice schema but drops to 52.2% on the Job Description Ontology, 62.2% on the Pain-Management ontology, and 78.3% on the Professional Services Contract ontology. The structural coverage holds regardless of how familiar the document type is to the model.

</details>


### [242] [Democratizing AI with Small Language Models: Structured Benchmarking and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Local Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16202)
*Daniel Cersosimo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对9个1.35亿至30亿参数的开源语言模型进行受控评估，证明在硬件和治理约束下，通过精调（4-bit NF4量化+DoRA/LoRA）可使小型模型在结构化本地部署任务中达到实用水平。Qwen Coder 3B在基线上达到75.67%准确率，精调后提升26.85个百分点，表明子30亿参数模型可作为结构化专业工作负载的本地专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI民主化不在于追求前沿规模的通用性，而在于普通机构能否在可承受的硬件和治理约束下选择、审计和专业化部署有能力的模型。

Method: 构建1,085例、16主题的多选题基准，采用单字母输出协议评估符号精度和约束格式化能力。使用4-bit NF4量化+DoRA/LoRA适配器在NVIDIA L4级预算下对模型进行参数高效微调。

Result: 基线评估中Qwen Coder 3B准确率75.67%领先；精调后Qwen Coder 3B提升+26.85%，SmolLM2 1.7B提升+25.92%，Qwen2.5 1.5B提升+19.44%。

Conclusion: 通过规范的基准构建、跨模型评估和低成本专业化，子30亿参数模型可作为结构化专业工作负载的可行本地专家。

Abstract: AI democratization is not primarily a question of matching frontier-scale generality; it is a question of whether capable models can be selected, audited, and specialized under hardware and governance constraints that ordinary institutions can actually satisfy. This paper studies that problem through a controlled evaluation of nine open-weight language models between 135M and 3B parameters on a 1,085-example, 16-topic multiple-choice benchmark designed for structured local deployment. The benchmark emphasizes symbolic precision, constrained formatting, extraction, and short-horizon semantic decision making under a strict one-letter output protocol. A shared parameter-efficient fine-tuning pipeline then adapts a subset of models using 4-bit NF4 quantization with DoRA/LoRA-style adapters on an NVIDIA L4-class budget. In base evaluation, Qwen Coder 3B leads at 75.67% strict accuracy, followed by Qwen2.5 1.5B at 67.10%, Qwen3.5 2B at 64.98%, and Granite 3.3 2B at 64.61%. On the shared 108-example held-out fine-tuning split, adaptation improves Qwen Coder 3B by +26.85 points, SmolLM2 1.7B by +25.92, Qwen2.5 1.5B by +19.44, SmolLM2 360M by +10.18, and SmolLM2 135M by +5.55. Across ranking, topic-level heterogeneity, difficulty strata, failure composition, efficiency frontiers, and topic-conditioned transfer, the same conclusion recurs: a disciplined workflow of benchmark construction, cross-model evaluation, and low-cost specialization already makes a subset of sub-3B models viable as local experts for structured niche workloads.

</details>


### [243] [Masked Diffusion Language Models are Strong and Steerable Text-Based World Models for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16204)
*Darshan Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的可控文本世界建模方法，替代自回归世界模型，在九种环境、十二个前沿模型家族上构建23.9万条状态-动作轨迹。MDLM通过双向锚点感知去噪，在连贯性、 groundedness 和 rollout多样性上超越4倍参数量的LLM，结合GRPO训练框架在三个OOD环境上实现高达47%的绝对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习需要多样化、专门化的训练环境，但手工环境的任务难度固定，随模型性能提升而失效；稀疏奖励在长时域下导致模式坍缩。自回归世界模型存在从左到右的偏见，无法对全局依赖的状态锚点（如工具模式、先前回合、预期结果）进行条件控制。

Method: (i) 将文本世界建模形式化为可操控的转移动力学问题，分解为初始状态、任务上下文、工具模式、领域规则和引导指令五个组件；(ii) 构建包含239,403条状态-动作轨迹的数据集；(iii) 对比AR LM与MDLM，MDLM通过双向锚点感知去噪实现更好的连贯性和多样性；(iv) 提出基于GRPO的即插即用训练框架，配合确定性状态检查；(v) 在三个OOD环境上进行零样本迁移消融实验。

Result: MDLM在连贯性、groundedness和rollout多样性上优于4倍参数量的LLM，推理延迟相当。在ScienceWorld、ALFWorld、AppWorld三个OOD环境上，基于LFM2.5、Qwen3、Mistral三种1.2B-7B骨干网络进行零样本迁移，相比基线实现了高达47%的绝对性能提升。

Conclusion: 掩码扩散语言模型作为一种可扩展、可操控的世界建模方法，能有效解决自回归模型在全局状态锚点条件控制上的局限，提供多样化的训练信号，且无需环境特定微调即可实现显著的零样本迁移性能。

Abstract: Recent growth in reinforcement learning (RL) has surfaced a need for diverse, specialized training environments. Hand-curated environments with fixed task and reward difficulties become ineffective signals as model performance improves, and sparse rewards over long horizons induce mode collapse on specific workflows or tool structures. World models that simulate environment states have matched pure rollout performance, making them promising for scaling diversity on-demand. However, autoregressive (AR) world models suffer from a left-to-right bias preventing conditioning on globally interdependent state anchors such as tool schemas, prior turns, and expected outcomes. We (i) formalize text-based world modeling as a steerable transition-dynamics problem decomposed into initial state, task context, tool schemas, domain rules, and steering directives, and (ii) curate 239,403 grounded state-action trajectories spanning nine open-source environments and twelve frontier model families. We compare AR LMs and masked diffusion language models (MDLMs), showing MDLMs, via bidirectional anchor-aware denoising, achieve better coherence, groundedness, and empirically validated rollout diversity than LLMs over 4x their parameter size, at comparable inference latency. We introduce a plug-and-play GRPO training framework with deterministic state checks, and perform zero-shot transfer ablations on three OOD environments (ScienceWorld, ALFWorld, AppWorld) across three 1.2B-7B agent backbones (LFM2.5, Qwen3, Mistral), achieving up to 47% absolute gains over baselines without environment-specific fine-tuning. We further conduct behavioral analysis of failure modes under adversarial scenarios and human evaluation on realism, outcome correctness, and training utility. We open-source our work to encourage research in this direction.

</details>


### [244] [It Takes 8 Tokens: Weak-to-Strong Off-Policy RL via Auxiliary Branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16205)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Liwei Qian,Xin Pei,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出W2SPO方法，通过弱到强的学习范式（利用弱辅助模型注入短片段）扩展强化学习中的探索支持，解决了LLM在推理任务中因样本语义冗余导致奖励对比不足的问题，在数学推理基准上取得更优性能并实现3.55倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法在提升LLM推理能力时存在关键瓶颈：在挑战性推理任务上，目标模型自生成样本呈现语义冗余，收敛到相同的错误"推理盆地"，导致策略更新时奖励对比不足，限制了学习效果。

Method: 提出W2SPO（Weak to Strong Policy Optimization），一种离策略RL方法。利用一个更弱但计算高效的辅助模型，在目标模型中间轨迹中注入短辅助片段（短至8个token），目标模型从这些转移状态继续完成推理路径。策略更新仅基于最终可验证奖励约束在这些短插入片段上。属于弱到强学习范式，通过弱模型引导扩展局部探索支持。

Result: 在4B规模模型上，W2SPO在数学推理基准上优于所有评估的post-trained基线。与相同采样预算的GRPO相比，Pass@1从62.3%提升至64.2%，同时实现3.55倍训练加速。

Conclusion: 弱辅助分支可以通过扩展局部探索支持来诱导更强的目标推理策略，有效克服了传统RL范式中的支持受限瓶颈。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has emerged as a standard approach for enhancing reasoning in large language models, which typically optimizes the policy by contrasting multiple self generated rollouts. However, we identify a critical support limited bottleneck in this paradigm: on challenging reasoning tasks, the target model's samples often exhibit semantic redundancy, converging into the same erroneous "reasoning basins" that offer negligible reward contrast for policy updates. In this paper, we propose to overcome this limitation through a weak to strong learning paradigm, where a policy's exploration is informed by a weaker but computationally efficient auxiliary model. We introduce W2SPO, an off policy RL method that injects short auxiliary segments often as brief as 8 tokens into intermediate target model trajectories and the target model then completes the reasoning path from these diverted states. Policy updates are restricted to these short inserted segments based on final verifiable rewards. Empirically, W2SPO achieves superior performance among evaluated 4B scale models on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, outperforming evaluated post trained baselines. Compared with vanilla GRPO under the same sampling budget, W2SPO improves Pass@1 from 62.3% to 64.2% while achieving a 3.55 times training speedup. These results suggest that weak auxiliary branches can induce stronger target reasoning policies by expanding local exploration support.

</details>


### [245] [PPO-HSC: An Exploratory Reinforcement Learning Framework Based on Wide-Area Policy Coverage Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16206)
*Yujie Shen,Haowen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PPO-HSC框架，通过高阶采样覆盖奖励机制解决LLM微调中的模式坍缩问题，在保持精度的同时显著提升推理路径多样性和状态空间覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR方法在LLM微调中会导致模型过度优化已知解，牺牲探索能力，陷入"无形枷锁"的模式坍缩问题，限制了模型发现更广泛解空间的能力。

Method: PPO-HSC在PPO基础上引入高阶采样覆盖奖励，通过维护动态轨迹库存储已验证的唯一解，提供可微分的语义新颖性奖励信号，同时通过合理性约束确保结构完整性。

Result: 在数学推理（GSM8K、SVAMP）和代码生成任务上，PPO-HSC显著提升了解决方案多样性和状态空间覆盖率，同时保持或超越了现有RL基线的准确率和语法完整性。

Conclusion: PPO-HSC有效平衡了探索与利用，通过奖励语义新颖性鼓励模型发现多样化有效推理模式，为解决LLM微调中的模式坍缩提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces PPO-HSC (Proximal Policy Optimization with High-order Sampling Coverage), an exploratory reinforcement learning framework designed to address the "Invisible Shackles" of mode collapse in Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuning. While standard Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) effectively reinforces high-reward trajectories, it often leads models to over-optimize known solutions, sacrificing curiosity and the ability to explore broader solution manifolds. To overcome this, PPO-HSC incorporates a High-order Sampling Coverage (HSC) reward that incentivizes the discovery of "low-similarity yet high-validity" reasoning patterns. By maintaining a dynamic trajectory library of verified unique solutions, the framework provides a differentiable signal that rewards semantic novelty while ensuring structural rationality through a plausibility constraint. Empirical evaluations on mathematical reasoning (GSM8K, SVAMP) and code generation tasks demonstrate that PPO-HSC significantly enhances solution diversity and state-space coverage while maintaining or surpassing the accuracy and syntax integrity of state-of-the-art RL baselines.

</details>


### [246] [JUMP: Single-Pass Membership Inference on Fine-Tuned Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16207)
*Yeachan Jun,Albert No*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JUMP是一种针对微调离散扩散语言模型（dLLM）的高效成员推断攻击方法，利用dLLM的任意顺序解码和并行解码特性，通过联合掩码探针选择低参考置信度位置并计算重建差分统计量，在LLaDA-8B-Base上相比SAMA将ROC-AUC从0.82提升至0.90。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对dLLM的成员推断攻击方法（如SAMA）虽然采用了损失模仿策略，但仅将任意顺序接口视为随机化手段，需要多次目标/参考查询，未能充分利用dLLM独特的并行解码和任意顺序解码能力。

Method: JUMP方法包含两个阶段：（1）选择阶段：利用任意顺序解码能力，通过单次前向传播识别参考模型低置信度位置；（2）评分阶段：利用并行解码能力，将选中的位置联合掩码，通过单次联合查询计算目标模型与参考模型之间的裁剪重建差分统计量。

Result: 在微调后的LLaDA-8B-Base模型上，跨六个MIMIR数据集，JUMP将平均ROC-AUC从SAMA的0.82提升至0.90，并在低假阳性率场景下显著提升检测性能。

Conclusion: JUMP通过充分挖掘dLLM的任意顺序和并行解码特性，实现了单次查询的高效成员推断攻击，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) test whether a candidate example appeared in a model's training data. We study MIAs for fine-tuned discrete diffusion language models (dLLMs), where membership means inclusion in the target model's fine-tuning set. Unlike autoregressive language models, dLLMs allow an attacker to choose arbitrary mask sets and obtain token distributions for all masked positions in parallel. The prior dLLM attack, SAMA, follows a natural loss-mimicking strategy by averaging reconstruction signals over many randomly sampled masks, but it uses the any-order interface only as randomization and requires many target/reference queries. We propose JUMP (Joint Uncertainty-Guided Mask Probing), a single-pass scoring attack that exploits both distinctive properties of dLLMs: any-order decodability is used to select low-reference-confidence positions, and parallel decodability is used to score all selected positions through one joint masked query per model. JUMP masks the selected positions jointly and computes a clipped target/reference reconstruction-gap statistic. On fine-tuned LLaDA-8B-Base across six MIMIR domains, JUMP improves mean ROC-AUC from 0.82 to 0.90 over SAMA and substantially improves low-FPR detection, while requiring only one selector pass and one scoring pass through each of the target and reference models.

</details>


### [247] [ColGraphRAG: Late-Interaction Evidence Retrieval for Multimodal GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16208)
*Seonok Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在基于图的多模态问答中，将视觉候选排序算子从单向量双编码器相似度替换为ColBERT/ColPali系列的后期交互多向量评分，可提升图链接图像候选的检索阶段点估计和下游QA性能，尤其在视觉证据主导的问题上效果更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图链接图像的端到端准确性依赖于多模态资产的排序质量，但单向量双编码器相似度会丢失细粒度对齐所需的patch级和token级结构信息，限制了图链接图像候选的检索精度。

Method: 在保持离线图构建、文本/表格侧检索、结构化抽取和下游推理不变的前提下，将图链接图像节点的视觉候选排序算子从单向量双编码器替换为MaxSim风格的后期交互多向量评分（源自ColBERT/ColPali系列）。

Result: 在MultimodalQA上，该替换提升了图链接图像候选的检索阶段点估计和下游QA准确率；视觉证据主导的问题提升更大，文本主导的问题趋势混杂。

Conclusion: 该模式为图链接视觉证据的纳入提供了机制层面的证据，但更广泛的验证和更细粒度的图级诊断仍是重要的未来工作。

Abstract: Graph-grounded multimodal question answering organizes text, tables, and images in a structured evidence graph, yet end-to-end accuracy depends on which multimodal assets are ranked highly enough to enter downstream reasoning; for graph-linked images, single-vector bi-encoder similarity can discard patch- and token-level structure needed for fine-grained alignment. We evaluate replacing the visual candidate-ranking operator over graph-linked image nodes with late-interaction MaxSim-style multi-vector scoring in the ColBERT/ColPali lineage, while keeping offline graph construction, text- and table-side retrieval, structured extraction, and downstream reasoning unchanged. On MultimodalQA, this change is associated with improved retrieval-stage point estimates for graph-linked image candidates and downstream QA gains, with larger movement where visual evidence matters most and mixed trends on text-dominant questions; we interpret the pattern as mechanism-level evidence for graph-linked visual evidence inclusion, while broader validation and finer graph-level diagnostics remain important future work.

</details>


### [248] [Shapley Context Pruning: A Cooperative Game Perspective for Context Reranking and Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16209)
*Yanqiao Chen,Dongsheng Hou,Yuhan Rui,Zhen Cao,Yepang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Shapley Context Pruning (SCP)框架，基于合作博弈论对RAG系统进行上下文重排序与剪枝，采用Deep Sets架构和蒙特卡洛采样实现高效推理，在多项长上下文任务中取得竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统的上下文重排序与剪枝缺乏可解释且统一的框架，现有方法多依赖启发式损失函数和经验归因，未能从理论角度提供重要性归因的合理解释。

Method: 将上下文建模为合作博弈，采用Shapley值进行重要性归因；使用Deep Sets架构近似排列不变的值函数（句子级别），结合预训练语言模型作为句子编码器；通过成对边距排序损失优化，并利用蒙特卡洛采样保证训练和推理的可扩展性，同时提供理论误差界和样本复杂度保证。

Result: 在支持句召回、Needle-in-the-Haystack、长上下文QA和多跳推理等多项任务上取得竞争性下游QA性能，并通过消融实验验证了嵌入质量和归因策略的有效性。

Conclusion: SCP为RAG上下文重排序提供了一个统一且理论可解释的博弈论框架，在保持数学严谨性的同时兼顾了实际可扩展性，为模型无关的上下文剪枝提供了新范式。

Abstract: Context reranking and pruning have become essential for improving the efficiency of modern Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems, yet an interpretable and unified framework remains underexplored. Previous work has primarily emphasized lexical retrieval, cross-encoder architectures, model distillation, and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), mostly relying on heuristic loss functions and empirical attribution. This paper presents Shapley Context Pruning (SCP), a novel framework for context reranking that establishes a cooperative-game-theory perspective for importance attribution by modeling the context as a cooperative game. Balancing the trade-off between fine-grained and coarse-grained representations, we employ a Deep Sets architecture to approximate a permutation-invariant value function at the sentence level, utilizing pre-trained language models as sentence embedders and optimizing via a pairwise margin ranking loss. To ensure practical scalability without sacrificing mathematical rigor, we leverage Monte-Carlo sampling for efficient training and inference, providing formal theoretical error bounds and sample complexity guarantees for preserving Top-K subset rankings. Furthermore, we conduct comprehensive experiments-spanning supporting-sentence recall, Needle-in-the-Haystack (NIAH) evaluations, long-context QA, and multi-hop reasoning-alongside rigorous ablation studies on embedding quality and attribution strategies. The model achieves competitive downstream QA performance against robust baselines.

</details>


### [249] [A Survey on the Verification of Reinforcement Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16210)
*Luca Marzari,Ezio Bartocci,Enrico Marchesini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了强化学习策略验证方法，提出了一个三维度分类体系（验证范式、时间范围、保证强度），统一了理论基础，明确了隐式假设和局限性，并指出了新兴研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在安全关键领域的部署面临缺乏严格行为保证的障碍，而现有关于RL策略验证的研究虽快速增长但概念碎片化，需要系统性梳理和统一视角。

Method: 通过提出三维分类体系（验证范式：形式化vs概率性；时间范围：单步vs多步；保证强度）来组织现有方法，并统一理论基础，明确隐式假设和局限性。

Result: 建立了一个统一的RL验证方法分类框架，明晰了不同方法之间的关系，揭示了现有方法的假设和局限性。

Conclusion: 该综述为RL策略验证领域提供了系统性视角，有助于推动该领域的理论统一和未来发展。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly applied in complex, safety-critical domains, yet the lack of rigorous behavioral guarantees for neural network-based policies remains a major barrier to deployment. Recent advances in policy expressiveness and scale have intensified this challenge, leading to a rapidly growing but conceptually fragmented body of work on RL policy verification. This survey provides a unifying perspective on RL verification methods. We introduce a taxonomy that clarifies relationships among existing approaches along three axes: verification paradigm (formal versus probabilistic), temporal scope (step-wise versus multi-step), and guarantees strength. Beyond taxonomy, we unify underlying theoretical foundations, make implicit assumptions and limitations explicit, and identify emerging directions.

</details>


### [250] [Accurate and Efficient Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16211)
*Zicheng Zhao,Xinyang Guo,Luyao Lv,Menghan Wang,Ming Li,Shuaicheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一个结构化、冲突感知的长时记忆框架，通过实体类型图存储、哈希加速双路径检索和主动冲突检测机制，显著提升了LLM智能体的记忆准确性和效率，在多个基准测试中达到最优性能，并将冲突检测率提升4.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的持久记忆系统存在两方面的局限性：一是平面化、非结构化的存储方式丢失了关系上下文，无法支持多跳推理和时间推理；二是依赖昂贵的LLM分类器，不适合延迟敏感的场景。同时，缺乏对新信息与存储知识进行验证的机制，导致系统不断累积矛盾。

Method: MOSAIC提出三个核心机制：(1)实体类型图存储与语义分类，保留事件、人物、关系等关系结构，支持多跳和时间推理；(2)哈希加速双路径检索，用局部敏感哈希替代LLM分类，实现近乎即时的查找且精度损失极小；(3)保存时的主动冲突检测，交叉验证新信息与现有图邻居，触发矛盾条目的更新或删除。

Result: 在LoCoMo上达到89.35%准确率（比最佳基线高27.21个百分点）；HaluMem-Medium和Long的提取F1分别为86.77%和85.84%，QA正确率分别为73.10%和70.75%；检测出66%的注入事实冲突，是基线（14%）的4.7倍；哈希加速检索保持平均0.58秒/问题的搜索延迟。

Conclusion: MOSAIC通过结构化图存储、哈希加速检索和主动冲突检测，在准确性和效率上显著超越现有长时记忆系统，为LLM智能体的持久记忆提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM agents augmented with persistent memory can recall past interactions, but existing systems suffer from two limitations: flat, unstructured storage loses relational context needed for multi-hop and temporal reasoning, and reliance on expensive LLM-based classification makes them impractical for latency-sensitive deployment. Without mechanisms to validate new information against stored knowledge, these systems silently accumulate contradictions. We present MOSAIC (Memory-Organized Structured Agent for Information Collection), a structured, conflict-aware long-term memory framework for LLM agents that is substantially more accurate and efficient. MOSAIC introduces three key capabilities: (1) entity-typed graph storage with semantic classification preserving relational structure across events, personas, and relationships, enabling multi-hop and temporal reasoning over conversation history; (2) hash-accelerated dual-path retrieval replacing LLM-based classification with locality-sensitive hashing, achieving near-instantaneous lookup with negligible accuracy loss; and (3) active conflict detection at save time that cross-references new information against existing graph neighbors, triggering updates or deletions for contradictory entries. Evaluated on LoCoMo (long-conversation QA), HaluMem, and a novel clinical-guideline error compounding test, MOSAIC achieves 89.35% accuracy on LoCoMo (+27.21 pp over the best baseline), best HaluMem-Medium extraction F1(86.77%) and HaluMem-Long extraction F1 (85.84%), best QA correctness on both Medium and Long (73.10%, 70.75%), and detects 66% of injected factual conflicts-4.7 times higher than the best baseline (14%)-while hash-accelerated retrieval keeps average search latency at 0.58 s per question.

</details>


### [251] [Symbolic Augmentation Closes a Canonical-Equivalence Blind Spot in Neural Fact-Checkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16212)
*Genpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出**符号增强**（Symbolic Augmentation）训练框架，通过逆向运行符号验证器模块生成标签保持的增强数据，解决LLM在科学文本中数值-单位幻觉问题（如95°C与368.15K的等价性盲区）。微调ModernBERT在五类数量错误分类基准上达到macro-F1=0.899，对规范等价改写准确率从36.5%提升至98.2%，且零推理开销下匹配封闭前沿LLM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在总结科学文本时频繁出现数值与单位的幻觉，这种错误会无声地反转科学结论，现有神经事实核查器无法检测这类错误，特别是无法识别物理等价但表面形式不同的数量（如摄氏与开尔文温度）。

Method: 1) 提出五类数量错误分类体系（typed-quantity error taxonomy）；2) 构建1500条基准数据集（PMC/arXiv来源，双LLM标注+仲裁，Krippendorff's α=0.882）；3) 微调ModernBERT；4) 提出**符号增强**框架：逆向运行符号验证器模块，生成规范等价改写（如温度、单位转换）作为数据增强；5) 对比实验：符号特征作为辅助输入、符号弱标签教师噪声等负结果分析。

Result: 1) ModernBERT微调达macro-F1=0.899，远超现有神经事实核查器；2) 规范等价改写准确率仅36.5%（盲区）；3) 符号增强后规范等价鲁棒性升至98.2%，ID准确率微升至0.902；4) 增强模型零推理开销匹配闭源前沿LLM；5) 外部基准SciFact-Open迁移macro-F1从0.791升至0.828；6) 符号特征作为辅助输入无效，符号弱标签在教师噪声下负向缩放。

Conclusion: 训练时数据增强是符号组件与学习组件之间正确的整合点；单纯注入符号特征或使用符号弱标签均非有效方案。符号增强框架有效解决了科学文本中数值-单位的规范等价盲区问题，且具有良好的迁移性。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate numbers and units when summarizing scientific text, a failure mode that can silently invert a scientific claim. We recast the detection of such errors as typed verification: we introduce a five-class typed-quantity error taxonomy and a 1500-item benchmark, rewritten from PMC and arXiv sources and labeled by two independent LLM annotators with adjudication (Krippendorff's alpha = 0.882). A ModernBERT encoder fine-tuned on this benchmark reaches macro-F1 = 0.899, far above any off-the-shelf neural fact-checker, yet four probes expose a sharp structural blind spot: on canonical-equivalent rewrites of physically equivalent quantities (e.g., 95°C and 368.15 K) its accuracy collapses to 36.5%. We propose Symbolic Augmentation, a training-time framework that runs the modules of a symbolic verifier in reverse to generate label-preserving augmented training data. The augmentation lifts canonical-equivalence robustness to 98.2% while slightly improving in-distribution accuracy (macro-F1: 0.899 to 0.902); the augmented encoder matches a closed-frontier LLM at no inference cost and transfers to an external benchmark (SciFact-Open binary macro-F1: 0.791 to 0.828). Two negative results sharpen the claim: symbolic features as auxiliary encoder inputs add nothing, and symbolic silver labels scale negatively under teacher noise. Together these results identify training-time augmentation as the right integration point between symbolic and learned components.

</details>


### [252] [SelKV: Selective KV Cache Merging with Per-Token Merge-or-Drop and Attention Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16213)
*Soumia Bouyahiaoui,Manel Kara laouar,Aicha Boutorh,Mohamed Hadj Ameur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的KV缓存压缩框架，通过软余弦门控和注意力比补偿机制，在仅保留25%缓存时仍能保持高质量生成，并在100k token下实现3.3倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自回归生成中KV缓存内存随上下文长度线性增长，现有token合并方法存在 indiscriminate aggregation 导致表示退化以及 attention sag（合并token与单token获得相同softmax质量的不匹配问题）。
